　　S1 (the lower bound of the Structural Positions is nonnegative, i.e. the synapses in question are excitatory), S2 (reset to 0 after firing, positive threshold, leak coefficient λ ≤ 1): determined by the synapse and cell types chosen; must be measured.  
　　S3 (ρ = 0: on the time scale in question, retained potentiation shows no spontaneous return toward baseline): must be measured; if there is a return, the case is outside the prediction.  
　　S4 (the presynaptic inputs of group A are always stimulated together, and the stimulus alphabet contains a probe stimulus that is exactly group A): must be controlled by the stimulation protocol.  
　　P0 (plasticity and backpropagation switched on throughout, with no blockade applied): must be controlled.  
　　K1 (single potentiation amplitude greater than the reading resolution), K2′ (potentiation under presynaptic activation alone no smaller than the depression weighted by the postsynaptic firing trace), K3(m) (after m rest beats the compound baseline EPSP of group A is subthreshold): must be measured.  
　　I0 (no residual potentiation and no residual traces before formation), F1 (the formation period contains only pairing and rest, with pairing spacing ≥ m\_F), the formation-amount condition (Φ form: the measured increment sequence of the pairing scheme makes the summed efficacy of group A cross the threshold, and each position's increment exceeds the resolution): the protocol must be controlled and the quantities measured.  
　　R1 and the subsequent regime (subsequent stimuli only from the declared alphabet, nonnegative injection), R2⁺ (the probe is preceded by m rest beats, or satisfies the certificate condition of 5.3): must be controlled by the protocol.  
　　F3ᵤ serves only to certify the source r and is not a condition when only self-sustainment and the pivot are predicted; to go further and predict qualification Fld, F3ᵤ, the read-back antecedents of 7.2 of Chapter 11 and E11 where NS\* fails must be carried as well, and what is predicted is only qualification under the model reading.  
　Conditional prediction (breachable form): when all the conditions listed hold, (i) after formation, the retained potentiation of group A does not decrease under any input conforming to the subsequent regime; (ii) the probe (stimulating group A alone after m rest beats) evokes firing and triggers an effective potentiation component PLUS (component ≥ A₊ \> ε); the weight is nondecreasing, and a net increment of 0 is allowed under saturation or cancelling components; (iii) when the retained potentiation of group A is returned to baseline by the declared same-beat setting (pharmacologically or optogenetically, for instance, and meeting the certificate condition of 5.3 and the same-beat conditions used in Lemma 14.5: the probe letter is exactly A and I\_j = 0), the same probe no longer evokes firing, and the target PLUS component does not occur. Three quantities must be kept apart: the occurrence and effectiveness of the PLUS component (process level, to be tested by process readings such as firing and plasticity-trigger signals), the net weight change (final-state reading), and actual qualification (the duty adjudication under the model reading, which further requires F3ᵤ, the read-back antecedents and the E11 clause). The theorem guarantees only the first and the nondecrease of the weight; at saturation PLUS cancels against clipping and the net increment can be 0. Example: the frozen member with hi = 7/10, n = 12, m\_F = m = 2; beat 36 is a certificate probe, and the condition group and regime all hold; before and after the probe beat w00 = w10 = 7/10, net increment 0, while the gate is open, PLUS00 = PLUS10 ≈ 0.142857 \> ε, and after T is set to baseline in the same beat the gate closes, R witness 1; with hi changed to 2 (unsaturated), the same history gives net increment ≈ 0.071429 \> 0 (Appendix B.10 G5). Additional scope (strict net potentiation, a proved sufficient condition): if d\_y,j \< 1, δ := A₊ − A₋/(1 − d\_y,j) \> 0, and hi\_ij − w\_ij \> 0 at that position before the probe beat, then the net increment at that position is ≥ min(δ, hi\_ij − w\_ij) \> 0; to require a net increment exceeding the resolution, min(δ, hi\_ij − w\_ij) \> ε is needed. Proof: at the probe beat the gate is open (Lemma 14.5), PLUS\_ij = A₊x′\_i ≥ A₊, \|MINUS\_ij\| = A₋y′\_j ≤ A₋/(1 − d\_y,j), ρ = 0, so the pre-clip increment is ≥ δ \> 0, there is no lower clipping, and the post-clip increment = min(pre-clip increment, hi\_ij − w\_ij) ≥ min(δ, hi\_ij − w\_ij). ∎ (Appendix B.10 G5: with A₋ = 1/32 and hi = 2, δ = 1/16, lower bound 1/16 \> ε, measured net increment ≈ 0.107143; for the frozen member δ = 0, and this additional scope does not apply.) This additional scope is not a guarantee of Theorem 14-1⁺; it predicts net potentiation only under its listed antecedents. Observable proxy directions: whether the compound EPSP of group A stimulated alone after rest is subthreshold; whether potentiation returns during rest; whether the probe stimulus evokes firing and triggers plasticity (process readings), and whether the net change in efficacy is nonnegative (final-state reading; it may be 0 near the upper limit); whether, after return to baseline, the probe stimulus no longer evokes firing and plasticity triggering.  
　Breach protocol: only when all the conditions actually used by this prediction (the items listed above) have been measured or controlled and hold, and one of (i)–(iii) fails, is there a breach; failure of (ii) means that the probe does not fire, that the PLUS component does not occur or is ineffective, or that the weight decreases, and does not include a net increment of 0—seeing only a net change of 0 gives no ground to deny the PLUS component or the historical dynamics, nor to judge qualification 0; strict net potentiation in the additional scope can be breached only when all its antecedents hold. When only K2′, K3 and threshold crossing at formation have been checked and S3, S4 and the rest have not, a failure is not a breach: for example, changing only A₊ = 1/4 and ρ = 1/10 on the frozen member, K2′ and K3(2) hold and Σ\_A w ≈ 1.577920 \> θ at the end of the formation period, yet after 100 rest beats the probe no longer fires; the cause of failure is that S3 fails, a case excluded by the condition table; with ρ = 0 restored all conditions hold, the probe fires, and the R witness is 1 (Appendix B.10 G3). Likewise, failures under decomposable stimulation protocols or nonzero return are not counterexamples to Theorem 14-1⁺.  
　Column C: the two evidence cards are V1 (qualified) and V0 respectively; they are not used to give 1. The listed conditions have no corresponding experimental measurements yet; they are registered as to be verified and are not judged 0.  
9.2  *Drosophila* mushroom body KC→MBON (evidence cards: Hige et al. 2015, Huang et al. 2024 and a summary card; the facts on the cards have not been independently verified)  
　Column A has at present Theorem 14-9 (FD level), which does not apply when depression dominates (6.6). Column B: on models with same-beat or delayed MBON→DAN return (Φ\_fly ∈ 𝒦\_PA^δ of 8.1 of Chapter 13), the conditions for a protection mechanism of depression-type Trace-Responses have not been given (U14-16), and no prediction is issued. Column C: no verdict.  
9.3  Slime mould, experimental deltas: the linear reading has only Lemma 14-L; whether the threshold-gated reading Family 1ᵍ corresponds to critical-shear transport in real river channels has not been checked (constitutive relations and budget in 6.7).  
9.4  *E. coli* chemotaxis: a continuous class, outside the host class of this paper; registered only as an object of conditional prediction.  
Three columns kept apart: model verdicts of 1 in Column A and FD-level conclusions are not written as certification in Columns B or C; no evidence card is judged 0, and experimental gaps are registered as to be verified.

10\. Summary Formulas  
　C\_LS⁺ := □C\_hold (P0, S1–S4, K1, K2′, K3(m), R1) ∧ C\_init (I0, F1, formation-amount condition \[F0 ∧ F2, or F0 ∧ F2′, or Φ(g) ≥ θ ∧ min(w − b) \> ε\], F3ᵤ) ∧ R2⁺.  
　Theorem 14-1⁺: C\_LS⁺ ⇒ ∀h ∀W: TraceSet ∧ Part ∧ HDyn (T), and hence Fld (algorithmic level, model mode; carrying the read-back antecedents of 7.2 of Chapter 11; carrying E11 where NS\* fails).  
　Theorem 14-7: under the background antecedents (S1–S4, P0, K1, F3ᵤ, I0, fixed g with min(w − b) \> ε, d\_x \< 1, ε \> 0; for the K3 direction additionally the no-clipping antecedent and B\_FD), \[∀ legitimate continuation ∀ window containing an m-clean probe: R(T)\] ⇔ K2′ ∧ K3(m) ∧ Φ(g) ≥ θ.  
　Proposition 14-8: under the i.i.d. reading, P(R(T) holds on a window of length L) ≥ 1 − (1 − p\_q^m p\_p)^⌊L/(m+1)⌋.  
　Theorem 14-9: C\_PA ⇒ R\_FD (q ∧ Act ∧ FD ∧ Path ∧ RemChange); rising to R requires B\_η^PA and B\_r^PA.  
　Master formula: in the event-gating class, “effective potentiation, self-sustainment, a subthreshold baseline after rest, sufficient formation and removability at the first pairing” make the retained Trace-Response self-sustaining against subsequent input and a pivot for the gate, so that qualification cannot fail to hold (carrying the read-back antecedents of 7.2 of Chapter 11, and E11 where NS\* fails); each coefficient threshold is exact, item by item, under the declared readings and background antecedents. The non-triviality of the theorems of this paper comes from threshold gating, which is the one route this paper takes, not the only route; other routes to non-triviality on the non-neural side (such as bilinear-partner support) are open (U14-5, U14-15).

11\. Declaration of Strength  
　Proved (with proofs): Lemmas 14.1–14.7, 14.3′, 14.7′; Theorem 14-1⁺ and Corollaries 14-1′, 14-1″; Propositions 14-2 (including λ = 1), 14-3 (d\_x \< 1), 14-4 (two sufficient ranges), 14-5(1) and (2) (including the antecedent S4), 14-6 (given g); Theorem 14-7 (under the listed background antecedents and readings); Proposition 14-8 (i.i.d. reading); Theorem 14-9 (FD level); Lemma 14-L (lemma level, a direct generalization; L5 given by the closed form L5′, proved equivalent to component-wise compatibility); the structural observation on the linear own-position family (only for that family).  
　Conditional conclusions: Theorem 14-1⁺ carries the full table C\_LS⁺, the read-back antecedents of Chapter 11 and H\_IND (where NS\* fails); Theorem 14-7 carries the background antecedents, its K3 direction carries B\_FD and the no-clipping antecedent, and K1 is a background antecedent and not within the equivalence characterization; Theorem 14-9 reaches only the FD level.  
　Finite verification by program: the readings listed in Appendix B; they cover their generating domains, and universal conclusions rest on the proofs of Sections 5 and 6.  
　Not issued:  
　　that any real host (biological neural tissue, neuromorphic hardware, material hosts) satisfies C\_LS⁺ or C\_PA, or has qualification;  
　　object-level HistCond and r on the gated extension class (B\_η^PA and B\_r^PA unproved);  
　　a complete characterization of when E11 is needed (intermediate range U14-14);  
　　the necessity of K1; a closed form for inf\_g Φ; the exact condition for self-sustainment when d\_x = 1;  
　　conclusions for windows with A but no certificate probe, for ρ \> 0, and for A-decomposable alphabets;  
　　exclusive conclusions of the kind “non-trivial results on the non-neural side can come only from the gating class”; a physical construction certificate on the non-neural side;  
　　negative verdicts (gaps in evidence cards are not judged 0; ⊥ is not read as 0);  
　　the open items listed in Section 12.  
　Evidence levels: mathematical proof and finite verification are listed separately; Columns A, B and C are kept apart.

12\. Open Items  
　U14-1  Deciding the uniform formation threshold inf\_g Φ(g) ≥ θ over the whole F1 class (arbitrary spacings ≥ m\_F) (the exact threshold for a given g is supplied by Proposition 14-6).  
　U14-2  Regime extension: probes with entries outside A, ρ \> 0, A-decomposable alphabets.  
　U14-3  The same-family necessity direction and equivalence characterization without background antecedents (Theorem 14-7 holds only under its background antecedents).  
　U14-4  Quantifiers under random input readings other than i.i.d. (for example, state-dependent input processes or input processes with memory).  
　U14-5  A physically non-trivial witness on the non-neural side (constitutive relations grounded, budget closable).  
　U14-6  Object-level read-back for gated extension classes of the fly type (depending on U13-16 of Chapter 13 and U8 of Chapter 11); the Necessity Form for continuous classes (of the *E. coli* type).  
　U14-7  While the individuation of clipping (U6 of Chapter 11) is open, windows that are saturated with NS\* failing can only carry E11.  
　U14-8′  Sources outside F3ᵤ with no blocking unit; a blocking criterion for A-decomposable alphabets (which must exhaust removals that change only part of the A writes); routes to r in the blocking case through removals at later pairing beats, cross-beat removal M1\_aux (carrying EF1′) or M2 (carrying B\_OD).  
　U14-9  The exact condition for self-sustainment when d\_x = 1.  
　U14-10  A closed form for inf\_g Φ(g).  
　U14-11  The exact role of K1: cases where K1 fails and MINUS bears HDyn.  
　U14-12  Windows with A but no certificate probe (introducing bounds on the actual w or multi-beat functional-dependence witnesses).  
　U14-13  The probability that NS\* holds; stochastic plasticity.  
　U14-14  The intermediate range for whether E11 is needed: windows outside the two sufficient ranges of Proposition 14-4.  
　U14-15  Host constructions for other routes to non-triviality on the non-neural side (bilinear-partner support c = k·x·y, k ≠ 0, and others).  
　U14-16  Conditions for a protection mechanism of depression-type Trace-Responses (with hi − w as depth), such as MBON→DAN return.

13\. Interfaces  
　*The Adjudication Form of Field Concretization for Candidate Hosts*: the read-back of this paper uses its Lemmas 3.1 and 3.2, Theorems COR, D2 and R7 and the condition ledger of 7.2 word for word; Theorem 14-1⁺ generalizes that paper's instance-by-instance verdicts to universal conclusions over parameter families × all subsequent inputs; whether E11 stays or goes belongs to its U6; read-back on gated extension classes belongs to its U8.  
　*The Adjudication Form of Material Field Concretization*: Theorem 14-9 uses its class definitions of 3.6 and PA-2; only when B\_η^PA (the FD → η bridge of its U13-16, depending on U8 of Chapter 11) and B\_r^PA (the counterpart of criterion PS and COR on the extension class) are both proved can Theorem 14-9 rise to C\_PA ⇒ R under its antecedents; until then it reaches only the FD level.  
　*The Construction Form of Field Concretization*: Lemma 14-L is the beat-by-beat generalization of its Propositions 1 and 4; the structural observation on the linear own-position family concerns only its encoding M(a, b, k, γ, ε).  
　Chapter 19 of the Field Concretization Group Form, *The Discrimination Form of Trace-Response Transformation in Compartmentalized Third-Factor Circuits*: Theorem 14-9, the obstacle of 6.6 and the conditional prediction of 9.1 serve as preliminaries for the fly and human-brain candidates.  
　Chapter 20 of the Field Concretization Group Form, *The Discrimination Form of Trace-Response Transformation in Artificial Neural Networks*: Theorems 14-1⁺ and 14-7 give conditions of holding on SNN parameter domains and input regimes, and can be cited conditionally (carrying the full table C\_LS⁺, the E11 clause and the read-back antecedents).  
　Chapter 21 of the Field Concretization Group Form, *The Mathematical Form of Field-Law Generation in Geomorphic Concretized Fields*: the reading and budget obstruction of Family 1ᵍ, and the bilinear route (U14-5, U14-15).

## Appendix A  Program Source

The following 15 programs run in the same directory; they use only the Python standard library, with no third-party dependencies, and all numerical values are computed exactly with fractions.Fraction. A.1–A.9 are only imported: A.3–A.8 are, in order, byte-for-byte identical to Appendix A.1–A.6 of *The Adjudication Form of Field Concretization for Candidate Hosts* v1.0, and A.9 is byte-for-byte identical to Appendix A.4 of *The Adjudication Form of Material Field Concretization* v1.0; each run entry checks their SHA-256 against the respective Appendix B.1 before importing, and aborts on a mismatch; A.10–A.15 are the run entries. The programs read no files outside the appendix. The programs give finite verification, exact witnesses and counterexamples; random generation covers only its generating domain, and universal conclusions rest on the proofs in the main text.  
A.1  t14\_core.py  Core library: an independent implementation Mem of 𝒦\_g members, the frozen member, the condition-group checks check\_C, check\_regime and f3\_mode (which do not run the dynamics), witness computation verify, and the linear family Lin (4.3, Section 5, 6.1, 6.7)

~~~python
"""Appendix program of The Necessity Form of Field-Concretization Qualification: core library (Python standard library only; all numerics computed exactly with fractions.Fraction).
Contents
  1. Family 3: Mem, an independent implementation of the event-gating class 𝒦_g (Chapter 11 v1.0, Definitions 1–3 and Definition 5);
      check_C, the check of the condition group C of the subclass 𝒦_LS (arithmetic only; the dynamics is not run);
      check_regime, the check of the trajectory regime (inspects only the letter sequence; the dynamics is not run);
      verify, the computation of dynamical facts and duty witnesses (runs the dynamics; the witnesses are evaluated under the atomic criteria of Chapter 11, 4.2–4.4).
  2. Families 1 and 2: F-layer evaluation and mechanism-layer validity for the linear F family M(a, b, k, γ, ε) (Chapter 6 v1.0, Proposition 5(ii)) (Family A: z ≤ z_max; Family E: receptor pool nonnegative, firing-rate upper limit).
Notation (as in Section 4 of the main text)
  Target gating unit j, probe entry set A, T = {(i, j) : i ∈ A}.
  Letter σ = (entry name, I), where I is the out-of-boundary injection (teaching current) into each gating unit and must be nonnegative.
  Rest beat (for j): the entry pattern is all 0 and I_j = 0.
  Pairing beat: the entry pattern contains all of A (under an A-indecomposable alphabet, simply “contains A”) and I_j ≥ θ_j.
  Probe beat: the entry pattern is exactly the indicator vector of A and I_j = 0; clean probe beat: a probe beat whose m immediately preceding beats are all rest beats.
"""
from fractions import Fraction as F
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- General
def fr(q):
    q = F(q)
    return f'{q}' if q.denominator < 10 ** 6 else f'{float(q):.6f}'

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 1. Family 3: members of 𝒦_g
class Mem:
    """A member of 𝒦_g (Chapter 11, Definition 1). State o = (w, v, x, y). This implementation is independent of Appendix A.2 of Chapter 11 and uses it and the frozen snn_core only for comparison (run_main S0)."""
    def __init__(self, n_in, n_out, pat, lam, theta, vreset, dx, dy, Ap, Am, rho, b, lo, hi, eps=F(1, 100)):
        self.n_in, self.n_out, self.pat = n_in, n_out, dict(pat)
        self.lam, self.theta, self.vreset = [F(q) for q in lam], [F(q) for q in theta], [F(q) for q in vreset]
        self.dx, self.dy = [F(q) for q in dx], [F(q) for q in dy]
        self.Ap, self.Am, self.rho = F(Ap), F(Am), F(rho)
        self.b = [[F(q) for q in r] for r in b]
        self.lo = [[F(q) for q in r] for r in lo]
        self.hi = [[F(q) for q in r] for r in hi]
        self.eps = F(eps)
        # parameter domain of Definition 1
        assert 'none' in self.pat and all(c == 0 for c in self.pat['none'])
        assert all(len(p) == n_in and all(c in (0, 1) for c in p) for p in self.pat.values())
        assert all(0 <= q <= 1 for q in self.lam + self.dx + self.dy) and 0 <= self.rho <= 1
        assert self.Ap >= 0 and self.Am >= 0
        assert all(self.lo[i][j] <= self.b[i][j] <= self.hi[i][j] for i in range(n_in) for j in range(n_out))
    def init(self):
        """Declared resting initial state: w = b, v = x = y = 0."""
        return ([[self.b[i][j] for j in range(self.n_out)] for i in range(self.n_in)],
                [F(0)] * self.n_out, [F(0)] * self.n_in, [F(0)] * self.n_out)
    def step(self, o, sig, plastic=True, bap=True):
        """The eight stages of Definition 2; returns (o′, rec). rec['ev'] holds the events of Definition 5 that act on v and w: key (kind, i, j), value the component c."""
        w, v, x, y = o
        name, I = sig
        s = self.pat[name]
        I = [F(q) for q in I]
        vt = [self.lam[j] * v[j] + sum(w[i][j] * s[i] for i in range(self.n_in)) + I[j] for j in range(self.n_out)]
        g = [1 if vt[j] >= self.theta[j] else 0 for j in range(self.n_out)]
        v2 = [self.vreset[j] if g[j] else vt[j] for j in range(self.n_out)]
        x2 = [self.dx[i] * x[i] + s[i] for i in range(self.n_in)]
        y2 = [self.dy[j] * y[j] + g[j] for j in range(self.n_out)]
        ev, S_pre = {}, {}
        w2 = [[None] * self.n_out for _ in range(self.n_in)]
        for j in range(self.n_out):
            for i in range(self.n_in):
                if s[i]:
                    ev[('SYN', i, j)] = w[i][j]
        for i in range(self.n_in):
            for j in range(self.n_out):
                S = w[i][j]
                if plastic and bap and g[j]:
                    c = self.Ap * x2[i]; ev[('PLUS', i, j)] = c; S += c
                if plastic and bap and s[i]:
                    c = -self.Am * y2[j]; ev[('MINUS', i, j)] = c; S += c
                if plastic and self.rho != 0:
                    c = -self.rho * (w[i][j] - self.b[i][j]); ev[('RELAX', i, j)] = c; S += c
                nw = min(self.hi[i][j], max(self.lo[i][j], S))
                if nw != S:
                    ev[('CLIP', i, j)] = nw - S
                S_pre[(i, j)] = S
                w2[i][j] = nw
        return (w2, v2, x2, y2), dict(s=s, I=I, vt=vt, g=g, ev=ev, S=S_pre)
    def run(self, hist, o0=None, **kw):
        o = o0 if o0 is not None else self.init()
        pre, recs = [o], []
        for sig in hist:
            o, r = self.step(o, sig, **kw)
            pre.append(o); recs.append(r)
        return pre, recs

def frozen_snn_member(W0=F(1, 4), Ap=F(1, 8), Am=F(1, 16), lam=F(3, 4), theta=F(1), dec=F(1, 2), wmax=F(2), rho=F(0)):
    """The frozen SNN (the constants in lines 15–20 of snn_core.py of the frozen SNN instance s1) as a member of 𝒦_g (Chapter 11, Proposition 1). Single parameters can be changed for comparison."""
    pat = {'A': (1, 1, 0, 0), 'B': (0, 0, 1, 1), 'AB': (1, 1, 1, 1), 'none': (0, 0, 0, 0)}
    full = lambda q: [[q] * 2 for _ in range(4)]
    return Mem(4, 2, pat, [lam] * 2, [theta] * 2, [0, 0], [dec] * 4, [dec] * 2, Ap, Am, rho, full(W0), full(F(0)), full(wmax))

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- condition group C (arithmetic only)
def _is_A_pattern(p, A):
    return all(p[i] == 1 for i in A) and all(p[i] == 0 for i in range(len(p)) if i not in A)

def check_C(M, j, A, n, mF, m, plastic=True, bap=True):
    """Coefficient and initial-resource part of the condition group C_LS⁺ (main text 4.3; F0–F2 form). Does not call M.step. Returns (all hold?, item table, derived quantities)."""
    A = sorted(A)
    th, lam, dy = M.theta[j], M.lam[j], M.dy[j]
    items, der = [], {}
    items.append(('P0 protocol switches plastic = bap = 1 (□C_hold)', plastic and bap, ''))
    items.append(('S1 lo_ij ≥ 0 (all entries i)', all(M.lo[i][j] >= 0 for i in range(M.n_in)), ''))
    items.append(('S2 v_reset,j = 0, θ_j > 0, λ_j ≤ 1', M.vreset[j] == 0 and th > 0 and lam <= 1, f'θ={fr(th)} λ={fr(lam)}'))
    items.append(('S3 ρ = 0', M.rho == 0, f'ρ={fr(M.rho)}'))
    indiv = all(all(p[i] == p[A[0]] for i in A) for p in M.pat.values())
    has_probe = any(_is_A_pattern(p, A) for p in M.pat.values())
    items.append(('S4 alphabet A-indecomposable and containing a probe letter that is exactly A', indiv and has_probe, ''))
    items.append(('K1 A₊ > ε', M.Ap > M.eps, f'A₊={fr(M.Ap)} ε={fr(M.eps)}'))
    k2 = dy < 1 and M.Ap * (1 - dy) >= M.Am
    ysup = (1 / (1 - dy)) if dy < 1 else None
    items.append(('K2 d_y < 1 and A₊ ≥ A₋/(1 − d_y) (self-sustainment)', k2,
                  f'A₋/(1−d_y)={fr(M.Am * ysup) if ysup is not None else "∞"}'))
    sb = sum(M.b[i][j] for i in A)
    k3_lhs = lam ** (m + 1) * th + sb
    items.append((f'K3(m={m}) λ^(m+1)·θ + Σ_A b ≤ θ and Σ_A b < θ (baseline subthreshold)', k3_lhs <= th and sb < th,
                  f'LHS={fr(k3_lhs)}, Σ_A b={fr(sb)}'))
    dF = M.Ap - M.Am / (1 - dy ** (mF + 1)) if dy ** (mF + 1) < 1 else None
    der['delta_F'] = dF
    items.append((f'F0 δ_F := A₊ − A₋/(1 − d_y^(m_F+1)) > 0 (m_F={mF})', dF is not None and dF > 0,
                  f'δ_F={fr(dF) if dF is not None else "undefined"}'))
    if dF is not None and dF > 0:
        om = {i: min(M.hi[i][j], M.b[i][j] + n * dF) for i in A}
        der['omega'] = om
        items.append((f'F2a Σ_A min(hi_i, b_i + n·δ_F) ≥ θ (n={n})', sum(om.values()) >= th, f'Σ={fr(sum(om.values()))}'))
        items.append(('F2b min_i min(hi_i − b_i, n·δ_F) > ε', min(min(M.hi[i][j] - M.b[i][j], n * dF) for i in A) > M.eps, ''))
    else:
        items.append((f'F2a Σ_A min(hi_i, b_i + n·δ_F) ≥ θ (n={n})', False, 'δ_F ≤ 0'))
        items.append(('F2b min_i min(hi_i − b_i, n·δ_F) > ε', False, 'δ_F ≤ 0'))
    # upper bounds needed for the non-saturation corollary (not part of C; added separately by Corollary 14-1′)
    dxmax = max(M.dx[i] for i in A)
    if dxmax ** (mF + 1) < 1 and dxmax < 1:
        XF = 1 / (1 - dxmax ** (mF + 1))
        der['Dbar_F'] = max(F(0), M.Ap * XF - M.Am)
        der['x_sup'] = 1 / (1 - dxmax)
    ok = all(v for _, v, _ in items)
    return ok, items, der

def k3_first_clause(M, j, A, m):
    """Weakened condition containing only the first clause of K3 (without Σ_A b < θ); used only in S3b to show that the second clause is necessary, never for any positive determination."""
    return M.lam[j] ** (m + 1) * M.theta[j] + sum(M.b[i][j] for i in A) <= M.theta[j]

def min_margin(M, j, A, mmax=200):
    """Smallest m for which K3 holds (None if there is none)."""
    sb = sum(M.b[i][j] for i in A)
    if sb >= M.theta[j]:
        return None
    for m in range(mmax + 1):
        if M.lam[j] ** (m + 1) * M.theta[j] + sb <= M.theta[j]:
            return m
    return None

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- trajectory regime (inspects only the letter sequence)
def is_quiet(M, j, sig):
    return all(c == 0 for c in M.pat[sig[0]]) and F(sig[1][j]) == 0

def is_pairing(M, j, A, sig):
    p = M.pat[sig[0]]
    return all(p[i] == 1 for i in A) and F(sig[1][j]) >= M.theta[j]

def is_probe(M, j, A, sig):
    return _is_A_pattern(M.pat[sig[0]], A) and F(sig[1][j]) == 0

def f3_mode(M, j, sig):
    """F3: local sufficient source condition on the letter sig of the first pairing beat (arithmetic only).
    'α': Σ_{s_i=1} b_ij < θ_j (removing I_j suffices); 'β': Σ_{s_i=1} b_ik ≥ 0 for all k ≠ j (remove the entry and I_j); returns None if neither holds."""
    s = M.pat[sig[0]]
    if sum(M.b[i][j] for i in range(M.n_in) if s[i]) < M.theta[j]:
        return 'α'
    if all(sum(M.b[i][k] for i in range(M.n_in) if s[i]) >= 0 for k in range(M.n_out) if k != j):
        return 'β'
    return None

def f3_withdrawal(M, j, sig, mode):
    """Same-beat removal letter for F3: α only sets I_j to 0; β also sets the entry to none. The injections into the other units keep their original values."""
    I = tuple(F(0) if jj == j else F(c) for jj, c in enumerate(sig[1]))
    return (sig[0], I) if mode == 'α' else ('none', I)

def check_regime(M, j, A, hist, W, n, mF, m):
    """Trajectory regime (F1, R1, R2 and F3 of main text 4.3). Does not call M.step. Returns dict(ok, why, pairs, probes)."""
    A = sorted(A)
    why = []
    if not all(sig[0] in M.pat and all(F(c) >= 0 for c in sig[1]) for sig in hist):
        why.append('letter not in the declared alphabet, or negative injection')
    pairs, t = [], 0
    while t < len(hist) and len(pairs) < n:
        sig = hist[t]
        if is_pairing(M, j, A, sig):
            if pairs and t - pairs[-1] - 1 < mF:
                why.append(f'fewer than m_F rest beats between pairing beats {pairs[-1]} and {t}'); break
            pairs.append(t)
        elif not is_quiet(M, j, sig):
            why.append(f'formation-period beat {t} is neither a pairing beat nor a rest beat'); break
        t += 1
    if len(pairs) < n:
        why.append('fewer than n pairing beats')
    ts, te = W
    pn = pairs[-1] if pairs else -1
    if not (pn < ts <= te < len(hist)):
        why.append('W is not after the formation period or exceeds the history length')
    probes = [u for u in range(ts, te + 1)
              if is_probe(M, j, A, hist[u]) and u - m >= 0 and all(is_quiet(M, j, hist[r]) for r in range(u - m, u))]
    if not probes:
        why.append('no clean probe beat in W')
    f3 = f3_mode(M, j, hist[pairs[0]]) if pairs else None
    if pairs and f3 is None:
        why.append('F3 fails (the letter of the first pairing beat is neither α nor β)')
    return dict(ok=not why, why=why, pairs=pairs, probes=probes, f3=f3)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- dynamical facts and duty witnesses (runs the dynamics)
def verify(M, j, A, hist, W, n, mF, m, o0=None, pairs=None, probe=None):
    """On a given member, history and window, computes: invariants, formation records, q, r (M1₀ witness), and the 𝒟₁ witnesses for Part and HDyn on the probe beat.
    Criteria: Chapter 11, 3.4, 4.2 (the verdict-1 tier of η), 4.3 (criterion PS and M1₀), 4.4; D16 component-wise threshold crossing; D17 available positions.
    Returns a dict; 'R' is the value of q ∧ r ∧ Part ∧ HDyn under the witnesses this program gives (1, or None = no witness obtained; 0 is never output)."""
    A = sorted(A)
    T = [(i, j) for i in A]
    eps, th = M.eps, M.theta[j]
    pre, recs = M.run(hist, o0)
    out = dict(inv=[], fail=[])
    z = lambda o, k: o[0][k[0]][k[1]] - M.b[k[0]][k[1]]
    # formation records (D16: component-wise threshold crossing)
    form = {k: [] for k in T}
    for t, r in enumerate(recs):
        for (kind, i, jj), c in r['ev'].items():
            k = (i, jj)
            if k in form and kind in ('PLUS', 'MINUS', 'RELAX', 'CLIP') and abs(c) > eps and z(pre[t], k) <= eps < z(pre[t], k) + c:
                form[k].append(t)
    avail = lambda k, t: z(pre[t], k) > eps and any(u < t for u in form[k])
    # invariants
    ysup = 1 / (1 - M.dy[j]) if M.dy[j] < 1 else None
    pn = pairs[-1] if pairs else None
    for t, r in enumerate(recs):
        o = pre[t]
        if not (0 <= o[1][j] < th):
            out['inv'].append(f'I1 beat {t} v_j={fr(o[1][j])}')
        if ysup is not None and not (o[3][j] <= ysup):
            out['inv'].append(f'I2 beat {t} y_j')
        if pn is not None and t > pn:
            if sum(o[0][i][j] for i in A) < th:
                out['inv'].append(f'I3 beat {t} Σ_A w < θ')
            if any(pre[t + 1][0][i][j] < o[0][i][j] for i in A):
                out['inv'].append(f'I3′ beat {t} T position decreased')
            if all(r['s'][i] == 1 for i in A) and r['g'][j] != 1:
                out['inv'].append(f'I4 beat {t} contains A but no firing')
    if pn is not None:
        out['w_after_form'] = {i: pre[pn + 1][0][i][j] for i in A}
    # r: on the first beat with an effective write acting on T, check M1₀ directly with the admissible same-beat removal selected by F3 (no opening ∧ a designated effective write changes);
    #   P4R is recorded only as an optional sufficient antecedent and does not enter the criterion.
    out['r'] = None
    first = None
    for t, r in enumerate(recs):
        hits = [(key, c) for key, c in r['ev'].items() if key[0] in ('PLUS', 'MINUS', 'RELAX', 'CLIP') and (key[1], key[2]) in T and abs(c) > eps]
        if hits:
            first = (t, hits); break
    if first is not None:
        t, hits = first
        o = pre[t]
        p4r = (all(o[0][i][jj] == M.b[i][jj] for i in range(M.n_in) for jj in range(M.n_out))
               and all(q == 0 for q in o[1] + o[2] + o[3]))
        mode = f3_mode(M, j, hist[t])
        detail = dict(t=t, P4R=p4r, mode=mode, n_eff=len(hits), noopen=None, changed=[])
        if mode is not None:
            sig_w = f3_withdrawal(M, j, hist[t], mode)
            _, rq = M.step(o, sig_w)
            noopen = all(rq['g'][jj] <= recs[t]['g'][jj] for jj in range(M.n_out))
            changed = [key for key, c in hits if rq['ev'].get(key) != c]
            detail.update(noopen=noopen, changed=changed, withdrawal=sig_w)
            if noopen and changed:
                out['r'] = 1
        out['r_detail'] = detail
    # q: window-end retention and formation records
    ts, te = W
    qv = all(z(pre[te + 1], k) > eps and any(u <= te for u in form[k]) for k in T)
    out['q'] = 1 if qv else None
    out['z_end'] = {k: z(pre[te + 1], k) for k in T}
    out['form'] = form
    # probe-beat witnesses (𝒟₁: u = t*, set every position of A_T(t*) to b)
    out['part'] = out['hdyn'] = None
    cands = [probe] if probe is not None else list(range(ts, te + 1))
    for tp in cands:
        r = recs[tp]
        if not (_is_A_pattern(r['s'], A) and r['I'][j] == 0):
            continue
        AT = [k for k in T if avail(k, tp)]
        if set(AT) != set(T):
            continue
        o = pre[tp]
        w_cf = [row[:] for row in o[0]]
        for (i, jj) in AT:
            w_cf[i][jj] = M.b[i][jj]
        _, rc = M.step((w_cf, o[1], o[2], o[3]), hist[tp])
        plus = [(('PLUS', i, jj), r['ev'].get(('PLUS', i, jj))) for (i, jj) in T]
        eff = [key for key, c in plus if c is not None and abs(c) > eps]
        fd_plus = [key for key in eff if rc['ev'].get(key) != r['ev'][key]]
        reads_gate = {(i, j) for i in range(M.n_in) if r['s'][i]}          # Structural Positions in the gating read set
        path = bool(set(AT) & reads_gate)
        syn_fd = [('SYN', i, jj) for (i, jj) in AT if rc['ev'].get(('SYN', i, jj)) != r['ev'].get(('SYN', i, jj))]
        sat = [k for k in T if ('CLIP', k[0], k[1]) in r['ev']]
        ns_star = any(M.hi[i][jj] - o[0][i][jj] > eps for (i, jj) in T)   # sufficient condition for the two readings to agree (main text 6.2)
        out['probe'] = dict(t=tp, g=r['g'][j], g_cf=rc['g'][j], vt=r['vt'][j], vt_cf=rc['vt'][j], plus_eff=eff,
                            fd_plus=fd_plus, path=path, syn_fd=syn_fd, sat=sat, ns_star=ns_star,
                            plus_c={key: c for key, c in plus})
        if eff and fd_plus and path:
            out['hdyn'] = 1
        if syn_fd:
            out['part'] = 1
        if out['hdyn'] == 1 and out['part'] == 1:
            break
    out['R'] = 1 if (out['q'] == 1 and out['r'] == 1 and out['part'] == 1 and out['hdyn'] == 1) else None
    out['pre'], out['recs'] = pre, recs
    return out

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 2. Families 1 and 2: linear F family
class Lin:
    """M(a, b, k, γ, ε): two positions z1, z2; the formation letter 'seed' is an out-of-boundary pulse a into z1 (no write); 'drive' writes b + k·z_i to each position;
    every letter relaxes each position by −γ·z_i (omitted when γ = 0); a write with b = k = 0 is omitted. The F layer books only components with |c| > ε (Mathematical Form 4.2).
    phys: 'A' (Family A, z ≤ z_max) or 'E' (Family E, receptor pool R = R0 − 20 − Σwrite/g₁ + Σdecay/g₁ ≥ 0, firing rate y = β(w₀ + z)x ≤ y_max)."""
    def __init__(self, a, b, k, g, eps, phys='A', zmax=F(10), R0=40, g1=F(1, 10), ymax=F(100)):
        self.a, self.b, self.k, self.g, self.eps = F(a), F(b), F(k), F(g), F(eps)
        self.phys, self.zmax, self.R0, self.g1, self.ymax = phys, F(zmax), F(R0), F(g1), F(ymax)
    def procs(self, letter, zz):
        """Returns the list of (process name, target position, component, functional-support set) for the beat; pulses are listed separately."""
        ps = []
        for i in (0, 1):
            if letter == 'drive' and (self.b != 0 or self.k != 0):
                ps.append((f'write{i + 1}', i, self.b + self.k * zz[i], {i} if self.k != 0 else set()))
            if self.g != 0:
                ps.append((f'relax{i + 1}', i, -self.g * zz[i], {i}))
        return ps
    def run(self, hist, W, T=(0,)):
        eps = self.eps
        zf = [F(0), F(0)]          # F layer (books only effective components)
        zm = [F(0), F(0)]          # mechanism layer (full components)
        form = {0: [], 1: []}; mark = {0: [], 1: []}
        legal, compat = True, True
        R = self.R0
        part = hd = 0
        pre = []
        for t, letter in enumerate(hist):
            pre.append(list(zf))
            avail = [zf[i] > eps and any(u < t for u in form[i]) for i in (0, 1)]
            dz = [F(0), F(0)]; dm = [F(0), F(0)]
            if letter == 'seed':
                if self.a > eps:
                    if zf[0] <= eps < zf[0] + self.a:
                        form[0].append(t)
                    mark[0].append(t); dz[0] += self.a
                elif self.a != 0:
                    compat = False
                dm[0] += self.a
                if self.phys == 'E':
                    R -= self.a / self.g1
            for name, i, c, supp in self.procs(letter, zf):
                if 0 < abs(c) <= eps:
                    compat = False
                if abs(c) > eps:
                    if zf[i] <= eps < zf[i] + c:
                        form[i].append(t)
                    dz[i] += c
                    if W[0] <= t <= W[1]:
                        sup_av = any(avail[s] and s in T for s in supp)
                        if sup_av:
                            part = 1
                            if i in T:
                                hd = 1
            for name, i, c, supp in self.procs(letter, zm):
                dm[i] += c
                if self.phys == 'E':
                    R -= c / self.g1
            zf = [zf[i] + dz[i] for i in (0, 1)]
            zm = [zm[i] + dm[i] for i in (0, 1)]
            if any(q < 0 for q in zm):
                legal = False
            if self.phys == 'A' and any(q > self.zmax for q in zm):
                legal = False
            if self.phys == 'E':
                if R < 0:
                    legal = False
                if letter == 'drive' and any((1 + q) > self.ymax for q in zm):
                    legal = False
        pre.append(list(zf))
        q = all(pre[W[1] + 1][i] > eps and any(u <= W[1] for u in form[i]) for i in T)
        r = any(mark[i] for i in T)
        same = (zf == zm)
        return dict(q=int(q), r=int(r), part=part, hdyn=hd, R=int(q and r and part and hd), legal=legal,
                    compat=compat, same=same, z_end=pre[W[1] + 1], zm_end=zm, pool=R)
~~~

A.2  t14\_ext.py  Extension library: upper bounds with strictness flags and certificate probes, F3\*, F3°, blocking units, exhaustive enumeration of same-beat removal domains, joint net increments, the formation recursion Φ and F2′, the three-gate design PA3 in 𝒦\_PA^≺ and C\_PA, the loose-material budget, i.i.d. lower bounds (5.3, 6.2–6.7)

~~~python
"""Appendix program of The Necessity Form of Field-Concretization Qualification: extension library (standard library only, exact rational arithmetic). Depends on t14_core.py.
1. Certificate probes (Lemma 14.7, regime extension R2⁺): the membrane-potential upper bound U(t), computed only from the letter sequence, hi, b, λ and θ, and the certificate-probe test (with a strictness flag); the upper-bound test without the strictness flag is kept for comparison.
2. Source: F3* (a relaxation of F3), enumeration of the admissible same-beat removals, adversarial prefixes with a single blocking unit (Proposition 14-5).
3. Clipping: the net increment of the joint events on T under the joint reading when saturated at window start (Proposition 14-4).
4. Formation threshold: the deterministic formation outcome Φ(g) for a given spacing sequence, the closed form for equal spacing, the improved bound F2′ (Proposition 14-6).
5. Three-gate design in the gated extension class 𝒦_PA^≺ (M ≺ D, Z never fires): independent implementation, condition group C_PA, FD-level witness (Theorem 14-9).
6. Family 1ᵍ (threshold-scour pool-network reading): a non-neural reading of 𝒦_LS members and the loose-material budget (main text 6.7).
7. Random input regime: a lower bound on the probability that a certificate probe occurs under i.i.d. letters (Proposition 14-8).
"""
from fractions import Fraction as F
from itertools import product
import t14_core as C
ZERO = F(0)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 1. Certificate probes
def membrane_upper_nostrict(M, j, A, hist, pn):
    """Upper bound without the strictness flag (for comparison only: it loses the strict-endpoint information; see main text 5.3)."""
    U = {pn + 1: ZERO}
    th, lam = M.theta[j], M.lam[j]
    for t in range(pn + 1, len(hist)):
        name, I = hist[t]
        s = M.pat[name]
        if all(s[i] == 1 for i in A):
            U[t + 1] = ZERO
        else:
            U[t + 1] = min(th, lam * U[t] + sum(M.hi[i][j] for i in range(M.n_in) if s[i]) + F(I[j]))
    return U

def certified_probes_nostrict(M, j, A, hist, W, pn):
    """Probe test without the strictness flag (“< θ”, or “U = θ, λ > 0 and λθ + Σb ≤ θ”; for comparison only)."""
    U = membrane_upper_nostrict(M, j, A, hist, pn)
    sb = sum(M.b[i][j] for i in A)
    th, lam = M.theta[j], M.lam[j]
    out = []
    for t in range(W[0], W[1] + 1):
        if t <= pn or not C.is_probe(M, j, A, hist[t]):
            continue
        u = U[t]
        if lam * u + sb < th or (u >= th and lam > 0 and lam * th + sb <= th):
            out.append(t)
    return out

def membrane_upper_strict(M, j, A, hist, pn):
    """Upper bound with the strictness flag (U(t), strict(t)).
    Meaning: v_j(t) < U(t) when strict is true, otherwise v_j(t) ≤ U(t). Recursion (t > pn):
      U(pn + 1) = 0, non-strict (v = 0); after a beat whose letter contains A, U = 0, non-strict (Lemma 14.4: that beat fires and resets);
      otherwise B := λU + Σ_{s_i=1} hi_ij + I_j; if B ≥ θ, then (θ, strict) (without firing v′ = vt < θ, with firing v′ = 0 < θ);
      if B < θ, then (B, strict ∧ λ > 0) (scaling by a positive λ preserves strictness, translation does not change it, and λ = 0 loses it). Reads only the letter sequence and the declared parameters.
    The values of U are the same as those of the unflagged upper bound; only the strictness flag is added."""
    th, lam = M.theta[j], M.lam[j]
    U = {pn + 1: (ZERO, False)}
    for t in range(pn + 1, len(hist)):
        name, I = hist[t]
        s = M.pat[name]
        u, st = U[t]
        if all(s[i] == 1 for i in A):
            U[t + 1] = (ZERO, False)
            continue
        B = lam * u + sum(M.hi[i][j] for i in range(M.n_in) if s[i]) + F(I[j])
        U[t + 1] = (th, True) if B >= th else (B, st and lam > 0)
    return U

def membrane_upper(M, j, A, hist, pn):
    """Values of U(t)."""
    return {t: u for t, (u, st) in membrane_upper_strict(M, j, A, hist, pn).items()}

def certified_probes(M, j, A, hist, W, pn):
    """Certificate probes (the definition following Lemma 14.7): on a probe beat t* (t* > pn), require λU + Σ_A b ≤ θ if strict and λ > 0; otherwise require λU + Σ_A b < θ."""
    U = membrane_upper_strict(M, j, A, hist, pn)
    sb = sum(M.b[i][j] for i in A)
    th, lam = M.theta[j], M.lam[j]
    out = []
    for t in range(W[0], W[1] + 1):
        if t <= pn or not C.is_probe(M, j, A, hist[t]):
            continue
        u, st = U[t]
        if (st and lam > 0 and lam * u + sb <= th) or lam * u + sb < th:
            out.append(t)
    return out

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 2. Source
def pat_le(p, q):
    return all(a <= b for a, b in zip(p, q))

def f3star(M, j, A, sig):
    """F3*: there is a declared letter a′ (entry pattern position-wise ≤ pat(a₁)) such that (i) pat(a′) is disjoint from A, or Σ_{pat(a′)} b_ij < θ_j; and (ii) for all k ≠ j,
    D_k(a′) := Σ_{pat(a₁) \\ pat(a′)} b_ik ≥ 0. Returns the list of satisfying a′ (sorted by name); α of F3 is a′ = a₁, and β is a′ = none."""
    p1 = M.pat[sig[0]]
    res = []
    for name in sorted(M.pat):
        p = M.pat[name]
        if not pat_le(p, p1):
            continue
        offA = all(p[i] == 0 for i in A)
        ci = offA or sum(M.b[i][j] for i in range(M.n_in) if p[i]) < M.theta[j]
        cii = all(sum(M.b[i][k] for i in range(M.n_in) if p1[i] and not p[i]) >= 0 for k in range(M.n_out) if k != j)
        if ci and cii:
            res.append(name)
    return res

def all_withdrawals(M, sig):
    """Same-beat removal domain of Chapter 11, 3.3: the entry takes a declared letter position-wise no greater than the actual pattern; the teaching current takes, component by component, its original value or 0 (the null removal, which removes nothing, included)."""
    p1 = M.pat[sig[0]]
    names = [n for n in sorted(M.pat) if pat_le(M.pat[n], p1)]
    Is = list(product(*[(F(c),) if F(c) == 0 else (F(c), ZERO) for c in sig[1]]))
    return [(n, I) for n in names for I in Is if (n, I) != (sig[0], tuple(F(c) for c in sig[1]))]

def m10_any(M, j, A, o, sig, eps):
    """Enumerates the admissible same-beat removals on pre-beat state o and letter sig: returns the list of removals for which “no opening ∧ some effective write on T changes” holds."""
    T = [(i, j) for i in A]
    _, ra = M.step(o, sig)
    hits = {k: c for k, c in ra['ev'].items() if k[0] in ('PLUS', 'MINUS', 'RELAX', 'CLIP') and (k[1], k[2]) in T and abs(c) > eps}
    ok = []
    for w in all_withdrawals(M, sig):
        _, rw = M.step(o, w)
        if all(rw['g'][k] <= ra['g'][k] for k in range(M.n_out)) and any(rw['ev'].get(k) != c for k, c in hits.items()):
            ok.append(w)
    return ok, hits

def f3deg(M, j):
    """F3°: θ_k > 0 and v_reset,k < θ_k for all k ≠ j. Then removal down to the rest letter (none, 0) is always an admissible removal on the first pairing beat (λ_k v_k < θ_k),
    and it closes gate j (v_j = 0), so PLUS_ij changes, whatever the inputs and states of the other units (Proposition 14-5(1))."""
    return all(M.theta[k] > 0 and M.vreset[k] < M.theta[k] for k in range(M.n_out) if k != j)

def blocker2(M, j, A, sig):
    """Blocking unit of Proposition 14-5(2): k ≠ j, λ_k·v_reset,k ≥ θ_k, λ_k v_reset,k + Σ_{pat(a₁)} b_ik < θ_k, and, for every candidate a′ satisfying F3*(i),
    λ_k v_reset,k + Σ_{pat(a′)} b_ik ≥ θ_k. Returns k or None.
    Domain of validity: the alphabet satisfies S4 (A-indecomposable). When the alphabet is decomposable, a removal of only part of A can change the writes on T without closing gate j, and the return value of this function does not constitute a block (run_scope G2)."""
    p1 = M.pat[sig[0]]
    cands = []
    for name in sorted(M.pat):
        p = M.pat[name]
        if pat_le(p, p1) and (all(p[i] == 0 for i in A) or sum(M.b[i][j] for i in range(M.n_in) if p[i]) < M.theta[j]):
            cands.append(p)
    for k in range(M.n_out):
        if k == j:
            continue
        base = M.lam[k] * M.vreset[k]
        if base < M.theta[k] or base + sum(M.b[i][k] for i in range(M.n_in) if p1[i]) >= M.theta[k]:
            continue
        if all(base + sum(M.b[i][k] for i in range(M.n_in) if p[i]) >= M.theta[k] for p in cands):
            return k
    return None

def blocker(M, j, A, sig):
    """Narrower single-blocking condition (when v_reset,k = 0 the candidate none is always an admissible removal and F3° holds, so this condition cannot be satisfied; for comparison only, not used in the verification): k ≠ j, λ_k > 0, v_reset,k = 0, 0 ≤ θ_k − Σ_{pat(a₁)} b_ik < λ_k θ_k,
    and D_k(a′) < 0 for every candidate a′ satisfying F3*(i). Returns (k, δ) or None."""
    p1 = M.pat[sig[0]]
    cands = []
    for name in sorted(M.pat):
        p = M.pat[name]
        if not pat_le(p, p1):
            continue
        offA = all(p[i] == 0 for i in A)
        if offA or sum(M.b[i][j] for i in range(M.n_in) if p[i]) < M.theta[j]:
            cands.append(p)
    for k in range(M.n_out):
        if k == j or M.lam[k] <= 0 or M.vreset[k] != 0:
            continue
        base1 = sum(M.b[i][k] for i in range(M.n_in) if p1[i])
        gap = M.theta[k] - base1
        if not (0 <= gap < M.lam[k] * M.theta[k]):
            continue
        Ds = [sum(M.b[i][k] for i in range(M.n_in) if p1[i] and not p[i]) for p in cands]
        if cands and all(D < 0 for D in Ds):
            delta = min(min(-D for D in Ds), gap) / 2 if gap > 0 else ZERO
            if gap > 0:
                return k, delta
    return None

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 3. Clipping
def joint_nets(V, M, j, A, W):
    """Joint net increment of the T positions at each beat in W (post-beat − pre-beat)."""
    pre = V['pre']
    return {(i, t): pre[t + 1][0][i][j] - pre[t][0][i][j] for t in range(W[0], W[1] + 1) for i in A}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 4. Formation threshold
def formation_outcome(M, j, A, gaps, n):
    """Deterministic formation: no events before the first pairing, n pairings, and gaps[k] rest beats for j between pairings k and k+1 (only the target column is computed; the other units do not affect it).
    Returns (dict of w_i(p_n + 1), table of increments per pairing)."""
    x = {i: ZERO for i in A}; y = ZERO
    w = {i: M.b[i][j] for i in A}
    incs = []
    dy = M.dy[j]
    for k in range(n):
        if k:
            g = gaps[k - 1]
            x = {i: M.dx[i] ** g * x[i] for i in A}
            y = dy ** g * y
        x = {i: M.dx[i] * x[i] + 1 for i in A}
        y = dy * y + 1
        inc = {}
        for i in A:
            S = w[i] + M.Ap * x[i] - M.Am * y
            nw = min(M.hi[i][j], max(M.lo[i][j], S))
            inc[i] = nw - w[i]; w[i] = nw
        incs.append(inc)
    return w, incs

def f2prime(M, j, A, n, mF):
    """F2′: Σ_A min(hi_i, b_i + Σ_{k=1..n}(A₊ − A₋·Ȳ_k)), Ȳ_k = (1 − ρ^k)/(1 − ρ), ρ = d_y^(m_F+1) (Ȳ_k = k when ρ = 1)."""
    rho = M.dy[j] ** (mF + 1)
    tot = ZERO
    for k in range(1, n + 1):
        Yk = (1 - rho ** k) / (1 - rho) if rho != 1 else F(k)
        tot += M.Ap - M.Am * Yk
    return sum(min(M.hi[i][j], M.b[i][j] + tot) for i in A), tot

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 5. Three-gate design in 𝒦_PA^≺
class PA3:
    """Independent implementation of 𝒦_PA^≺ members (Chapter 13 v1.0, Definitions 5 and 7), restricted to the design of this paper: gating units M = 0, D = 1, Z = 2, order M ≺ D ≺ Z,
    U_MD = u (all other U = 0), gate_of = (D, Z, Z); lo and hi are scalars (as in t13_gate of the Chapter 13 appendix)."""
    def __init__(self, n_in, pat, lam, theta, dx, dy, Ap, Am, u, bM, hi, eps=F(1, 100)):
        self.n_in, self.pat = n_in, dict(pat)
        self.lam, self.theta = [F(q) for q in lam], [F(q) for q in theta]
        self.dx, self.dy = [F(q) for q in dx], [F(q) for q in dy]
        self.Ap, self.Am, self.u = F(Ap), F(Am), F(u)
        self.b = [[F(bM[i]), ZERO, ZERO] for i in range(n_in)]
        self.lo, self.hi, self.eps = ZERO, F(hi), F(eps)
        self.gate_of = (1, 2, 2)
    def init(self):
        return ([[q for q in r] for r in self.b], [ZERO] * 3, [ZERO] * self.n_in, [ZERO] * 3)
    def step(self, o, sig):
        w, v, x, y = o
        name, I = sig
        s = self.pat[name]
        I = [F(c) for c in I]
        vt = [None] * 3; g = [None] * 3
        for jj in (0, 1, 2):
            vt[jj] = self.lam[jj] * v[jj] + sum(w[i][jj] for i in range(self.n_in) if s[i]) + I[jj] + (self.u * g[0] if jj == 1 and g[0] else ZERO)
            g[jj] = 1 if vt[jj] >= self.theta[jj] else 0
        v2 = [ZERO if g[jj] else vt[jj] for jj in range(3)]
        x2 = [self.dx[i] * x[i] + s[i] for i in range(self.n_in)]
        y2 = [self.dy[jj] * y[jj] + g[jj] for jj in range(3)]
        ev = {}; w2 = [[None] * 3 for _ in range(self.n_in)]
        for i in range(self.n_in):
            for jj in range(3):
                G = self.gate_of[jj]
                S = w[i][jj]
                if g[G]:
                    c = self.Ap * x2[i]; ev[('PLUS', i, jj)] = c; S += c
                if s[i]:
                    c = -self.Am * y2[G]; ev[('MINUS', i, jj)] = c; S += c
                nw = min(self.hi, max(self.lo, S))
                if nw != S:
                    ev[('CLIP', i, jj)] = nw - S
                w2[i][jj] = nw
        return (w2, v2, x2, y2), dict(s=s, I=I, vt=vt, g=g, ev=ev)
    def run(self, hist, o0=None):
        o = o0 if o0 is not None else self.init()
        pre, recs = [o], []
        for sig in hist:
            o, r = self.step(o, sig)
            pre.append(o); recs.append(r)
        return pre, recs
    def as_t13(self):
        """Parameter format of t13_gate in the Chapter 13 appendix (mode = 'dag')."""
        U = [[ZERO] * 3 for _ in range(3)]; U[0][1] = self.u
        return dict(mode='dag', order=[0, 1, 2], U=U, lam=self.lam, theta=self.theta, vres=[ZERO] * 3, dx=self.dx, dy=self.dy,
                    Ap=self.Ap, Am=self.Am, rho=ZERO, b=self.b, lo=self.lo, hi=self.hi, gate_of=list(self.gate_of), plastic=True, bap=True)

def check_C_PA(P, A, n, mF, m):
    """C_PA (main text 6.6): K1, K2_D, K3_M(m), PD1 (u ≥ θ_D), the declaration that Z never fires (θ_Z > 0 and I_Z = 0 in the regime), formation F0_D, F2_M. Arithmetic only."""
    items = []
    thM, thD = P.theta[0], P.theta[1]
    sb = sum(P.b[i][0] for i in A)
    items.append(('K1 A₊ > ε', P.Ap > P.eps))
    items.append(('K2_D d_y,D < 1 and A₊(1 − d_y,D) ≥ A₋', P.dy[1] < 1 and P.Ap * (1 - P.dy[1]) >= P.Am))
    items.append((f'K3_M(m={m})', P.lam[0] ** (m + 1) * thM + sb <= thM and sb < thM))
    items.append(('PD1 u ≥ θ_D', P.u >= thD and thD > 0))
    items.append(('Z θ_Z > 0', P.theta[2] > 0))
    rho = P.dy[1] ** (mF + 1)
    dF = P.Ap - P.Am / (1 - rho) if rho < 1 else None
    items.append(('F0_D δ_F > 0', dF is not None and dF > 0))
    ok2 = dF is not None and dF > 0 and sum(min(P.hi, P.b[i][0] + n * dF) for i in A) >= thM and \
        min(min(P.hi - P.b[i][0], n * dF) for i in A) > P.eps
    items.append((f'F2_M (n={n})', ok2))
    indiv = all(all(p[i] == p[A[0]] for i in A) for p in P.pat.values())
    items.append(('S4 A-indecomposable, contains a probe letter', indiv and any(all(p[i] == (1 if i in A else 0) for i in range(P.n_in)) for p in P.pat.values())))
    return all(v for _, v in items), items

def pa_witness(P, A, hist, W, tp):
    """FD-level witness: PLUS_iM is effective on probe beat tp; after T = {(i, M)} is set to b, neither M nor D fires and PLUS_iM does not occur; actual event-graph path T → M → D → PLUS;
    window-end retention; Z never fires; invariants. Returns a dict."""
    pre, recs = P.run(hist)
    T = [(i, 0) for i in A]
    o = pre[tp]; r = recs[tp]
    wcf = [row[:] for row in o[0]]
    for (i, jj) in T:
        wcf[i][jj] = P.b[i][jj]
    _, rc = P.step((wcf, o[1], o[2], o[3]), hist[tp])
    eff = [(i, 0) for i in A if abs(r['ev'].get(('PLUS', i, 0), ZERO)) > P.eps]
    fd = [k for k in eff if ('PLUS', k[0], 0) not in rc['ev']]
    path = r['g'][0] == 1 and r['g'][1] == 1 and rc['g'][0] == 0 and rc['g'][1] == 0
    z_end = {i: pre[W[1] + 1][0][i][0] - P.b[i][0] for i in A}
    zfire = any(rr['g'][2] for rr in recs)
    sat = [i for i in A if ('CLIP', i, 0) in r['ev']]
    return dict(eff=eff, fd=fd, path=path, q=all(zz > P.eps for zz in z_end.values()), z_end=z_end, zfire=zfire,
                gM=r['g'][0], gD=r['g'][1], gM_cf=rc['g'][0], gD_cf=rc['g'][1], sat=sat, pre=pre, recs=recs)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 6. Family 1ᵍ
def loose_store(M, j, recs, L0):
    """Loose-material store at sink j under the Family 1ᵍ reading: L(t+1) = L(t) + Σ_i (scour PLUS_ij) − Σ_i |clogging MINUS_ij|; returns the beat-by-beat values and the minimum."""
    L = [F(L0)]
    for r in recs:
        add = sum(c for k, c in r['ev'].items() if k[0] == 'PLUS' and k[2] == j)
        sub = sum(-c for k, c in r['ev'].items() if k[0] == 'MINUS' and k[2] == j)
        L.append(L[-1] + add - sub)
    return L, min(L)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 7. Random input
def iid_lower_bound(pq, pp, m, L):
    """I.i.d. letters: at each beat the probability of a rest letter for j is pq and that of the probe letter is pp. Lower bound on the probability that a window of length L contains an m-clean probe:
    1 − (1 − pq^m·pp)^⌊L/(m+1)⌋ (disjoint-block argument)."""
    p = pq ** m * pp
    return 1 - (1 - p) ** (L // (m + 1))
~~~

A.3  snn\_core.py  Verbatim identical to Appendix A.1 of *The Adjudication Form of Field Concretization for Candidate Hosts* v1.0: the frozen SNN (only loaded and checked)

~~~python
"""Frozen SNN instance s1: a replayable SNN instance (program host). Exact rational arithmetic, Python standard library, deterministic, replayable event by event.
Host instance χ_SNN = (P, L, B, W):
  P this program; L the algorithmic level (discrete time steps); B = {4 input neurons, 2 output LIF neurons, 8 synapses}; W the declared window.
  Input-neuron firing is fixed by an external stimulus sequence (out-of-boundary signal); the teaching current teach is injected into output 0 only during the formation phase (out-of-boundary signal).
  Output neurons: LIF, v ← v·LEAK + Σ w_ij·s_i + I_ext; when v ≥ θ the neuron fires and resets to 0.
  Plasticity: pair-based STDP (trace form): presynaptic traces x_i and postsynaptic traces y_j are multiplied by DEC each step and incremented by 1 on firing;
    when postsynaptic neuron j fires (via the backpropagated signal reaching the synapse), w_ij += A_PLUS·x_i; when presynaptic neuron i fires, w_ij -= A_MINUS·y_j;
    weights are clipped to [0, W_MAX]. Optional relaxation toward baseline (RELAX, default 0).
  Intervention switches: plastic (plasticity on/off); bap (whether postsynaptic firing propagates back to the synapse; when off, STDP does not see postsynaptic firing, i.e. the route is cut).
Trace-Response: above-baseline weight z_ij = w_ij − W0.
"""
from fractions import Fraction as F
import hashlib
N_IN, N_OUT = 4, 2
W0 = F(1, 4)
LEAK, THETA, DEC = F(3, 4), F(1), F(1, 2)
A_PLUS, A_MINUS, W_MAX = F(1, 8), F(1, 16), F(2)
PAT = {'A': (1, 1, 0, 0), 'B': (0, 0, 1, 1), 'AB': (1, 1, 1, 1), 'none': (0, 0, 0, 0)}

def init(w=None):
    return dict(w=[[W0 if w is None else w[i][j] for j in range(N_OUT)] for i in range(N_IN)],
                v=[F(0)] * N_OUT, x=[F(0)] * N_IN, y=[F(0)] * N_OUT)

def step(st, inp, teach=(0, 0), plastic=True, bap=True, relax=F(0)):
    """One step: returns (new state, event record). The event record lists presynaptic firing, postsynaptic firing and the plasticity components of each synapse (positive, negative, relaxation)."""
    w = [row[:] for row in st['w']]
    s_in = PAT[inp]
    v = [st['v'][j] * LEAK + sum(w[i][j] * s_in[i] for i in range(N_IN)) + teach[j] for j in range(N_OUT)]
    s_out = [1 if v[j] >= THETA else 0 for j in range(N_OUT)]
    v = [F(0) if s_out[j] else v[j] for j in range(N_OUT)]
    x = [st['x'][i] * DEC + s_in[i] for i in range(N_IN)]
    y = [st['y'][j] * DEC + s_out[j] for j in range(N_OUT)]
    comp = {}
    for i in range(N_IN):
        for j in range(N_OUT):
            pos = A_PLUS * x[i] if (plastic and bap and s_out[j]) else F(0)
            neg = -A_MINUS * y[j] if (plastic and s_in[i] and bap) else F(0)
            rel = -relax * (w[i][j] - W0) if plastic else F(0)
            nw = min(W_MAX, max(F(0), w[i][j] + pos + neg + rel))
            comp[(i, j)] = (pos, neg, rel, nw - w[i][j])
            w[i][j] = nw
    ev = dict(inp=inp, teach=teach, s_in=s_in, s_out=tuple(s_out), comp=comp)
    return dict(w=w, v=v, x=x, y=y), ev

def run(hist, st=None, **kw):
    """hist: [(input name, teaching current)]. Returns (final state, event log)."""
    st = st or init()
    log = []
    for inp, teach in hist:
        st, ev = step(st, inp, teach, **kw)
        log.append(ev)
    return st, log

def log_hash(log):
    """Fingerprint of the frozen log: SHA-256 of the events serialized in a fixed order."""
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    for ev in log:
        h.update(repr((ev['inp'], ev['teach'], ev['s_in'], ev['s_out'],
                       sorted((k, tuple(str(c) for c in v)) for k, v in ev['comp'].items()))).encode())
    return h.hexdigest()

def z(st):
    return [[st['w'][i][j] - W0 for j in range(N_OUT)] for i in range(N_IN)]
~~~

A.4  gate\_class.py  Verbatim identical to Appendix A.2 of *The Adjudication Form of Field Concretization for Candidate Hosts* v1.0: Definitions 1–3 of 𝒦\_g and Gate.step

~~~python
"""Independent implementation of the formal definition of the event-gating class 𝒦_g (main text 3.1, Definitions 1–3), and beat-by-beat finite verification that “the frozen SNN is a member of 𝒦_g” (main text, Proposition 1).
Standard library only, exact rational arithmetic. Run: python3 gate_class.py
The frozen snn_core.py (Appendix A.1) is loaded only as the object under check; its SHA-256 is verified before loading.
Parameters of a K_g member G (Definition 1):
  n_in, n_out          number of entry units, number of gating units; Structural Positions X = {(i, j)} (fully connected)
  pat: Σ_in -> {0,1}^n_in  entry letter to entry pulse vector (including rest 'none' -> zero vector)
  lam[j], theta[j], vreset[j]   leak, threshold, reset value
  dx[i], dy[j]         eligibility-trace decay
  Ap, Am, rho          potentiation amplitude, depression amplitude, relaxation rate
  b[i][j], lo[i][j], hi[i][j]   baseline, valid interval
Protocol switches (declared items, not part of the state): plastic, bap
Letter σ = (entry name, I vector); I is the out-of-boundary injection (teaching current).
"""
import sys, hashlib, random, itertools, importlib.util
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
SNN_DIR = __import__('os').path.dirname(__import__('os').path.abspath(__file__))   # the frozen snn_core.py sits in the same directory as this program (Appendix A.1)
FROZEN_SHA = {'snn_core.py': '4e0fd45d2442890ecf3a7687ea285f89ed161e6fb4ab1083ca0fb79821960e49'}

class Gate:
    """A member of K_g (Definition 1). State o = (w, v, x, y); w[i][j] are the Structural Positions, v, x, y the auxiliary positions."""
    def __init__(self, n_in, n_out, pat, lam, theta, vreset, dx, dy, Ap, Am, rho, b, lo, hi):
        self.n_in, self.n_out, self.pat = n_in, n_out, dict(pat)
        self.lam, self.theta, self.vreset = list(lam), list(theta), list(vreset)
        self.dx, self.dy = list(dx), list(dy)
        self.Ap, self.Am, self.rho = Ap, Am, rho
        self.b, self.lo, self.hi = b, lo, hi
        self.check_domain()
    def check_domain(self):
        """Parameter constraints of Definition 1; anything that violates them is not a K_g member."""
        assert 'none' in self.pat and all(v == 0 for v in self.pat['none'])
        assert all(len(p) == self.n_in and all(c in (0, 1) for c in p) for p in self.pat.values())
        assert all(0 <= l <= 1 for l in self.lam) and all(0 <= d <= 1 for d in self.dx + self.dy)
        assert 0 <= self.rho <= 1 and self.Ap >= 0 and self.Am >= 0
        for i in range(self.n_in):
            for j in range(self.n_out):
                assert self.lo[i][j] <= self.b[i][j] <= self.hi[i][j]
        return True
    # ---------- Definition 2: per-beat execution order (eight stages, reading the pre-beat state) ----------
    def step(self, o, sigma, plastic=True, bap=True):
        """Returns (o', rec). rec lists the gate arguments vt, g, and for each synapse (P+, P-, Pr, pre-clip sum, post-clip value)."""
        w, v, x, y = o
        name, I = sigma
        s = self.pat[name]                                                     # (1) read input
        vt = [self.lam[j] * v[j] + sum(w[i][j] * s[i] for i in range(self.n_in)) + I[j]
              for j in range(self.n_out)]                                      # (2) integrate (pre-beat w)
        g = [1 if vt[j] >= self.theta[j] else 0 for j in range(self.n_out)]    # (3) gate, comparison >=
        v2 = [self.vreset[j] if g[j] else vt[j] for j in range(self.n_out)]    # (4) reset
        x2 = [self.dx[i] * x[i] + s[i] for i in range(self.n_in)]              # (5) eligibility traces
        y2 = [self.dy[j] * y[j] + g[j] for j in range(self.n_out)]
        w2 = [[None] * self.n_out for _ in range(self.n_in)]
        comp = {}
        for i in range(self.n_in):
            for j in range(self.n_out):
                pp = self.Ap * x2[i] if (plastic and bap and g[j]) else F(0)   # (6) potentiation: only if g_j = 1
                pm = -self.Am * y2[j] if (plastic and bap and s[i]) else F(0)  #     depression: only if s_i = 1
                pr = -self.rho * (w[i][j] - self.b[i][j]) if (plastic and self.rho != 0) else F(0)
                S = w[i][j] + pp + pm + pr
                nw = min(self.hi[i][j], max(self.lo[i][j], S))                 # (7) clipping
                w2[i][j] = nw
                comp[(i, j)] = (pp, pm, pr, S, nw)
        rec = dict(s=s, I=tuple(I), vt=vt, g=tuple(g), comp=comp)              # (8) read out g
        return (w2, v2, x2, y2), rec
    def init(self, w=None):
        return ([[self.b[i][j] if w is None else w[i][j] for j in range(self.n_out)] for i in range(self.n_in)],
                [F(0)] * self.n_out, [F(0)] * self.n_in, [F(0)] * self.n_out)
    def run(self, hist, o=None, **kw):
        o = o or self.init()
        traj, recs = [o], []
        for sig in hist:
            o, r = self.step(o, sig, **kw)
            traj.append(o); recs.append(r)
        return traj, recs

def snn_params(S):
    """Reads the K_g parameters from the current global constants of the snn_core module (correspondence between Definition 1 and lines 15–20 of snn_core; main text, Proposition 1)."""
    n_in, n_out = S.N_IN, S.N_OUT
    full = lambda val: [[val] * n_out for _ in range(n_in)]
    return dict(n_in=n_in, n_out=n_out, pat=S.PAT, lam=[S.LEAK] * n_out, theta=[S.THETA] * n_out,
                vreset=[F(0)] * n_out, dx=[S.DEC] * n_in, dy=[S.DEC] * n_out, Ap=S.A_PLUS, Am=S.A_MINUS,
                b=full(S.W0), lo=full(F(0)), hi=full(S.W_MAX))

def load_snn_core():
    p = SNN_DIR + '/snn_core.py'
    h = hashlib.sha256(open(p, 'rb').read()).hexdigest()
    assert h == FROZEN_SHA['snn_core.py'], 'snn_core.py is not the frozen version'
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('snn_core', p)
    m = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec); spec.loader.exec_module(m)
    return m, h

def snn_gate(S, rho=F(0)):
    return Gate(rho=rho, **snn_params(S))

def WIN0():
    return [('A', (0, 0)), ('none', (0, 0)), ('none', (0, 0)), ('B', (0, 0)), ('none', (0, 0)), ('none', (0, 0))] * 3

def compare_run(S, G, hist, w_init=None, o_init=None, plastic=True, bap=True, relax=F(0)):
    """Runs the same history through snn_core.step and through G.step and compares the full state and the plasticity components beat by beat.
    Returns (number of beats, number of clipping events, number of times the gate argument equals the threshold exactly, list of mismatches)."""
    if o_init is not None:
        st = dict(w=[r[:] for r in o_init[0]], v=list(o_init[1]), x=list(o_init[2]), y=list(o_init[3]))
        o = (o_init[0], o_init[1], o_init[2], o_init[3])
    else:
        st = S.init(w_init); o = G.init(w_init)
    bad, nclip, ntie = [], 0, 0
    for t, (inp, teach) in enumerate(hist):
        st, ev = S.step(st, inp, teach, plastic=plastic, bap=bap, relax=relax)
        o, r = G.step(o, (inp, teach), plastic=plastic, bap=bap)
        ntie += sum(1 for j in range(G.n_out) if r['vt'][j] == G.theta[j])
        if st['w'] != o[0] or st['v'] != o[1] or st['x'] != o[2] or st['y'] != o[3]:
            bad.append((t, 'state'))
        if tuple(ev['s_out']) != r['g'] or tuple(ev['s_in']) != tuple(r['s']):
            bad.append((t, 'spikes'))
        for k, (pp, pm, pr, Ssum, nw) in r['comp'].items():
            c = ev['comp'][k]
            w_before = Ssum - pp - pm - pr
            if (c[0], c[1], c[2], c[3]) != (pp, pm, pr, nw - w_before):
                bad.append((t, 'comp', k))
            if nw != Ssum:
                nclip += 1
    return len(hist), nclip, ntie, bad

def main():
    S, h = load_snn_core()
    print('1. Frozen-version verification')
    print('  snn_core.py SHA-256 =', h, '(matches frozen value)')
    base = {k: getattr(S, k) for k in ['LEAK', 'THETA', 'DEC', 'A_PLUS', 'A_MINUS', 'W0', 'W_MAX']}
    def setp(**kw):
        for k, val in base.items():
            setattr(S, k, kw.get(k, val))
    G = snn_gate(S)
    print('  Definition 1 parameter domain: λ =', G.lam[0], ', d =', G.dx[0], ', ρ = 0 (RELAX default), A₊ =', G.Ap, ', A₋ =', G.Am,
          ', b =', G.b[0][0], ', [lo, hi] = [', G.lo[0][0], ',', G.hi[0][0], '], θ =', G.theta[0], ', v_reset =',
          G.vreset[0], '; entry vectors', sorted(G.pat.items()), '— all constraints satisfied:', G.check_domain())
    FORM = [('A', (F(1), 0)), ('none', (0, 0)), ('none', (0, 0))] * 6
    CTRL = [('A', (0, 0)), ('none', (0, 0)), ('none', (0, 0))] * 6
    REST = [('none', (0, 0))] * 8
    WIN = WIN0()
    tot = dict(ticks=0, clip=0, tie=0, bad=[])
    def acc(res, label):
        n, c, ti, bad = res
        tot['ticks'] += n; tot['clip'] += c; tot['tie'] += ti; tot['bad'] += [(label,) + b for b in bad]
        return n, c, ti, len(bad)
    print('2. Frozen declared history (as used by snn_instance.py), compared beat by beat: step of K_g Definition 2 vs snn_core.step')
    rows = []
    rows.append(('FORM (formation phase)', acc(compare_run(S, G, FORM), 'FORM')))
    rows.append(('CTRL (no-teaching control)', acc(compare_run(S, G, CTRL), 'CTRL')))
    rows.append(('FORM+REST+WIN (with Trace-Response)', acc(compare_run(S, G, FORM + REST + WIN), 'FRW')))
    st_r = S.run(REST, st=S.run(FORM)[0])[0]
    o_er = (S.init()['w'], st_r['v'], st_r['x'], st_r['y'])
    rows.append(('erasure-side WIN (R1(a′): weights reset, v, x, y carried over from the end-of-rest state, plasticity on)', acc(compare_run(S, G, WIN, o_init=o_er), 'ERASE')))
    o_r = (st_r['w'], st_r['v'], st_r['x'], st_r['y'])
    rows.append(('route-cut WIN (bap off)', acc(compare_run(S, G, WIN, o_init=o_r, bap=False), 'BAPOFF')))
    rows.append(('frozen-plasticity WIN (plastic off)', acc(compare_run(S, G, WIN, o_init=o_r, plastic=False), 'FROZEN')))
    rows.append(('relaxation RELAX = 1/10, FORM+REST+WIN', acc(compare_run(S, snn_gate(S, F(1, 10)), FORM + REST + WIN, relax=F(1, 10)), 'RELAX')))
    for lab, (n, c, ti, nb) in rows:
        print(f'  {lab}: {n} beats, clipping {c}, gate argument = θ {ti} times, mismatches {nb}')
    print('3. Parameter grid (the 108 groups of snn_scan.py; snn_core global constants rewritten as it does, K_g parameters read out alongside)')
    ng, tie_grid = 0, 0
    for W0, AP, teach, n in itertools.product([F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(3, 8), F(1, 2)], [F(1, 16), F(1, 8), F(1, 4)],
                                              [F(1, 2), F(1), F(2)], [1, 3, 6]):
        setp(W0=W0, A_PLUS=AP)
        Gp = snn_gate(S)
        form = ([('A', (teach, 0))] + [('none', (0, 0))] * 2) * n
        ctrl = ([('A', (0, 0))] + [('none', (0, 0))] * 2) * n
        tie_grid += acc(compare_run(S, Gp, form + REST + WIN0()), 'GRID')[2]
        tie_grid += acc(compare_run(S, Gp, ctrl), 'GRIDC')[2]
        ng += 1
    setp()
    print(f'  {ng} groups × 2 histories done; beats with gate argument exactly θ: {tie_grid} (at W0 = 1/2 the baseline input is 1/2 + 1/2 = θ)')
    print('4. Random histories (seed 20261101; 300 histories of length 60; letters cover the four entry patterns and two teaching currents in {0, 1/2, 1, 2};')
    print('    initial weights random rationals in [0, 2]; protocol switches and RELAX ∈ {0, 1/10, 1/2} random)')
    rng = random.Random(20261101)
    tv = [F(0), F(1, 2), F(1), F(2)]
    for _ in range(300):
        rho = rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 10), F(1, 2)])
        Gr = snn_gate(S, rho)
        hist = [(rng.choice(['A', 'B', 'AB', 'none', 'none']), (rng.choice(tv), rng.choice(tv))) for _ in range(60)]
        w0 = [[F(rng.randint(0, 16), 8) for _ in range(S.N_OUT)] for _ in range(S.N_IN)]
        acc(compare_run(S, Gr, hist, w_init=w0, plastic=rng.random() < 0.85, bap=rng.random() < 0.85, relax=rho), 'RAND')
    print('5. Saturation history (tests the clipping branch): A_PLUS = 1/2, A_MINUS = 1/2, strong teaching pairings alternating with presynaptic-only firing')
    setp(A_PLUS=F(1, 2), A_MINUS=F(1, 2))
    Gs = snn_gate(S)
    sat = ([('AB', (F(2), F(2)))] * 6 + [('A', (0, 0))] * 6 + [('B', (F(2), 0)), ('none', (0, 0))] * 6) * 2
    n, c, ti, nb = acc(compare_run(S, Gs, sat), 'SAT')
    setp()
    print(f'  {n} beats, clipping {c}, mismatches {nb}')
    print('6. Totals')
    print(f'  beats compared: {tot["ticks"]}; clipping took effect at {tot["clip"]} places; gate argument exactly at threshold {tot["tie"]} times; mismatches at {len(tot["bad"])} places')
    print('  Conclusion (finite verification): on all histories listed above, the per-beat execution of K_g Definition 2 agrees exactly with snn_core.step, beat by beat and component by component, '
          'including the clipping branch and the at-threshold branch.' if not tot['bad'] else '  Mismatches found: ' + repr(tot['bad'][:10]))

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
~~~

A.5  r\_layer\_witness.py  Verbatim identical to Appendix A.3 of *The Adjudication Form of Field Concretization for Candidate Hosts* v1.0: R-layer witnesses, slot dependency graph, Lemmas R1–R3

~~~python
"""R-layer declared instance of 𝒦_g for *The Mathematical Form of Field Concretization* (main text 3.2, Definitions 4–7): event witness Ev, slot-dependency graph, finite verification of Lemmas R1 (K_t closure), R2 (read set) and R3 (non-interference), and a beat-by-beat check against witnesses generated independently from the frozen SNN log.
Standard library only, exact rational arithmetic. Run: python3 r_layer_witness.py
"""
import sys, random, io, contextlib
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
import gate_class as GC
B, EXT = 'B', 'out-of-boundary'
HALF = F(1, 2)

def gate_reads(G, s, j):
    return frozenset([('v', j)] + [('w', i, j) for i in range(G.n_in) if s[i]])

def Ev(G, o, sigma, t, plastic=True, bap=True):
    """Event witness of Definition 5 (a, κ, η are certified separately; see gate_criteria.py). Uses only the pre-beat state o and the letter σ. Returns {id: dict}."""
    w, v, x, y = o
    name, I = sigma
    s = G.pat[name]
    out = {}
    def add(kind, idx, car, tgt, c, reads):
        eid = (kind, idx, t)
        assert eid not in out
        out[eid] = dict(id=eid, t=t, car=car, tgt=tgt, c=c, reads=frozenset(reads))
    vt = [G.lam[j] * v[j] + sum(w[i][j] * s[i] for i in range(G.n_in)) + I[j] for j in range(G.n_out)]
    g = [1 if vt[j] >= G.theta[j] else 0 for j in range(G.n_out)]
    for j in range(G.n_out):
        gr = gate_reads(G, s, j)
        add('LEAK', (j,), B, ('v', j), (G.lam[j] - 1) * v[j], {('v', j)})
        for i in range(G.n_in):
            if s[i]:
                add('SYN', (i, j), B, ('v', j), w[i][j], {('w', i, j)})
        if I[j] != 0:
            add('INJ', (j,), EXT, ('v', j), F(I[j]), set())
        if g[j]:
            add('RESET', (j,), B, ('v', j), G.vreset[j] - vt[j], gr)
            add('YIN', (j,), B, ('y', j), F(1), gr)
        add('YDEC', (j,), B, ('y', j), (G.dy[j] - 1) * y[j], {('y', j)})
    for i in range(G.n_in):
        add('XDEC', (i,), B, ('x', i), (G.dx[i] - 1) * x[i], {('x', i)})
        if s[i]:
            add('XIN', (i,), B, ('x', i), F(1), set())
    for i in range(G.n_in):
        for j in range(G.n_out):
            gr = gate_reads(G, s, j)
            S = w[i][j]
            if plastic and bap and g[j]:
                c = G.Ap * (G.dx[i] * x[i] + s[i]); S += c
                add('PLUS', (i, j), B, ('w', i, j), c, gr | {('x', i)})
            if plastic and bap and s[i]:
                c = -G.Am * (G.dy[j] * y[j] + g[j]); S += c
                add('MINUS', (i, j), B, ('w', i, j), c, gr | {('y', j)})
            if plastic and G.rho != 0:
                c = -G.rho * (w[i][j] - G.b[i][j]); S += c
                add('RELAX', (i, j), B, ('w', i, j), c, {('w', i, j)})
            cl = min(G.hi[i][j], max(G.lo[i][j], S))
            if cl != S:
                add('CLIP', (i, j), B, ('w', i, j), cl - S, gr | {('w', i, j), ('x', i), ('y', j)})
    return out

def slot_deps(G, sigma, plastic=True, bap=True):
    """Definition 7: slot dependency. For a given letter (independent of the state), returns {post-state node n: set of pre-state nodes}, i.e. every pre-beat node on which n(t+1) depends:
    the union of the read sets of all event slots pointing to n (whether each occurs is fixed by the state), plus the node itself for Structural Positions (persistence edge)."""
    s = G.pat[sigma[0]]
    dep = {}
    for j in range(G.n_out):
        gr = set(gate_reads(G, s, j))
        dep[('v', j)] = set(gr)                 # LEAK reads v; SYN reads active w; RESET slot reads gr
        dep[('y', j)] = {('y', j)} | gr         # YDEC reads y; YIN slot reads gr
    for i in range(G.n_in):
        dep[('x', i)] = {('x', i)}
    for i in range(G.n_in):
        for j in range(G.n_out):
            d = {('w', i, j)}                   # persistence
            if plastic and bap:
                d |= set(gate_reads(G, s, j)) | {('x', i)}   # PLUS slot
                if s[i]:
                    d |= {('y', j)}                          # MINUS slot
            d |= {('x', i), ('y', j)} if plastic and bap else set()   # CLIP slot (upper bound by union)
            dep[('w', i, j)] = d
    return dep

def slot_reads(G, e):
    """Slot read set of event e (the pre-beat nodes on which its occurrence and component depend; identical to the reads given by Ev, listed separately to show that it depends only on the letter)."""
    return e['reads']

def node_val(o, k):
    w, v, x, y = o
    return w[k[1]][k[2]] if k[0] == 'w' else {'v': v, 'x': x, 'y': y}[k[0]][k[1]]

def nodes(G):
    return [('w', i, j) for i in range(G.n_in) for j in range(G.n_out)] + [('v', j) for j in range(G.n_out)] + \
           [('x', i) for i in range(G.n_in)] + [('y', j) for j in range(G.n_out)]

def set_nodes(o, assign):
    """Writes assign = {node: value} into the state (returns a new state)."""
    w = [r[:] for r in o[0]]; v, x, y = list(o[1]), list(o[2]), list(o[3])
    for k, val in assign.items():
        if k[0] == 'w': w[k[1]][k[2]] = val
        elif k[0] == 'v': v[k[1]] = val
        elif k[0] == 'x': x[k[1]] = val
        else: y[k[1]] = val
    return (w, v, x, y)

def apply_events(G, o, evs):
    """Declared form of K_t: post-state = pre-state + the sum of all event components on the same node (Lemma R1)."""
    w, v, x, y = o
    w2 = [r[:] for r in w]; v2, x2, y2 = list(v), list(x), list(y)
    for e in evs.values():
        k = e['tgt']
        if k[0] == 'w': w2[k[1]][k[2]] += e['c']
        elif k[0] == 'v': v2[k[1]] += e['c']
        elif k[0] == 'x': x2[k[1]] += e['c']
        else: y2[k[1]] += e['c']
    return (w2, v2, x2, y2)

def ev_from_log(G, o, ev, t, plastic, bap, rho):
    """Generates the witness independently from the snn_core log (s_in, s_out, comp, teach) and the pre-beat state: occurrence is taken from the firings in the log and the protocol switches,
~~~
