~~~python
    'gate_class.py': 'b07c0327be0383f020de11e03c656e6967df12b972a4e7e97dd0af99885eb630',
    'r_layer_witness.py': '91af0c7f77c0f744ea5f723ba7c7f54cbe4652751d99af617addd0a979e3e1d2',
    'gate_criteria.py': '2429f282f4b83c21113f4f0d412cd8601e11963470aa920f1c9f1b002e61a364',
    'lp_exact.py': '23d6f167ffc1a2e7b49eae57d8d1ac991ace426ea79352740380bba3658c09b4',
    'decision.py': 'c35de9f447bd4bb880b754f537c7c394cac6fa4f108fca6ef98c27df8d528ae1',
}

def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(open(p, 'rb').read()).hexdigest()

def s0():
    print('S0  version binding and comparison with independent implementations')
    for f, h in APPX_B.items():
        got = sha(os.path.join(HERE, f))
        assert got == h, f
        print(f'  {f}  SHA-256 {got[:16]}…  matches Appendix B.1 of Chapter 11')
    import gate_class as GC
    S, h = GC.load_snn_core()
    G = GC.snn_gate(S)
    M = C.frozen_snn_member()
    NONE = ('none', (0, 0))
    FORM = [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE] * 6
    P = FORM + [NONE] * 8 + GC.WIN0()
    rng = random.Random(20261103)
    hists = [P]
    for _ in range(300):
        hists.append([(rng.choice(['A', 'B', 'AB', 'none']), (rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 2), F(1), F(2)]), rng.choice([F(0), F(1)])))
                      for _ in range(60)])
    beats = bad = clip = 0
    for hst in hists:
        st = S.init(); o = M.init(); og = G.init()
        for (inp, teach) in hst:
            st, ev = S.step(st, inp, teach)
            o, r = M.step(o, (inp, teach))
            og, rg = G.step(og, (inp, teach))
            beats += 1
            if not (st['w'] == o[0] == og[0] and st['v'] == o[1] == og[1] and st['x'] == o[2] == og[2] and st['y'] == o[3] == og[3]):
                bad += 1
            if tuple(ev['s_out']) != tuple(r['g']):
                bad += 1
            for (i, j), (pos, neg, rel, d) in ev['comp'].items():
                if pos != r['ev'].get(('PLUS', i, j), F(0)) or neg != r['ev'].get(('MINUS', i, j), F(0)):
                    bad += 1
                if ('CLIP', i, j) in r['ev']:
                    clip += 1
    print(f'  frozen snn_core.step (SHA-256 {h[:16]}…), Gate.step of Chapter 11 Appendix A.2 and Mem.step of this paper: {len(hists)} histories, {beats} beats, '
          f'mismatches in full state and PLUS/MINUS components {bad}; clipping in effect at {clip} places among them')
    # random members: Mem vs Gate
    bad2 = n2 = 0
    for _ in range(200):
        n_in, n_out = rng.choice([2, 3, 4]), rng.choice([1, 2])
        pats = {'none': (0,) * n_in}
        for kk in range(3):
            pats[f'p{kk}'] = tuple(rng.choice([0, 1]) for _ in range(n_in))
        q = lambda: F(rng.randint(0, 8), 8)
        b = [[F(rng.randint(0, 4), 8) for _ in range(n_out)] for _ in range(n_in)]
        lo = [[F(0)] * n_out for _ in range(n_in)]
        hi = [[F(2)] * n_out for _ in range(n_in)]
        args = dict(n_in=n_in, n_out=n_out, pat=pats, lam=[q() for _ in range(n_out)], theta=[F(1)] * n_out,
                    vreset=[F(0)] * n_out, dx=[q() for _ in range(n_in)], dy=[q() for _ in range(n_out)],
                    Ap=F(rng.randint(0, 4), 16), Am=F(rng.randint(0, 4), 16), rho=rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 10)]), b=b, lo=lo, hi=hi)
        M2 = C.Mem(**args); G2 = GC.Gate(**args)
        o, og = M2.init(), G2.init()
        for _ in range(40):
            sig = (rng.choice(list(pats)), tuple(rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 2), F(1)]) for _ in range(n_out)))
            o, r = M2.step(o, sig); og, rg = G2.step(og, sig)
            n2 += 1
            if o != og or tuple(r['g']) != tuple(rg['g']):
                bad2 += 1
    print(f'  200 random 𝒦_g members (ρ = 1/10 included), 40 beats each, {n2} beats in all: full-state mismatches between Mem and Gate {bad2}')
    print()
    return GC, S

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- S1 Instance P
NONE2 = ('none', (F(0), F(0)))
FORM_P = [('A', (F(1), F(0))), NONE2, NONE2] * 6
WIN0 = [('A', (F(0), F(0))), NONE2, NONE2, ('B', (F(0), F(0))), NONE2, NONE2] * 3
HIST_P = FORM_P + [NONE2] * 8 + WIN0

def show_C(items):
    for name, v, d in items:
        print(f'    [{"holds" if v else "fails"}] {name}' + (f'  {d}' if d else ''))

def s1():
    print('S1  Instance P (s1 frozen declared history; Chapter 11, 7.5 (1)): item-by-item check of C, regime check and witnesses')
    M = C.frozen_snn_member()
    j, A, n, mF = 0, [0, 1], 6, 2
    m = C.min_margin(M, j, A)
    ok, items, der = C.check_C(M, j, A, n, mF, m)
    print(f'  declared: j = output 0, A = {{entry 0, entry 1}}, T = {{w00, w10}}, n = {n}, m_F = {mF}, minimum rest margin of K3 m = {m}')
    show_C(items)
    print(f'  C (coefficient and initial-resource part): {"all hold" if ok else "fails"}; δ_F = {fr(der["delta_F"])}, ω = ' +
          ', '.join(f'w{i}0 ≥ {fr(v)}' for i, v in der['omega'].items()))
    W = (26, 43)
    rg = C.check_regime(M, j, A, HIST_P, W, n, mF, m)
    print(f'  regime: {"in 𝒟" if rg["ok"] else "not in 𝒟: " + "; ".join(rg["why"])}; pairing beats {rg["pairs"]}; clean probe beats in W = beats 26–43: {rg["probes"]}')
    V = C.verify(M, j, A, HIST_P, W, n, mF, m, pairs=rg['pairs'])
    print(f'  violations of invariants I1–I4: {len(V["inv"])}')
    print('  w at the end of the formation period: ' + ', '.join(f'w{i}0 = {fr(v)}' for i, v in V['w_after_form'].items()) +
          f' (lower bound ω = {fr(der["omega"][0])}; upper bound b + n·Δ̄_F = {fr(M.b[0][0] + n * der["Dbar_F"])})')
    rd = V['r_detail']
    print(f'  r: first effective write acting on T at beat {rd["t"]} ({rd["n_eff"]} of them); F3 = {rd["mode"]}, same-beat removal {rd["withdrawal"][0]}, I = {tuple(fr(c) for c in rd["withdrawal"][1])}: '
          f'no opening = {rd["noopen"]}, {len(rd["changed"])} events changed ⇒ M1₀, r = {V["r"]} (P4R = {rd["P4R"]}, an optional sufficient antecedent only, not used)')
    print('  q: z at window end = ' + ', '.join(f'w{k[0]}0 {fr(v)}' for k, v in V['z_end'].items()) +
          f'; formation-record beats {V["form"][(0, 0)][:1]}, {V["form"][(1, 0)][:1]}; q = {V["q"]}')
    pb = V['probe']
    print(f'  probe beat {pb["t"]}: actual vt = {fr(pb["vt"])}, firing {pb["g"]}; under the 𝒟₁ setting (u = {pb["t"]}, every position of T set to b = 1/4) vt = {fr(pb["vt_cf"])}, firing {pb["g_cf"]}')
    print('    PLUS components: ' + ', '.join(f'{k[0]}{k[1]}{k[2]} = {fr(c)}' for k, c in pb['plus_c'].items()) +
          f'; effective and changed by the setting: {len(pb["fd_plus"])}; gating read set contains a position of T (Path_act) = {pb["path"]}; SYN changed by the setting: {len(pb["syn_fd"])}; saturated positions {pb["sat"]}')
    print(f'  ⇒ Part witness = {V["part"]}, HDyn witness = {V["hdyn"]}, R(T) = q ∧ r ∧ Part ∧ HDyn = {V["R"]}')
    xs = der['x_sup']
    bound = M.b[0][0] + n * der['Dbar_F'] + M.Ap * xs
    print(f'  non-saturation condition of Corollary 14-1′: b + n·Δ̄_F + A₊·x_sup = {fr(bound)} ≤ hi = {fr(M.hi[0][0])}: {bound <= M.hi[0][0]}')
    print('  compare the readings of Chapter 11, 7.5 (1): z at window end = 0.607871, PLUS00@26 = 0.125070, Fld = 1 (algorithmic level).')
    print()

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- S2 exhaustive verification (frozen member)
def s2():
    print('S2  exhaustive regime verification on the frozen member: formation (the 6 pairings of P) + interlude 2 beats + rest 2 beats + probe + tail 3 beats;')
    print('    each interlude and tail beat ranges over {none, A, B, AB} × teaching current I₀ ∈ {0, 1} (I₁ = 0), 8^5 = 32768 histories in all; W = first through last interlude beat')
    M = C.frozen_snn_member()
    j, A, n, mF, m = 0, [0, 1], 6, 2, 2
    letters = [(nm, (F(i0), F(0))) for nm in ['none', 'A', 'B', 'AB'] for i0 in (0, 1)]
    form = FORM_P
    cnt = dict(runs=0, R=0, inv=0, sat=0, noC=0, multi=0)
    vmin = None
    for combo in itertools.product(letters, repeat=5):
        inter, tail = list(combo[:2]), list(combo[2:])
        hist = form + inter + [NONE2, NONE2, ('A', (F(0), F(0)))] + tail
        W = (len(form), len(hist) - 1)
        tp = len(form) + 4
        rg = C.check_regime(M, j, A, hist, W, n, mF, m)
        if not rg['ok'] or tp not in rg['probes']:
            cnt['noC'] += 1; continue
        V = C.verify(M, j, A, hist, W, n, mF, m, pairs=rg['pairs'], probe=tp)
        cnt['runs'] += 1
        cnt['R'] += V['R'] == 1
        cnt['inv'] += bool(V['inv'])
        cnt['sat'] += bool(V['probe']['sat'])
        cnt['multi'] += len(rg['probes']) > 1
        mz = min(V['z_end'].values())
        vmin = mz if vmin is None else min(vmin, mz)
    print(f'  trajectories in 𝒟: {cnt["runs"]} (failed the regime check: {cnt["noC"]}); R(T) witness holds: {cnt["R"]}; trajectories with invariant violations: {cnt["inv"]}; '
          f'T positions saturated at the probe beat: {cnt["sat"]}; more than one clean probe beat in W: {cnt["multi"]}; min z at window end = {fr(vmin)}')
    print()

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- S3 random members (satisfying C) and random trajectories
def rand_member(rng, need=True, eps=F(1, 100)):
    """Generate a member of 𝒦_LS; with need=True, adjust the parameters so that K1–K3 and F0 hold (F2 is met by the choice of n)."""
    while True:
        n_in = rng.choice([2, 3, 4]); n_out = rng.choice([1, 2])
        a_sz = rng.randint(1, n_in - 1) if n_in > 1 else 1
        A = sorted(rng.sample(range(n_in), a_sz))
        others = [i for i in range(n_in) if i not in A]
        pats = {'none': (0,) * n_in, 'pA': tuple(1 if i in A else 0 for i in range(n_in))}
        for kk in range(rng.randint(1, 3)):
            withA = rng.choice([0, 1])
            pats[f'q{kk}'] = tuple(withA if i in A else rng.choice([0, 1]) for i in range(n_in))
        th = F(rng.choice([1, 1, 2, 3]), rng.choice([1, 2]))
        lam = F(rng.randint(0, 7), 8)
        dy = F(rng.randint(0, 7), 8); dx = [F(rng.randint(0, 7), 8) for _ in range(n_in)]
        Am = F(rng.randint(0, 6), 32)
        Ap = max(Am / (1 - dy), eps) + F(rng.randint(1, 6), 64)
        m = rng.randint(0, 6)
        room = th - lam ** (m + 1) * th
        if room <= 0:
            continue
        b = [[F(0)] * n_out for _ in range(n_in)]
        for i in A:
            b[i][0] = room / len(A) * F(rng.randint(1, 8), 8)
        for i in others:
            b[i][0] = F(rng.randint(0, 8), 8) * th
        for jj in range(1, n_out):
            for i in range(n_in):
                b[i][jj] = F(rng.randint(0, 8), 8)
        hi = [[b[i][jj] + F(rng.randint(1, 16), 4) * th for jj in range(n_out)] for i in range(n_in)]
        lo = [[F(0)] * n_out for _ in range(n_in)]
        M = C.Mem(n_in, n_out, pats, [lam] * n_out, [th] * n_out, [0] * n_out, dx, [dy] * n_out, Ap, Am, 0, b, lo, hi, eps)
        mF = rng.randint(0, 4)
        ok0, items, der = C.check_C(M, 0, A, 1, mF, m)
        dF = der['delta_F']
        if dF is None or dF <= 0:
            continue
        n = 1
        while True:
            ok, items, der = C.check_C(M, 0, A, n, mF, m)
            if ok or n > 400:
                break
            n += 1
        if not ok:
            continue
        n += rng.randint(0, 3)
        return M, A, n, mF, m

def rand_traj(rng, M, j, A, n, mF, m, inter_len, tail_len, gap_extra=3):
    th = M.theta[j]; no = M.n_out
    zero = tuple(F(0) for _ in range(no))
    def quiet():
        return ('none', tuple(F(0) if jj == j else F(rng.randint(0, 2), 2) for jj in range(no)))
    withA = [nm for nm, p in M.pat.items() if all(p[i] for i in A)]
    hist = []
    for k in range(n):
        if k:
            hist += [quiet() for _ in range(mF + rng.randint(0, gap_extra))]
        else:
            hist += [quiet() for _ in range(rng.randint(0, 2))]
        hist.append((rng.choice(withA), tuple(th + F(rng.randint(0, 4), 4) if jj == j else F(rng.randint(0, 4), 4) for jj in range(no))))
    pn = len(hist) - 1
    def anylet():
        r = rng.random()
        I = tuple(rng.choice([F(0), F(0), th / 2, th, 2 * th]) for _ in range(no))
        return (rng.choice(list(M.pat)), I)
    hist += [anylet() for _ in range(inter_len)]
    hist += [quiet() for _ in range(m + rng.randint(0, 2))]
    tp = len(hist)
    hist.append(('pA', tuple(F(0) if jj == j else F(rng.randint(0, 2), 2) for jj in range(no))))
    hist += [anylet() for _ in range(tail_len)]
    ts = rng.randint(pn + 1, tp)
    return hist, (ts, len(hist) - 1), tp

def s3():
    print('S3  random 𝒦_LS members (satisfying C) × random trajectories (in 𝒟): dynamical invariants and duty witnesses')
    rng = random.Random(20261104)
    stats = dict(mem=0, traj=0, R=0, inv=0, sat=0, nsf=0, nin={}, asz={})
    for _ in range(300):
        M, A, n, mF, m = rand_member(rng)
        stats['mem'] += 1
        stats['nin'][M.n_in] = stats['nin'].get(M.n_in, 0) + 1
        stats['asz'][len(A)] = stats['asz'].get(len(A), 0) + 1
        for _ in range(5):
            hist, W, tp = rand_traj(rng, M, 0, A, n, mF, m, rng.randint(0, 40), rng.randint(0, 40))
            rg = C.check_regime(M, 0, A, hist, W, n, mF, m)
            assert rg['ok'] and tp in rg['probes'], rg['why']
            V = C.verify(M, 0, A, hist, W, n, mF, m, pairs=rg['pairs'], probe=tp)
            stats['traj'] += 1
            stats['R'] += V['R'] == 1
            stats['inv'] += bool(V['inv'])
            stats['sat'] += bool(V['probe']['sat'])
            stats['nsf'] += not V['probe']['ns_star']
            if V['R'] != 1 or V['inv']:
                print('  counterexample:', V['inv'][:3], V['q'], V['r'], V['part'], V['hdyn'])
    print(f'  members {stats["mem"]} (distribution of entry counts {dict(sorted(stats["nin"].items()))}, distribution of |A| {dict(sorted(stats["asz"].items()))}), '
          f'trajectories {stats["traj"]} (length up to {""}about 120 beats, with interludes and tails over arbitrary letters and I ∈ {{0, θ/2, θ, 2θ}})')
    print(f'  R(T) witness holds {stats["R"]} / {stats["traj"]}; invariant violations {stats["inv"]}; T positions clipped at the probe beat {stats["sat"]}, '
          f'of which every position of T already above hi − ε (NS* fails, E11 must be carried) {stats["nsf"]}')
    print()

def s3b():
    print('S3b  necessity of the second clause of K3: the weakened condition with only the first clause (λ^(m+1)θ + Σ_A b ≤ θ) loses strictness at λ = 0')
    # (1) replay the same generation as S3 (seed 20261104) with a K3 that has only the first clause, and classify the trajectories without a witness
    orig = C.check_C
    def weak_k3(M, j, A, n, mF, m, **kw):
        ok, items, der = orig(M, j, A, n, mF, m, **kw)
        items = [(nm, (C.k3_first_clause(M, j, A, m) if nm.startswith('K3') else v), d) for nm, v, d in items]
        return all(v for _, v, _ in items), items, der
    C.check_C = weak_k3
    try:
        rng = random.Random(20261104)
        tot = miss = cls = 0
        for _ in range(300):
            M, A, n, mF, m = rand_member(rng)
            for _ in range(5):
                hist, W, tp = rand_traj(rng, M, 0, A, n, mF, m, rng.randint(0, 40), rng.randint(0, 40))
                rg = C.check_regime(M, 0, A, hist, W, n, mF, m)
                V = C.verify(M, 0, A, hist, W, n, mF, m, pairs=rg['pairs'], probe=tp)
                tot += 1
                if V['R'] != 1:
                    miss += 1
                    sb = sum(M.b[i][0] for i in A)
                    cls += (M.lam[0] == 0 and sb == M.theta[0] and V['q'] == 1 and V['r'] == 1 and V['part'] == 1
                            and V['probe']['g_cf'] == 1)
    finally:
        C.check_C = orig
    print(f'  replaying the S3 generation with a K3 that has only the first clause: {tot} trajectories, {miss} without an R witness; of these, {cls} have “λ = 0 and Σ_A b = θ; q, r, Part hold; with T set to b at the probe beat the gate still opens”')
    # (2) minimal explicit example
    th = F(2)
    M = C.Mem(2, 1, {'none': (0, 0), 'pA': (0, 1), 'q0': (1, 0)}, [F(0)], [th], [0], [F(1, 2)] * 2, [F(1, 2)], F(3, 8), F(1, 16), 0,
              [[F(1, 2)], [th]], [[F(0)], [F(0)]], [[F(4)], [F(5)]])
    A, m, n, mF = [1], 0, 1, 1
    first = C.k3_first_clause(M, 0, A, m)
    ok, items, der = C.check_C(M, 0, A, n, mF, m)
    Q = ('none', (F(0),))
    hist = [('pA', (th,)), Q, Q, ('pA', (F(0),)), Q]
    W = (3, 4)
    rg = C.check_regime(M, 0, A, hist, W, n, mF, m)
    V = C.verify(M, 0, A, hist, W, n, mF, m, pairs=rg['pairs'], probe=3)
    pb = V['probe']
    print(f'  minimal explicit example: λ = 0, θ = 2, A = {{entry 1}}, b₁ = 2 = θ; K3(m=0) with only the first clause: {first}; full K3: {[v for nm, v, d in items if nm.startswith("K3")][0]}')
    print(f'    probe beat 3: actual vt = {fr(pb["vt"])}; with T set to b, vt = {fr(pb["vt_cf"])} ≥ θ, the gate still opens ⇒ no HDyn witness ({V["hdyn"]})')
    print('  Reason: the proof step vt_cf = λ·v_pre + Σ_A b < λ^(m+1)θ + Σ_A b uses the strictness of v_pre < λ^m θ; at λ = 0 the product is 0 and strictness is lost.')
    print('  Hence K3 takes two clauses, “λ^(m+1)θ + Σ_A b ≤ θ and Σ_A b < θ” (for λ > 0 the latter follows from the former); S3 generates with the full K3.')
    print()

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- S4 the 108-group grid of Chapter 11 and 200 random histories
def s4():
    print('S4  C check and witnesses on the 108-group grid of Chapter 11 (W0 × A₊ × teach × n; P-type histories); regime classification of the 200-history random input family')
    j, A, mF = 0, [0, 1], 2
    rows = []
    for W0, AP, teach, n in itertools.product([F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(3, 8), F(1, 2)], [F(1, 16), F(1, 8), F(1, 4)],
                                              [F(1, 2), F(1), F(2)], [1, 3, 6]):
        M = C.frozen_snn_member(W0=W0, Ap=AP)
        form = [('A', (teach, F(0))), NONE2, NONE2] * n
        hist = form + [NONE2] * 8 + WIN0
        W = (len(form) + 8, len(hist) - 1)
        m = C.min_margin(M, j, A)
        okC = m is not None and C.check_C(M, j, A, n, mF, m)[0]
        rg = C.check_regime(M, j, A, hist, W, n, mF, m if m is not None else 0)
        V = C.verify(M, j, A, hist, W, n, mF, m if m is not None else 0, pairs=rg['pairs'] if rg['pairs'] else None)
        rows.append((W0, AP, teach, n, okC and rg['ok'], V['R'], V['q'], V['hdyn']))
    inC = [r for r in rows if r[4]]
    print(f'  groups where C ∧ regime holds: {len(inC)}; R(T) witness holds in {sum(1 for r in inC if r[5] == 1)} of them')
    for r in inC:
        print(f'    W0 = {fr(r[0])}, A₊ = {fr(r[1])}, teach = {fr(r[2])}, n = {r[3]}: R witness = {r[5]}')
    outC = [r for r in rows if not r[4]]
    print(f'  groups where C or the regime fails: {len(outC)}; of these, this program still obtains an R witness in {sum(1 for r in outC if r[5] == 1)} (sufficient, not necessary) and none in {sum(1 for r in outC if r[5] != 1)} (no witness is not read as 0)')
    # the random input family of lp03_exact in Chapter 11 (same generation and seed)
    rng = random.Random(20261031)
    M = C.frozen_snn_member()
    noA = clean = Aonly = 0
    form = [('A', (F(1), F(0))), NONE2, NONE2] * 6
    for kk in range(200):
        win = [(rng.choice(['A', 'B', 'none', 'none']), (F(0), F(0))) for _ in range(18)]
        hist = form + [NONE2] * 8 + win
        W = (26, 43)
        rg = C.check_regime(M, j, A, hist, W, 6, mF, 2)
        hasA = any(s[0] == 'A' for s in win)
        noA += not hasA
        clean += rg['ok']
        Aonly += hasA and not rg['ok']
    print(f'  random input family, 200 histories (Chapter 11 lp03_exact, part two, seed 20261031): no letter A in W {noA}; with a clean probe beat (in 𝒟) {clean}; with A but no clean probe beat {Aonly}')
    print('    compare Appendix B.2 of Chapter 11, second item: Part = 0, HDyn = 0 in 4 histories; Part = 1, HDyn = 1 in the other 196.')
    print()

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- S5 condition-removal witnesses (one item of C fails and R(T) fails)
def run_one(M, A, hist, W, n, mF, m, title, j=0):
    rg = C.check_regime(M, j, A, hist, W, n, mF, m)
    V = C.verify(M, j, A, hist, W, n, mF, m, pairs=rg['pairs'] if len(rg['pairs']) == n else None)
    return rg, V

def erosion_hist(M, A, n, mF, single=None, K1=12, K2=12, cycles=200, teach_at_A=True):
    """Construction of Proposition 14-3: after formation, one clean probe beat, then repeat [rest K2 beats → firing driven by the teaching current alone for K1 beats → A (or a single-entry letter)]."""
    th = M.theta[0]
    Q = ('none', (F(0),) * M.n_out)
    hist = []
    for k in range(n):
        hist += [Q] * (mF if k else 0) + [('A', (th,) + (F(0),) * (M.n_out - 1))]
    hist += [Q] * 3 + [('A', (F(0),) * M.n_out)]
    ts = len(hist) - 1
    lt = single if single else 'A'
    for _ in range(cycles):
        hist += [Q] * K2 + [('none', (th,) + (F(0),) * (M.n_out - 1))] * K1
        hist += [(lt, ((th if teach_at_A else F(0)),) + (F(0),) * (M.n_out - 1))]
    return hist, ts

def s5():
    print('S5  condition-removal witnesses: break only one item of C, all others holding; show that R(T) fails on the declared T (the HDyn verdict is cross-checked separately by run_xcheck with the Chapter 11 adjudication program)')
    j, A, mF = 0, [0, 1], 2
    # D-K3: with rest margin m = 1, K3 fails; preload the membrane potential
    M = C.frozen_snn_member()
    m = 1
    ok, items, der = C.check_C(M, j, A, 6, mF, m)
    bad = [nm for nm, v, d in items if not v]
    hist = FORM_P + [NONE2] * 3 + [('none', (F(15, 16), F(0))), NONE2, ('A', (F(0), F(0))), NONE2, NONE2]
    W = (len(hist) - 3, len(hist) - 1)
    rg, V = run_one(M, A, hist, W, 6, mF, m, 'D-K3')
    pb = V['probe'] if 'probe' in V else None
    tp = len(hist) - 3
    o = V['pre'][tp]
    vt_b = M.lam[0] * o[1][0] + 2 * M.b[0][0]
    print(f'  D-K3 (m = 1): failing items {bad}; regime {rg["ok"]}; v before probe beat {tp} = {fr(o[1][0])}, with T set to b vt = {fr(vt_b)} ≥ θ: '
          f'the gate opens under every setting in 𝒟₁ (vt is monotone nondecreasing in σ); probe witness of this program: HDyn = {V["hdyn"]}, q = {V["q"]}, r = {V["r"]}, Part = {V["part"]}')
    # D-K3′: W0 = 1/2, Σ_A b = θ
    M = C.frozen_snn_member(W0=F(1, 2))
    mm = C.min_margin(M, j, A)
    print(f'  D-K3′ (W0 = 1/2, Σ_A b = θ): K3 fails for every m (min_margin = {mm}); on the P-type history, with T set to b at the probe beat vt ≥ 1; in the same group of the Chapter 11 grid HDyn is tier (b) 0 [B_FD] / ⊥_Over')
    # D-K2: A₋ = 1/12
    M = C.frozen_snn_member(Am=F(1, 12))
    m = C.min_margin(M, j, A)
    n = 1
    while not all(v for nm, v, d in C.check_C(M, j, A, n, mF, m)[1] if not nm.startswith('K2')):
        n += 1
    ok, items, der = C.check_C(M, j, A, n, mF, m)
    bad = [nm for nm, v, d in items if not v]
    hist, ts = erosion_hist(M, A, n, mF)
    pre, recs = M.run(hist)
    zs = [min(o[0][i][0] - M.b[i][0] for i in A) for o in pre]
    te = next(t for t in range(ts + 1, len(hist)) if zs[t + 1] <= M.eps)
    rg, V = run_one(M, A, hist[:te + 1], (ts, te), n, mF, m, 'D-K2')
    print(f'  D-K2 (A₋ = 1/12, A₋/(1−d_y) = 1/6 > A₊ = 1/8; n = {n}): failing items {bad}; regime {rg["ok"]} (W = beats {ts}–{te}); '
          f'Σ_A w after formation = {fr(sum(pre[ts][0][i][0] for i in A))}; z at window end = {", ".join(fr(v) for v in V["z_end"].values())} ⇒ q = {V["q"]} (R(T) fails: the T positions are not retained at window end)')
    # D-F2: n = 3
    M = C.frozen_snn_member()
    m = 2
    ok, items, der = C.check_C(M, j, A, 3, mF, m)
    bad = [nm for nm, v, d in items if not v]
    form3 = [('A', (F(1), F(0))), NONE2, NONE2] * 3
    hist = form3 + [NONE2] * 8 + [('A', (F(0), F(0)))] + [NONE2] * 5
    rg, V = run_one(M, A, hist, (len(form3) + 8, len(hist) - 1), 3, mF, m, 'D-F2')
    pb = V.get('probe')
    print(f'  D-F2 (n = 3; W = one clean probe beat + rest 5 beats): failing items {bad}; regime {rg["ok"]}; Σ_A w at the end of the formation period = {fr(sum(V["pre"][len(form3)][0][i][0] for i in A))} < θ; '
          f'T-position events in W: ' + summarize_T_events(V, A, (len(form3) + 8, len(hist) - 1), M.eps) + f'; HDyn witness = {V["hdyn"]}')
    # D-S3: ρ = 1/10, long rest tail
    M = C.frozen_snn_member(rho=F(1, 10))
    hist = FORM_P + [NONE2] * 8 + [('A', (F(0), F(0)))] + [NONE2] * 60
    W = (26, len(hist) - 1)
    ok, items, der = C.check_C(M, j, A, 6, mF, 2)
    bad = [nm for nm, v, d in items if not v]
    rg, V = run_one(M, A, hist, W, 6, mF, 2, 'D-S3')
    print(f'  D-S3 (ρ = 1/10, rest 60 beats after the probe): failing items {bad}; regime {rg["ok"]}; z at window end = {", ".join(fr(v) for v in V["z_end"].values())} ⇒ q = {V["q"]}'
          f' (also: RELAX reads the Trace-Response of its own position, so a relaxation component inside W suffices to make HDyn adjudicate 1. This is the shallowing route that C excludes; see main text 6.8)')
    # D-S4: the alphabet contains the single-entry letter A0 = (1,0,0,0)
    pat = {'A': (1, 1, 0, 0), 'B': (0, 0, 1, 1), 'AB': (1, 1, 1, 1), 'A0': (1, 0, 0, 0), 'none': (0, 0, 0, 0)}
    full = lambda q: [[q] * 2 for _ in range(4)]
    M = C.Mem(4, 2, pat, [F(3, 4)] * 2, [1, 1], [0, 0], [F(1, 2)] * 4, [F(1, 2)] * 2, F(1, 8), F(1, 16), 0, full(F(1, 4)), full(F(0)), full(F(2)))
    ok, items, der = C.check_C(M, j, A, 6, mF, 2)
    bad = [nm for nm, v, d in items if not v]
    hist, ts = erosion_hist(M, A, 6, mF, single='A0', teach_at_A=False, cycles=60)
    pre, recs = M.run(hist)
    zs = [pre[t][0][0][0] - M.b[0][0] for t in range(len(pre))]
    te = next((t for t in range(ts + 1, len(hist)) if zs[t + 1] <= M.eps), len(hist) - 1)
    rg, V = run_one(M, A, hist[:te + 1], (ts, te), 6, mF, 2, 'D-S4')
    print(f'  D-S4 (alphabet contains the single-entry letter A0): failing items {bad}; z at window end = {", ".join(fr(v) for v in V["z_end"].values())} ⇒ q = {V["q"]} (w00 is eroded by the depression component on the single-entry letter)')
    # D-W: no clean probe beat in W (all rest)
    M = C.frozen_snn_member()
    hist = FORM_P + [NONE2] * 8 + [NONE2] * 6
    rg, V = run_one(M, A, hist, (26, 31), 6, mF, 2, 'D-W')
    print(f'  D-W (W all rest): regime {rg["ok"]} ({"; ".join(rg["why"])}); events acting on T in W: {count_T_events(V, A, (26, 31))} ⇒ HDyn has no bearer (a = 0), Part has no conditional process inside B')
    print()

def count_T_events(V, A, W):
    return sum(1 for t in range(W[0], W[1] + 1) for key in V['recs'][t]['ev'] if key[0] in ('PLUS', 'MINUS', 'RELAX', 'CLIP') and key[1] in A and key[2] == 0)

def summarize_T_events(V, A, W, eps):
    tot = eff = 0
    for t in range(W[0], W[1] + 1):
        for key, c in V['recs'][t]['ev'].items():
            if key[0] in ('PLUS', 'MINUS', 'RELAX', 'CLIP') and key[1] in A and key[2] == 0:
                tot += 1; eff += abs(c) > eps
    return f'{tot}, of which effective (|c| > ε) {eff}'

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- S6 sufficient but not necessary (R holds while some item of C fails)
def s6():
    print('S6  witnesses that C is sufficient but not necessary (same family, same regime class, same declared level and resolution): some item of C fails while the R(T) witness holds')
    j, A, mF = 0, [0, 1], 2
    cases = [
        ('teach 1/2 < θ (F1 requires I ≥ θ on pairing beats; fails)', C.frozen_snn_member(), F(1, 2), 6),
        ('n = 4 (F2a fails: δ_F is a conservative lower bound; the actual increment per pairing is ≥ 1/16)', C.frozen_snn_member(), F(1), 4),
    ]
    for title, M, teach, n in cases:
        form = [('A', (teach, F(0))), NONE2, NONE2] * n
        hist = form + [NONE2] * 8 + WIN0
        W = (len(form) + 8, len(hist) - 1)
        m = C.min_margin(M, j, A)
        ok, items, der = C.check_C(M, j, A, n, mF, m)
        rg = C.check_regime(M, j, A, hist, W, n, mF, m)
        bad = [nm for nm, v, d in items if not v] + rg['why']
        pairs = [3 * k for k in range(n)]
        V = C.verify(M, j, A, hist, W, n, mF, m, pairs=pairs)
        print(f'  {title}: failing items {bad}; R(T) witness = {V["R"]} (q = {V["q"]}, r = {V["r"]}, Part = {V["part"]}, HDyn = {V["hdyn"]})')
    M = C.frozen_snn_member()
    form3 = [('A', (F(1), F(0))), NONE2, NONE2] * 3
    hist = form3 + [NONE2] * 8 + WIN0
    W = (len(form3) + 8, len(hist) - 1)
    ok, items, der = C.check_C(M, j, A, 3, mF, 2)
    bad = [nm for nm, v, d in items if not v]
    V = C.verify(M, j, A, hist, W, 3, mF, 2, pairs=[0, 3, 6])
    fired = [t for t in range(W[0], W[1] + 1) if V['recs'][t]['g'][0] == 1]
    pb = V['probe']
    print(f'  n = 3 on the P-type window (A, rest, rest, B, rest, rest) × 3: failing items {bad}; the first probe does not fire; beats {fired} fire: the membrane potential left by the preceding non-firing B beat and the Trace-Response together cross threshold, '
          f'and with T set to b vt = {fr(pb["vt_cf"])} < θ; R(T) witness = {V["R"]} (the same member as S5 D-F2: on the single-probe window R fails)')
    M = C.frozen_snn_member(Am=F(1, 12))
    m = C.min_margin(M, j, A); n = 9
    form = [('A', (F(1), F(0))), NONE2, NONE2] * n
    hist = form + [NONE2] * 8 + WIN0
    W = (len(form) + 8, len(hist) - 1)
    ok, items, der = C.check_C(M, j, A, n, mF, m)
    bad = [nm for nm, v, d in items if not v]
    V = C.verify(M, j, A, hist, W, n, mF, m, pairs=[3 * k for k in range(n)])
    print(f'  A₋ = 1/12 (K2 fails; the same member as S5 D-K2) on the P-type window: failing items {bad}; R(T) witness = {V["R"]}. The same member fails R on another legitimate trajectory (S5), so K2 is necessary for the universal claim over the regime class, not for an individual trajectory')
    print()

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- S7 linear families (Family A, Family E)
def s7():
    print('S7  Family 1 (Family A parameters) and Family 2 (Family E parameters): the contrast Lemma 14-L on the linear F family M(a, b, k, γ, ε) (a direct generalization, registered at lemma level)')
    a, b, k, g, eps = F(2), F(1, 10), F(1, 10), F(1, 10), F(1, 1000)
    for phys, label in (('A', 'Family A (constant-head flow network, non-neural)'), ('E', 'Family E (firing-rate Hebb, neural)')):
        L = Lin(a, b, k, g, eps, phys=phys)
        tot = Rn = leg = same = allrest = allrest_R = 0
        predicted = bad = 0
        for Ln in range(1, 11):
            zall = a
            for _ in range(Ln):
                zall = zall * (1 + k - g) + b
            for seq in itertools.product(['drive', 'rest'], repeat=Ln):
                hist = ['seed'] + list(seq)
                for ts in range(1, Ln + 1):
                    for te in (Ln,):
                        W = (ts, te)
                        res = L.run(hist, W)
                        tot += 1
                        cond = (a > eps and a * (1 - g) ** te > eps and g * a * (1 - g) ** (ts - 1) > eps
                                and (zall <= L.zmax if phys == 'A' else zall <= L.g1 * L.R0 - 0 and 1 + zall <= L.ymax))
                        predicted += cond
                        if cond and not (res['R'] and res['legal']):
                            bad += 1
                        Rn += res['R']; leg += res['legal']; same += res['same']
                        if all(s == 'rest' for s in seq[ts - 1:te]):
                            allrest += 1; allrest_R += res['R']
        print(f'  {label}: histories “seed + {{drive, rest}}^L”, L = 1–10, windows [ts, L] (ts = 1–L), {tot} cases in all; C_lin holds in {predicted}, of which R ∧ validity fails in {bad}; '
              f'validity {leg}; F-layer and mechanism-layer trajectories identical beat by beat {same}; of the {allrest} cases with W all rest, R = 1 in {allrest_R} (borne by the relaxation component)')
    # condition removal
    L = Lin(a, b, k, g, eps, phys='E', R0=10)
    r = L.run(['seed', 'drive'], (1, 1))
    print(f'  Family E with initial receptor pool 10: formal R = {r["R"]}, physically valid = {r["legal"]} (receptor pool {fr(r["pool"])} < 0; no construction certificate is issued; this is a failure of the physical-validity item, not R failing on a valid instance)')
    L = Lin(F(1, 2000), b, k, g, eps)
    r = L.run(['seed', 'drive', 'rest'], (1, 2))
    print(f'  a = 1/2000 ≤ ε: r = {r["r"]}, R = {r["R"]}')
    L = Lin(a, b, k, g, eps)
    r = L.run(['seed'] + ['rest'] * 80 + ['drive'], (81, 81))
    zpre = a * (1 - g) ** 80
    print(f'  rest 80 beats after formation, then drive one beat (W = the drive beat): F-layer R = {r["R"]}, resolution-compatible = {r["compat"]}, F layer identical to mechanism layer = {r["same"]}; '
          f'at the mechanism layer z₁ before the drive beat = 2·(9/10)^80 = {fr(zpre)} ≤ ε, so z₁ is not an available position, and neither the write nor the relaxation has HistCond at the mechanism layer (the sub-resolution retention frozen in the F layer makes it misread as available; '
          f'Chapter 6, Propositions 2 and 3: m > 50 is incompatible, the F reading is not read back); “γ·a(1−γ)^(ts−1) > ε” of C_lin fails')
    L = Lin(a, b, k, F(0), eps)
    r1 = L.run(['seed', 'rest', 'rest'], (1, 2)); r2 = L.run(['seed', 'rest', 'drive'], (1, 2))
    print(f'  γ = 0: with W all rest HDyn = {r1["hdyn"]}; with drive in W (k ≠ 0) HDyn = {r2["hdyn"]}; that is, the beat-by-beat generalization of the component condition of Proposition 1 to the regime class')
    print()

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- S8 tightness constructions (Propositions 14-2, 14-3) on random members
def s8():
    print('S8  tightness constructions on random members: the preload construction when K3 fails (others holding); the erosion construction when K2 fails (others holding)')
    rng = random.Random(20261105)
    k3n = k3ok = k3clip = 0
    k2n = k2ok = 0
    tries = 0
    while (k3n < 60 or k2n < 30) and tries < 20000:
        tries += 1
        M, A, n, mF, m = rand_member(rng)
        j = 0; th = M.theta[0]; lam = M.lam[0]
        if k3n < 60 and m >= 1 and lam > 0:
            mm = m - 1      # with m − 1 as the declared margin, K3 fails
            sb = sum(M.b[i][0] for i in A)
            vstar = (th - sb) / lam ** (mm + 1)
            if vstar < th:
                c = (vstar + th) / 2
                Q = ('none', (F(0),) * M.n_out)
                hist = []
                for kk in range(n):
                    hist += [Q] * (mF if kk else 0) + [('pA', (th,) + (F(0),) * (M.n_out - 1))]
                hist += [Q] * 2 + [('none', (c,) + (F(0),) * (M.n_out - 1))] + [Q] * mm + [('pA', (F(0),) * M.n_out)] + [Q] * 2
                tp = len(hist) - 3
                pre, recs = M.run(hist)
                o = pre[tp]
                vt_b = lam * o[1][0] + sb
                k3n += 1
                noclip = not any(('CLIP', i, 0) in recs[tp]['ev'] for i in A)
                k3clip += not noclip
                k3ok += vt_b >= th and recs[tp]['g'][0] == 1 and noclip
        if k2n < 30 and M.dy[0] > 0 and min(M.dx[i] for i in A) < 1:
            # raise A₋ until K2 just fails, then reselect n by F2
            ysup = 1 / (1 - M.dy[0])
            Am2 = M.Ap / ysup * F(5, 4)
            M2 = C.Mem(M.n_in, M.n_out, M.pat, M.lam, M.theta, M.vreset, M.dx, M.dy, M.Ap, Am2, 0, M.b, M.lo, M.hi, M.eps)
            dF = C.check_C(M2, 0, A, 1, mF, m)[2]['delta_F']
            if dF is None or dF <= 0:
                continue
            n2 = 1
            while not all(v for nm, v, d in C.check_C(M2, 0, A, n2, mF, m)[1] if not nm.startswith('K2')) and n2 < 2000:
                n2 += 1
            if n2 >= 2000:
                continue
            th = M2.theta[0]
            Q = ('none', (F(0),) * M2.n_out)
            hist = []
            for kk in range(n2):
                hist += [Q] * (mF if kk else 0) + [('pA', (th,) + (F(0),) * (M2.n_out - 1))]
            hist += [Q] * (m + 1) + [('pA', (F(0),) * M2.n_out)]
            ts = len(hist) - 1
            o, _ = M2.run(hist)
            o = o[-1]
            done = False
            for cyc in range(3000):
                for sig in [Q] * 30 + [('none', (th,) + (F(0),) * (M2.n_out - 1))] * 30 + [('pA', (th,) + (F(0),) * (M2.n_out - 1))]:
                    o, _ = M2.step(o, sig)
                if all(o[0][i][0] - M2.b[i][0] <= M2.eps for i in A):
                    done = True; break
            k2n += 1
            k2ok += done
    print(f'  K3 preload construction: {k3n} members (declared margin = the minimum margin of each member minus 1); T positions not clipped at the probe beat (antecedent of Proposition 14-2) {k3n - k3clip}, of which the gate still opens with T set to b in {k3ok}; '
          f'T positions clipped at the probe beat {k3clip}, outside the antecedent of Proposition 14-2 (a clipping event reads w; treated separately, see main text 6.4)')
    print(f'  K2 erosion construction: {k2n} members (A₋ = 1.25·A₊(1 − d_y)); every position of T falls to z ≤ ε within at most 3000 cycles in {k2ok}')
    print()

from t14_core import Lin
if __name__ == '__main__':
    print('The Necessity Form of Field-Concretization Qualification: main verification (run_main.py)')
    print()
    s0()
    s1()
    s2()
    s3()
    s3b()
    s4()
    s5()
    s6()
    s7()
    s8()
    print('End.')
~~~

A.11  run\_xcheck.py  Run entry: instance-by-instance cross-check with the adjudication program of Chapter 11 (X1–X6, parts a–d)

~~~python
"""Appendix program of The Necessity Form of Field-Concretization Qualification: uses the adjudication programs of Appendix A of Chapter 11 v1.0 (Theorems D1, D2; same directory; checksums in Appendix B.1 of that paper)
to cross-check, instance by instance, the predictions of Theorem 14-1⁺ of this paper and the condition-removal witnesses (X1–X6). This program does not modify the Chapter 11 programs; it only imports and calls them.
Run (in this directory, in parts; the parts are independent, the output contains no timings and is byte-for-byte reproducible):
  python3 run_xcheck.py a   (X1, X4, X5)
  python3 run_xcheck.py b   (X2)
  python3 run_xcheck.py c   (X3)
  python3 run_xcheck.py d   (X6)
  Without an argument, all six sections run in order (the title line notes “part all”).
Criteria: the Chapter 11 verdicts Part and HDyn are taken in two columns, “B_FD[𝒟₁] declared” and “not declared”; a verdict of 1 does not consume B_FD (Chapter 11, 7.2).
"""
import sys, os, hashlib, random, itertools
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
sys.path.insert(0, HERE)
from fractions import Fraction as F
import t14_core as C
from t14_core import fr
import run_main as RM
import gate_class as GC
import gate_criteria as GK
import decision as DC
for f, h in RM.APPX_B.items():
    assert hashlib.sha256(open(os.path.join(HERE, f), 'rb').read()).hexdigest() == h, f
NONE = ('none', (F(0), F(0)))

def gate_of(M):
    return GC.Gate(M.n_in, M.n_out, M.pat, M.lam, M.theta, M.vreset, M.dx, M.dy, M.Ap, M.Am, M.rho, M.b, M.lo, M.hi)

def d2(M, hist, t0, T):
    assert M.eps == GK.EPS
    G = gate_of(M)
    I = GK.Instance('', G, hist, t0, T=[('w', i, j) for (i, j) in T])
    D = DC.Decider(I)
    j1, j0 = DC.judge(I, D, True), DC.judge(I, D, False)
    return dict(P1=j1['Part'], H1=j1['HDyn'], P0=j0['Part'], H0=j0['HDyn'], comp=all(D.complete_u.values()), vf=D.verify_fail,
                tiers=sorted({r['tier'] for r in j1['rows'] if r['e']['tgt'] in I.T and r['a'] == 1}))

def line(tag, r):
    return (f'{tag}{": " if tag else ""}B_FD declared → Part = {r["P1"]}, HDyn = {r["H1"]}; not declared → Part = {r["P0"]}, HDyn = {r["H0"]}; '
            f'exhausted {r["comp"]}, witness re-run failures {r["vf"]}; η tiers of effective events on T {r["tiers"]}')

T = [(0, 0), (1, 0)]
M = C.frozen_snn_member()
def x1():
    # X1
    print('X1  Instance P (s1 frozen declared history, W = beats 26–43)')
    r = d2(M, RM.HIST_P, 26, T)
    print('  ' + line('P', r))
    print()
def x2():
    # X2
    print('X2  Frozen member, 24 random trajectories in 𝒟 (formation = the 6 pairings of P; interlude of 0–3 beats and tail of 0–3 beats drawn from {none, A, B, AB} × I₀ ∈ {0, 1}; 2 rest beats before the clean probe)')
    rng = random.Random(20261106)
    letters = [(nm, (F(i0), F(0))) for nm in ['none', 'A', 'B', 'AB'] for i0 in (0, 1)]
    agree = tot = 0
    for kk in range(24):
        inter = [rng.choice(letters) for _ in range(rng.randint(0, 3))]
        tail = [rng.choice(letters) for _ in range(rng.randint(0, 3))]
        hist = RM.FORM_P + inter + [NONE, NONE, ('A', (F(0), F(0)))] + tail
        t0 = len(RM.FORM_P)
        rg = C.check_regime(M, 0, [0, 1], hist, (t0, len(hist) - 1), 6, 2, 2)
        V = C.verify(M, 0, [0, 1], hist, (t0, len(hist) - 1), 6, 2, 2, pairs=rg['pairs'])
        r = d2(M, hist, t0, T)
        tot += 1
        ok = rg['ok'] and V['R'] == 1 and r['P1'] == r['P0'] == 1 and r['H1'] == r['H0'] == 1 and r['comp'] and r['vf'] == 0
        agree += ok
        if not ok:
            print('  disagreement:', [s[0] + str(s[1][0]) for s in inter + tail], line('', r))
    print(f'  of {tot} trajectories in 𝒟 for which this paper predicts R(T) = 1, Chapter 11 D2 gives Part = 1, HDyn = 1 in both columns (exhausted, no witness re-run failures) for {agree}')
    print()
def x3():
    # X3
    print('X3  Groups of the 108-group Chapter 11 grid on which C ∧ regime holds (run_main S4): Chapter 11 D2 verdicts')
    n_ok = n_tot = 0
    for W0, AP, teach, n in itertools.product([F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(3, 8), F(1, 2)], [F(1, 16), F(1, 8), F(1, 4)],
                                              [F(1, 2), F(1), F(2)], [1, 3, 6]):
        Mg = C.frozen_snn_member(W0=W0, Ap=AP)
        form = [('A', (teach, F(0))), NONE, NONE] * n
        hist = form + [NONE] * 8 + RM.WIN0
        W = (len(form) + 8, len(hist) - 1)
        m = C.min_margin(Mg, 0, [0, 1])
        if m is None or not C.check_C(Mg, 0, [0, 1], n, 2, m)[0] or not C.check_regime(Mg, 0, [0, 1], hist, W, n, 2, m)['ok']:
            continue
        r = d2(Mg, hist, W[0], T)
        n_tot += 1
        ok = r['P1'] == r['P0'] == 1 and r['H1'] == r['H0'] == 1
        n_ok += ok
        print(f'  W0 = {fr(W0)}, A₊ = {fr(AP)}, teach = {fr(teach)}, n = {n}: ' + line('', r))
    print(f'  total: of {n_tot} groups, {n_ok} receive Part = 1, HDyn = 1 in both D2 columns')
    print()
def x4():
    # X4
    print('X4  Chapter 11 verdicts on the condition-removal witnesses')
    hist = RM.FORM_P + [NONE] * 3 + [('none', (F(15, 16), F(0))), NONE, ('A', (F(0), F(0))), NONE, NONE]
    r = d2(M, hist, len(hist) - 3, T)
    print('  ' + line('D-K3 (m = 1, preloaded membrane potential; W = 3 beats from the probe beat)', r))
    Mh = C.frozen_snn_member(W0=F(1, 2))
    r = d2(Mh, RM.HIST_P, 26, T)
    print('  ' + line('D-K3′ (W0 = 1/2, P-type history)', r))
    form3 = [('A', (F(1), F(0))), NONE, NONE] * 3
    hist = form3 + [NONE] * 8 + [('A', (F(0), F(0)))] + [NONE] * 5
    r = d2(M, hist, len(form3) + 8, T)
    print('  ' + line('D-F2 (n = 3, single-probe window)', r))
    hist = RM.FORM_P + [NONE] * 8 + [NONE] * 6
    r = d2(M, hist, 26, T)
    print('  ' + line('D-W (W all rest)', r))
    print('  Note: a tier-(b) 0 carries B_FD[𝒟₁] and is ⊥_Over when B_FD is not declared (Chapter 11, 4.2, 7.2); a = 0 or a tier-(a) 0 does not consume B_FD.')
    print()
def x5():
    # X5
    print('X5  Necessity of the second clause of K3 (minimal explicit example of run_main S3b: λ = 0, Σ_A b = θ; K3 with only the first clause holds while the full K3 fails)')
    th = F(2)
    Ml = C.Mem(2, 1, {'none': (0, 0), 'pA': (0, 1), 'q0': (1, 0)}, [F(0)], [th], [0], [F(1, 2)] * 2, [F(1, 2)], F(3, 8), F(1, 16), 0,
               [[F(1, 2)], [th]], [[F(0)], [F(0)]], [[F(4)], [F(5)]])
    Q = ('none', (F(0),))
    hist = [('pA', (th,)), Q, Q, ('pA', (F(0),)), Q]
    r = d2(Ml, hist, 3, [(1, 0)])
    print('  ' + line('λ = 0 example (W = beats 3–4)', r))
    print('  Reading: if K3 with only the first clause replaced the full K3, the theorem would predict R = 1 on this member and trajectory, whereas D2 with B_FD declared gives HDyn = 0—the weakened condition is breached (carrying B_FD); the full K3 excludes this member.')
    print()
def x6():
    # X6
    print('X6  Random 𝒦_LS members (satisfying the full C, ε = 1/100, n ≤ 6) and short trajectories in 𝒟: Chapter 11 D2 verdicts')
    rng = random.Random(20261107)
    got = cnt = ns_t = ns_f = ind_f = 0
    while cnt < 10:
        Mr, A, n, mF, m = RM.rand_member(rng)
        if n > 6 or mF > 2 or m > 3 or Mr.n_in * Mr.n_out > 6:
            continue
        hist, W, tp = RM.rand_traj(rng, Mr, 0, A, n, mF, m, rng.randint(0, 3), rng.randint(0, 2), gap_extra=0)
        rg = C.check_regime(Mr, 0, A, hist, (W[0], len(hist) - 1), n, mF, m)
        if not rg['ok']:
            continue
        V = C.verify(Mr, 0, A, hist, (W[0], len(hist) - 1), n, mF, m, pairs=rg['pairs'])
        r = d2(Mr, hist, W[0], [(i, 0) for i in A])
        cnt += 1
        sep_ok = r['H1'] == 1 and r['P1'] == 1
        got += sep_ok
        if V['probe']['ns_star']:
            ns_t += 1; got_ns = sep_ok
        else:
            ns_f += 1; ind_f += r['H1'] == '⊥_Ind' and r['H0'] == '⊥_Ind'
        print(f'  member {cnt}: entries {Mr.n_in}, outputs {Mr.n_out}, |A| = {len(A)}, n = {n}, history length {len(hist)}; witness of this paper R = {V["R"]}, NS* = {V["probe"]["ns_star"]}; ' + line('D2', r))
    print(f'  total: of {cnt} members, D2 gives Part = 1, HDyn = 1 (B_FD-declared column) for {got}; NS* holds for {ns_t} (prediction: verdict 1 without consuming E11); '
          f'NS* fails for {ns_f}, of which D2 gives HDyn = ⊥_Ind in both columns for {ind_f} (prediction: the two clipping readings disagree, and 1 is obtained only with E11 declared; ⊥ is not read as 0)')
    print()
PARTS = {'a': (x1, x4, x5), 'b': (x2,), 'c': (x3,), 'd': (x6,)}
if __name__ == '__main__':
    part = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 'all'
    print(f'The Necessity Form of Field-Concretization Qualification: cross-check (run_xcheck.py, part {part}): Chapter 11 Theorem D2 verdicts vs the predictions of Theorem 14-1⁺ of this paper')
    print()
    for fn in (PARTS[part] if part != 'all' else (x1, x2, x3, x4, x5, x6)):
        fn()
    print('End.')
~~~

A.12  run\_source.py  Run entry: sources and windows without a clean probe (V1–V4)

~~~python
"""Appendix program of The Necessity Form of Field-Concretization Qualification: verification of sources and of windows without a clean probe (V1–V4). Standard library only, exact rational arithmetic; fixed seeds, deterministic output.
Run (in this directory): python3 run_source.py
V1  One beat (none, I = (0, 1)) prepended to the formative history: P4R fails for the whole network, and r is certified by the source proof (the same-beat removal selected by F3, checking M1₀ directly).
V2  Random members × arbitrary input prefixes to the other units (baselines of the other columns may be negative, pairing letters may contain entries outside A): α/β coverage of F3, the relation between M1₀ witnesses and P4R; an explicit example where F3 fails.
V3  Two consecutive A beats, with W taking only the second: no clean probe in W, yet events on T; the Chapter 11 D2 verdicts serve as a comparison, and no negative verdict is drawn.
V4  A concrete example without events: beat-by-beat check of S5 D-W (W all rest).
"""
import sys, os, random, hashlib
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
sys.path.insert(0, HERE)
from fractions import Fraction as F
import t14_core as C
from t14_core import fr
import run_main as RM
for f, h in RM.APPX_B.items():
    assert hashlib.sha256(open(os.path.join(HERE, f), 'rb').read()).hexdigest() == h, f
Q = ('none', (F(0), F(0)))
FORM = [('A', (F(1), F(0))), Q, Q] * 6

def tev(V, A, t, j=0):
    return [(k, c) for k, c in V['recs'][t]['ev'].items() if k[0] in ('PLUS', 'MINUS', 'RELAX', 'CLIP') and k[1] in A and k[2] == j]

def v1():
    print('V1  Prefix in which another unit is injected first: one beat (none, I = (0, 1)) before the formative history, then 6 pairings, 3 rest beats and one probe; W is the single probe beat')
    M = C.frozen_snn_member(); A = [0, 1]
    h = [('none', (F(0), F(1)))] + FORM + [Q] * 3 + [('A', (F(0), F(0)))]
    W = (len(h) - 1, len(h) - 1)
    okC = C.check_C(M, 0, A, 6, 2, 2)[0]
    rg = C.check_regime(M, 0, A, h, W, 6, 2, 2)
    V = C.verify(M, 0, A, h, W, 6, 2, 2, pairs=rg['pairs'])
    rd = V['r_detail']
    o = V['pre'][rd['t']]
    print(f'  C = {okC}; regime = {rg["ok"]} (pairing beats {rg["pairs"]}, F3 = {rg["f3"]}); first effective write on T at beat {rd["t"]}; pre-beat state at that beat y = {tuple(fr(q) for q in o[3])}, v = {tuple(fr(q) for q in o[1])}, P4R = {rd["P4R"]}')
    print(f'  target column in the pre-beat state: w_i0 = b ({all(o[0][i][0] == M.b[i][0] for i in range(4))}), x = 0 ({all(q == 0 for q in o[2])}), v_0 = y_0 = 0 ({o[1][0] == 0 and o[3][0] == 0})—the conclusion of Lemma 14.3′, independent of the other units')
    print(f'  F3 = {rd["mode"]}: same-beat removal {rd["withdrawal"][0]}, I = {tuple(fr(c) for c in rd["withdrawal"][1])} (only I_0 removed; the injection into output 1 is kept); no opening = {rd["noopen"]}; '
          f'changed effective writes on T {len(rd["changed"])}/{rd["n_eff"]} ⇒ M1₀ ⇒ (Theorem COR(1)) r = {V["r"]}')
    print(f'  q = {V["q"]}, Part = {V["part"]}, HDyn = {V["hdyn"]} ⇒ R(T) witness = {V["R"]}. The source uses only M1₀ under the F3 removal, not P4R.')
    print()

def v2():
    print('V2  Random members (satisfying C) × arbitrary input prefixes to the other units: F3 coverage, M1₀ witnesses and P4R')
    rng = random.Random(20261108)
    cnt = dict(traj=0, a=0, b=0, none=0, r=0, r_f3=0, p4r_false=0, p4r_false_r=0, R=0)
    for _ in range(250):
        M0, A, n, mF, m = RM.rand_member(rng)
        b = [row[:] for row in M0.b]; lo = [row[:] for row in M0.lo]; hi = [row[:] for row in M0.hi]
        for i in range(M0.n_in):
            for k in range(1, M0.n_out):
                lo[i][k] = F(-2); b[i][k] = F(rng.randint(-8, 8), 8); hi[i][k] = F(2)
        M = C.Mem(M0.n_in, M0.n_out, M0.pat, M0.lam, M0.theta, M0.vreset, M0.dx, M0.dy, M0.Ap, M0.Am, 0, b, lo, hi, M0.eps)
        assert C.check_C(M, 0, A, n, mF, m)[0]
        for _ in range(4):
            hist, W, tp = RM.rand_traj(rng, M, 0, A, n, mF, m, rng.randint(0, 6), rng.randint(0, 6))
            pre = [('none', tuple(F(0) if k == 0 else F(rng.randint(0, 8), 4) * M.theta[0] for k in range(M.n_out)))
                   for _ in range(rng.randint(1, 3))]
            hist = pre + hist; W = (W[0] + len(pre), W[1] + len(pre)); tp += len(pre)
            rg = C.check_regime(M, 0, A, hist, W, n, mF, m)
            V = C.verify(M, 0, A, hist, W, n, mF, m, pairs=rg['pairs'], probe=tp)
            rd = V['r_detail']
            cnt['traj'] += 1
            md = rg['f3']
            cnt['a' if md == 'α' else 'b' if md == 'β' else 'none'] += 1
            cnt['r'] += V['r'] == 1
            if md is not None:
                cnt['r_f3'] += V['r'] == 1
            if not rd['P4R']:
                cnt['p4r_false'] += 1; cnt['p4r_false_r'] += V['r'] == 1
            cnt['R'] += V['R'] == 1
    print(f'  {cnt["traj"]} trajectories (each prefixed by 1–3 beats injecting only into the other outputs; baselines of the other columns in [−1, 1]): F3 = α {cnt["a"]}, β {cnt["b"]}, fails {cnt["none"]};')
    print(f'  of the {cnt["a"] + cnt["b"]} with F3 holding, the M1₀ witness gives r = 1 for {cnt["r_f3"]}; P4R fails for {cnt["p4r_false"]}, of which r = 1 for {cnt["p4r_false_r"]}; in 𝒟 with the R(T) witness established: {cnt["R"]}')
    # explicit example where F3 fails: the pairing letter AB makes the target-column baseline sum = θ (α fails), and the column baseline sum of output 1 is negative (β fails)
    pat = {'A': (1, 1, 0, 0), 'B': (0, 0, 1, 1), 'AB': (1, 1, 1, 1), 'none': (0, 0, 0, 0)}
    b = [[F(1, 4), F(-1, 2)] for _ in range(4)]
    lo = [[F(0), F(-2)] for _ in range(4)]
    hi = [[F(2), F(2)] for _ in range(4)]
    M = C.Mem(4, 2, pat, [F(3, 4)] * 2, [1, 1], [0, 0], [F(1, 2)] * 4, [F(1, 2)] * 2, F(1, 8), F(1, 16), 0, b, lo, hi)
    okC = C.check_C(M, 0, [0, 1], 6, 2, 2)[0]
    h = [('none', (F(0), F(3, 4)))] + [('AB', (F(1), F(3, 4))), Q, Q] * 6 + [Q] * 3 + [('A', (F(0), F(0)))]
    W = (len(h) - 1, len(h) - 1)
    rg = C.check_regime(M, 0, [0, 1], h, W, 6, 2, 2)
    V = C.verify(M, 0, [0, 1], h, W, 6, 2, 2, pairs=rg['pairs'])
    o = V['pre'][1]
    quiet_all = ('none', (F(0), F(0)))
    _, rq = M.step(o, quiet_all); _, ra = M.step(o, h[1])
    _, rb = M.step(o, ('none', (F(0), F(3, 4))))
    print(f'  F3-failure example: coefficient part C = {okC}; regime = {rg["ok"]} ({"; ".join(rg["why"])}); output 1 on first pairing beat 1: actual vt = {fr(ra["vt"][1])}, no firing; '
          f'after a β-type removal (entry set to none, I_1 kept) vt = {fr(rb["vt"][1])}, fires (new opening; the removal is not admissible); after removing everything down to the rest letter vt = {fr(rq["vt"][1])}, firing {rq["g"][1]}')
    print(f'    The criterion of this paper yields no r witness (r = {V["r"]}); following Chapter 11, 4.3, this is recorded as ⊥, not 0. The example lies outside C (F3 is an item of C_init).')
    print()

def v3():
    print('V3  Window without a clean probe: after the formative history, 3 rest beats and two consecutive A beats; W takes only the second A beat (m = 2)')
    import gate_class as GC, gate_criteria as GK, decision as DC
    M = C.frozen_snn_member(); A = [0, 1]
    h = FORM + [Q] * 3 + [('A', (F(0), F(0))), ('A', (F(0), F(0)))]
    W = (len(h) - 1, len(h) - 1)
    rg = C.check_regime(M, 0, A, h, W, 6, 2, 2)
    V = C.verify(M, 0, A, h, W, 6, 2, 2, pairs=rg['pairs'])
    ev = tev(V, A, W[0])
    pb = V['probe']
    print(f'  clean probe beats {rg["probes"]} (regime R2 fails: {"; ".join(rg["why"])}); {len(ev)} events in W acting on T: ' +
          ', '.join(f'{k[0]}{k[1]}{k[2]} = {fr(c)}' for k, c in ev))
    print(f'  at that beat (not a clean probe) pre-beat v_0 = {fr(V["pre"][W[0]][1][0])}; actual vt = {fr(pb["vt"])}, fires; with T set to b, vt = {fr(pb["vt_cf"])}, firing {pb["g_cf"]}; witness of this program R = {V["R"]}')
    G = GC.Gate(M.n_in, M.n_out, M.pat, M.lam, M.theta, M.vreset, M.dx, M.dy, M.Ap, M.Am, M.rho, M.b, M.lo, M.hi)
    I = GK.Instance('', G, h, W[0])
    D = DC.Decider(I)
    j1, j0 = DC.judge(I, D, True), DC.judge(I, D, False)
    print(f'  Chapter 11 D2: B_FD declared → Part = {j1["Part"]}, HDyn = {j1["HDyn"]}; not declared → Part = {j0["Part"]}, HDyn = {j0["HDyn"]}; exhausted {all(D.complete_u.values())}, witness re-run failures {D.verify_fail}')
    print('  Reading: the absence of a clean probe in W means only that the clean-probe witness does not apply (this window has a certificate probe instead, covered by Theorem 14-1⁺; see run_ext D1); it implies neither the absence of events nor a failure of qualification. The example is kept as a comparison and receives no negative verdict (as with the windows without a clean probe in main text 8.5; this example has a certificate probe; see run_ext D1).')
    print()

def v4():
    print('V4  Concrete example without events (S5 D-W; the statement concerns this specific history only): formative history of P + 8 rest beats + W = 6 rest beats')
    M = C.frozen_snn_member(); A = [0, 1]
    h = FORM + [Q] * 8 + [Q] * 6
    pre, recs = M.run(h)
    W = (26, 31)
    nT = sum(len([k for k in recs[t]['ev'] if k[0] in ('PLUS', 'MINUS', 'RELAX', 'CLIP') and k[1] in A and k[2] == 0]) for t in range(W[0], W[1] + 1))
    fired = [t for t in range(W[0], W[1] + 1) if recs[t]['g'][0]]
    print(f'  firing beats of gate 0 in W {fired}; {nT} events acting on T. Proof: no entry in W (no MINUS), I_0 = 0 and v_0 < θ (Lemmas 14.1, 14.2; no firing, no PLUS), ρ = 0 (no RELAX), w unchanged inside [lo, hi] (no CLIP).')
    print()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    print('The Necessity Form of Field-Concretization Qualification: sources and windows without a clean probe (run_source.py)')
    print()
    v1(); v2(); v3(); v4()
    print('End.')
~~~

A.13  run\_ext.py  Run entry: extended verification (D0–D8)

~~~python
"""Appendix program of The Necessity Form of Field-Concretization Qualification: extended verification D0–D8 (main text, Sections 5 and 6). Standard library only, exact rational arithmetic; seeds fixed, output deterministic.
Run (in this directory): python3 run_ext.py
The program gives finite verification, explicit witnesses and counterexamples; the universal conclusions rest on the proofs in the main text.
"""
import sys, os, hashlib, random, itertools
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
sys.path.insert(0, HERE)
from fractions import Fraction as F
import t14_core as C
import t14_ext as X
from t14_core import fr
import run_main as RM
T13_SHA = {'t13_gate.py': 'd5bf9f2d82642d59986481bfe28ef4ba71e40a4ed5b84b392eecfcf04a217974'}
Q2 = ('none', (F(0), F(0)))
FORM = [('A', (F(1), F(0))), Q2, Q2] * 6
A01 = [0, 1]

def d2(M, hist, t0, T):
    import gate_class as GC, gate_criteria as GK, decision as DC
    G = GC.Gate(M.n_in, M.n_out, M.pat, M.lam, M.theta, M.vreset, M.dx, M.dy, M.Ap, M.Am, M.rho, M.b, M.lo, M.hi)
    I = GK.Instance('', G, hist, t0, T=[('w', i, j) for (i, j) in T])
    D = DC.Decider(I)
    j1, j0 = DC.judge(I, D, True), DC.judge(I, D, False)
    return dict(P1=j1['Part'], H1=j1['HDyn'], P0=j0['Part'], H0=j0['HDyn'], hs=j1['HDyn_sep'], hj=j1['HDyn_joint'],
                comp=all(D.complete_u.values()), vf=D.verify_fail)

def d0():
    print('D0  version binding; beat-by-beat comparison of an independent implementation of the three-gate design of 𝒦_PA^≺ with t13_gate.step of the Chapter 13 appendix')
    for f, h in RM.APPX_B.items():
        assert hashlib.sha256(open(os.path.join(HERE, f), 'rb').read()).hexdigest() == h
    for f, h in T13_SHA.items():
        got = hashlib.sha256(open(os.path.join(HERE, f), 'rb').read()).hexdigest()
        assert got == h
        print(f'  {f}  SHA-256 {got[:16]}…  matches Appendix B.1 of Chapter 13 v1.0; the six Chapter 11 files as in run_main S0')
    import t13_gate as TG
    rng = random.Random(20261201)
    bad = n = 0
    for _ in range(200):
        P = rand_pa(rng, need=False)
        st = P.init(); st13 = (st[0], st[1], st[2], st[3], [0, 0, 0])
        Pt = P.as_t13()
        for _ in range(30):
            nm = rng.choice(list(P.pat)); I = (F(rng.randint(0, 4), 2), F(rng.randint(0, 4), 2), F(0))
            st, r = P.step(st, (nm, I))
            st13, g, vt, ev = TG.step(Pt, st13, P.pat[nm], list(I))
            n += 1
            if st[0] != st13[0] or st[1] != st13[1] or st[2] != st13[2] or st[3] != st13[3] or [int(q) for q in g] != r['g']:
                bad += 1
            for k, c in r['ev'].items():
                if k[0] in ('PLUS', 'MINUS', 'CLIP') and ev[k] != (True, c):
                    bad += 1
    print(f'  200 random PA3 members × 30 beats = {n} beats: mismatches between PA3.step of this paper and t13_gate.step of Chapter 13 (mode = dag) in full state, gates and PLUS/MINUS/CLIP components {bad}')
    print()

def d1():
    print('D1  Theorem 14-1⁺: certificate probes (Lemma 14.7) in place of clean probes; windows without a clean probe and with several probes')
    M = C.frozen_snn_member()
    rng = random.Random(20261031)
    cls = dict(noA=0, clean=0, cert_only=0, uncert=0)
    certW = []
    for kk in range(200):
        win = [(rng.choice(['A', 'B', 'none', 'none']), (F(0), F(0))) for _ in range(18)]
        hist = FORM + [Q2] * 8 + win
        W = (26, 43)
        rg = C.check_regime(M, 0, A01, hist, W, 6, 2, 2)
        cp = X.certified_probes(M, 0, A01, hist, W, 15)
        if not any(s[0] == 'A' for s in win):
            cls['noA'] += 1
        elif rg['ok']:
            cls['clean'] += 1
        elif cp:
            cls['cert_only'] += 1; certW.append((hist, W, cp))
        else:
            cls['uncert'] += 1
    print(f'  random input family of Chapter 11, 200 histories (seed 20261031): no A {cls["noA"]}; with a clean probe {cls["clean"]}; without a clean probe but with a certificate probe {cls["cert_only"]}; with A but no certificate probe {cls["uncert"]}')
    okc = 0
    for hist, W, cp in certW:
        V = C.verify(M, 0, A01, hist, W, 6, 2, 2, pairs=[3 * k for k in range(6)], probe=cp[0])
        okc += V['R'] == 1
    print(f'    of the {len(certW)} histories without a clean probe but with a certificate probe: R witness of this program holds on the first certificate probe in {okc}')
    xs = certW[:4]
    for hist, W, cp in xs:
        r = d2(M, hist, W[0], [(0, 0), (1, 0)])
        print(f'    Chapter 11 D2 (certificate probes {cp}): B_FD declared → Part = {r["P1"]}, HDyn = {r["H1"]}; not declared → HDyn = {r["H0"]}; exhausted {r["comp"]}, re-run failures {r["vf"]}')
    # window with two consecutive A beats
    h = FORM + [Q2] * 3 + [('A', (F(0), F(0))), ('A', (F(0), F(0)))]
    cp = X.certified_probes(M, 0, A01, h, (len(h) - 1, len(h) - 1), 15)
    print(f'  two consecutive A beats, W = the second (run_source V3): certificate probes {cp} (the preceding beat contains A, U = 0; Σ_A b = 1/2 < θ); covered by Theorem 14-1⁺')
    # random members × random windows without a rest requirement
    rng = random.Random(20261202)
    tot = cert = Rok = unc = 0
    for _ in range(300):
        Mr, A, n, mF, m = RM.rand_member(rng)
        for _ in range(4):
            hist, W, tp = RM.rand_traj(rng, Mr, 0, A, n, mF, 0, rng.randint(0, 12), rng.randint(0, 12))
            pn = C.check_regime(Mr, 0, A, hist, W, n, mF, 0)['pairs'][-1]
            cp = X.certified_probes(Mr, 0, A, hist, W, pn)
            tot += 1
            if cp:
                cert += 1
                V = C.verify(Mr, 0, A, hist, W, n, mF, 0, probe=cp[0])
                Rok += V['R'] == 1 and V['hdyn'] == 1
            else:
                unc += 1
    print(f'  random 𝒦_LS members 300 × 4 histories (no rest imposed before the probe): with a certificate probe {cert}, R witness holds on {Rok} of them; without a certificate probe {unc} (not adjudicated)')
    # exhaustive on the frozen member: interlude 3 beats + probe + tail 2 beats, no rest requirement
    letters = [(nm, (F(i0), F(0))) for nm in ['none', 'A', 'B', 'AB'] for i0 in (0, 1)]
    c1 = c2 = c3 = c4 = 0
    for combo in itertools.product(letters, repeat=5):
        hist = FORM + list(combo[:3]) + [('A', (F(0), F(0)))] + list(combo[3:])
        W = (18, len(hist) - 1)
        cp = X.certified_probes(M, 0, A01, hist, W, 15)
        if cp:
            c1 += 1
            V = C.verify(M, 0, A01, hist, W, 6, 2, 2, probe=cp[0])
            c2 += V['R'] == 1
        else:
            c3 += 1
            V = C.verify(M, 0, A01, hist, W, 6, 2, 2)
            c4 += V['R'] == 1
    print(f'  exhaustive on the frozen member (interlude 3 beats after formation, fixed probe, tail 2 beats, 8^5 = 32768 histories): with a certificate probe {c1}, R witness {c2}; '
          f'without a certificate probe {c3}, of which this program still obtains an R witness at some other probe beat in {c4} (outside the theorem, not adjudicated)')
    print()

def d2_sat():
    print('D2  clipping and E11 (Proposition 14-4): with saturation at window start, no effective joint action on T inside W under the joint reading')
    M = C.frozen_snn_member(wmax=F(7, 10))
    hist = FORM + ([('A', (F(0), F(0))), Q2, Q2] * 6) + [Q2] * 2 + [('A', (F(0), F(0))), Q2, Q2, ('AB', (F(1), F(0))), Q2, Q2, ('A', (F(0), F(0)))]
    ts = 18 + 18 + 2
    W = (ts, len(hist) - 1)
    ok, items, der = C.check_C(M, 0, A01, 6, 2, 2)
    V = C.verify(M, 0, A01, hist, W, 6, 2, 2, pairs=[3 * k for k in range(6)])
    gap = [M.hi[i][0] - V['pre'][ts][0][i][0] for i in A01]
    nets = X.joint_nets(V, M, 0, A01, W)
    sep_eff = [k for t in range(W[0], W[1] + 1) for k, c in V['recs'][t]['ev'].items() if k[0] == 'PLUS' and k[1] in A01 and k[2] == 0 and abs(c) > M.eps]
    print(f'  frozen member with hi = 7/10: C = {ok}; hi − w at window start = {[fr(g) for g in gap]} (≤ ε); maximal joint net increment of the T positions in W {fr(max(nets.values()))}; effective PLUS under the separate reading {len(sep_eff)}; R witness of this program (separate) = {V["R"]}, NS* = {V["probe"]["ns_star"]}')
    r = d2(M, hist, W[0], [(0, 0), (1, 0)])
    print(f'  Chapter 11 D2: separate HDyn = {r["hs"]}, joint HDyn = {r["hj"]} ⇒ verdict {r["H1"]} (exhausted {r["comp"]}); E11 cannot be dropped here. A criterion for zero net change under the joint reading belongs to the open items of Chapter 1, Section 8')
    # random: saturation at window start ⇒ joint net increment ≤ ε; NS* holds ⇒ the joint reading also yields a witness
    rng = random.Random(20261203)
    satn = viol = nsok = nsn = 0
    for _ in range(300):
        Mr, A, n, mF, m = RM.rand_member(rng)
        for _ in range(4):
            hist, W, tp = RM.rand_traj(rng, Mr, 0, A, n, mF, m, rng.randint(0, 30), rng.randint(0, 10))
            V = C.verify(Mr, 0, A, hist, W, n, mF, m, probe=tp)
            if all(Mr.hi[i][0] - V['pre'][W[0]][0][i][0] <= Mr.eps for i in A):
                satn += 1
                viol += any(v > Mr.eps for v in X.joint_nets(V, Mr, 0, A, W).values())
            if V['probe']['ns_star']:
                nsn += 1
                o = V['pre'][tp]; rr = V['recs'][tp]
                good = False
                for i in A:
                    net = V['pre'][tp + 1][0][i][0] - o[0][i][0]
                    clip = ('CLIP', i, 0) in rr['ev']
                    if (not clip and abs(rr['ev'].get(('PLUS', i, 0), F(0))) > Mr.eps) or (clip and net > Mr.eps):
                        good = True
                nsok += good
    print(f'  random 300 members × 4 histories: saturated at window start {satn}, of which an effective joint net increment occurs in W in {viol}; NS* holds in {nsn}, of which the probe beat carries an effective T action under the joint reading (an unclipped effective PLUS, or clipped with net increment > ε) in {nsok}')
    print()

def d3():
    print('D3  source: the two relaxations F3*, F3° of F3 (Proposition 14-5 (1)) and the blocking construction outside F3ᵤ (Proposition 14-5 (2))')
    rng = random.Random(20261204)
    tot = f3 = f3s = f3d = fu = ok_u = 0
    blk = blk_ok = rest = 0
    for _ in range(2000):
        n_in = rng.choice([2, 3, 4]); n_out = rng.choice([2, 3])
        A = sorted(rng.sample(range(n_in), rng.randint(1, n_in - 1)))
        pats = {'none': (0,) * n_in, 'pA': tuple(1 if i in A else 0 for i in range(n_in))}
        for kk in range(rng.randint(1, 4)):
            wa = rng.choice([0, 1])
            pats[f'q{kk}'] = tuple(wa if i in A else rng.choice([0, 1]) for i in range(n_in))
        th = [F(1)] + [F(rng.randint(1, 4), 2) for _ in range(n_out - 1)]
        vres = [F(0)] + [rng.choice([F(0), th[k], 2 * th[k]]) for k in range(1, n_out)]
        b = [[F(rng.randint(0, 6), 8)] + [F(rng.randint(-12, 6), 8) for _ in range(n_out - 1)] for _ in range(n_in)]
        lo = [[F(0)] + [F(-3)] * (n_out - 1) for _ in range(n_in)]
        hi = [[F(3)] * n_out for _ in range(n_in)]
        lam = [F(rng.randint(1, 8), 8) for _ in range(n_out)]
        M = C.Mem(n_in, n_out, pats, lam, th, vres, [F(1, 2)] * n_in, [F(1, 2)] * n_out, F(1, 8), F(1, 16), 0, b, lo, hi)
        withA = [nm for nm, p in pats.items() if all(p[i] for i in A)]
        a1 = rng.choice(withA)
        sig = (a1, tuple(th[0] if k == 0 else F(rng.randint(0, 2), 2) for k in range(n_out)))
        tot += 1
        c3 = C.f3_mode(M, 0, sig) is not None
        cs = bool(X.f3star(M, 0, A, sig))
        cd = X.f3deg(M, 0)
        f3 += c3; f3s += cs; f3d += cd
        if c3 or cs or cd:
            fu += 1
            # after an arbitrary prefix on the other units (1–3 beats, injecting only into the other units, which may make them fire), check at the first pairing beat: an admissible same-beat removal satisfying M1₀ exists
            o = M.init()
            for _ in range(rng.randint(1, 3)):
                o, _r = M.step(o, ('none', tuple(F(0) if k == 0 else F(rng.randint(0, 6), 2) for k in range(n_out))))
            ws, hits = X.m10_any(M, 0, A, o, sig, M.eps)
            ok_u += bool(ws)
        else:
            k = X.blocker2(M, 0, A, sig)
            if k is None:
                rest += 1
                continue
            pre = ('none', tuple(F(0) if kk != k else M.theta[k] + 1 for kk in range(n_out)))
            sig2 = (a1, tuple(th[0] if kk == 0 else F(0) for kk in range(n_out)))
            o, r0 = M.step(M.init(), pre)
            ws, hits = X.m10_any(M, 0, A, o, sig2, M.eps)
            blk += 1
            blk_ok += r0['g'][k] == 1 and not ws
    print(f'  random members {tot} (other units: θ_k ∈ {{1/2, 1, 3/2, 2}}, v_reset,k ∈ {{0, θ_k, 2θ_k}}, baselines of the other columns may be negative; alphabet of 1–4 A-indecomposable letters):')
    print(f'    F3 holds {f3}; F3* holds {f3s}; F3° holds {f3d}; F3ᵤ = F3 ∨ F3* ∨ F3° holds {fu}; of these, after an arbitrary prefix on the other units an admissible same-beat removal satisfying M1₀ exists at the first pairing beat in {ok_u}/{fu}')
    print(f'    F3ᵤ fails while the blocking condition holds: {blk}; a one-beat prefix makes blocking unit k fire (v_k = v_reset,k), and exhausting all admissible same-beat removals at the first pairing beat finds none satisfying M1₀ in {blk_ok}; neither holds: {rest} (no verdict, registered)')
    # explicit example
    pats = {'none': (0, 0), 'pA': (1, 0), 'AB': (1, 1)}
    M = C.Mem(2, 2, pats, [F(1, 2), F(1)], [F(1), F(1)], [F(0), F(2)], [F(1, 2)] * 2, [F(1, 2)] * 2, F(1, 8), F(1, 16), 0,
              [[F(1, 2), F(-1, 2)], [F(3, 4), F(-3, 4)]], [[F(0), F(-3)], [F(0), F(-3)]], [[F(3), F(3)], [F(3), F(3)]])
    sig = ('AB', (F(1), F(0)))
    o, r0 = M.step(M.init(), ('none', (F(0), F(2))))
    ws, hits = X.m10_any(M, 0, [0], o, sig, M.eps)
    print(f'  explicit example: A = {{entry 0}}, first pairing letter AB (sum of target-column baselines 5/4 ≥ θ, α fails); output 1: v_reset = 2, λ = 1, column baselines (−1/2, −3/4); '
          f'F3 = {C.f3_mode(M, 0, sig)}, F3* = {X.f3star(M, 0, [0], sig)}, F3° = {X.f3deg(M, 0)}, blocking unit = {X.blocker2(M, 0, [0], sig)}; '
          f'after a prefix makes output 1 fire, effective writes on T at the first pairing beat {len(hits)}, same-beat removals satisfying M1₀ {len(ws)}')
    print()

def d4():
    print('D4  formation threshold (Proposition 14-6): deterministic formation outcome Φ(g), improved bound F2′ and exactness')
    M = C.frozen_snn_member()
    rows = []
    for n in range(1, 9):
        w, incs = X.formation_outcome(M, 0, A01, [2] * (n - 1), n)
        hist = [('A', (F(1), F(0))), Q2, Q2] * n
        pre, recs = M.run(hist)
        sim = pre[3 * (n - 1) + 1][0][0][0]
        phi = sum(w.values())
        f2 = sum(min(M.hi[i][0], M.b[i][0] + n * (M.Ap - M.Am / (1 - M.dy[0] ** 3))) for i in A01)
        f2p, _ = X.f2prime(M, 0, A01, n, 2)
        rows.append((n, phi, f2p, f2, sim == w[0]))
    print('  frozen member, equal spacing m_F = 2: n | Φ = Σ_A w(p_n+1) | F2′ bound | F2 bound | agrees with the Mem run')
    for n, phi, f2p, f2, same in rows:
        print(f'    {n} | {fr(phi)} | {fr(f2p)} | {fr(f2)} | {same}')
    print('  threshold θ = 1: Φ first ≥ 1 at n = 4; F2′ at n = ' + str(next(n for n, p, q, r, s in rows if q >= 1)) + '; F2 at n = ' + str(next(n for n, p, q, r, s in rows if r >= 1)))
    # necessity construction: single-probe window after a long rest when Φ < θ
    form3 = [('A', (F(1), F(0))), Q2, Q2] * 3
    for L in (8, 20):
        hist = form3 + [Q2] * L + [('A', (F(0), F(0)))]
        W = (len(hist) - 1, len(hist) - 1)
        V = C.verify(M, 0, A01, hist, W, 3, 2, 2)
        evs = [(k, c) for k, c in V['recs'][W[0]]['ev'].items() if k[0] in ('PLUS', 'MINUS', 'CLIP', 'RELAX') and k[1] in A01 and k[2] == 0]
        print(f'  n = 3 (Φ = {fr(rows[2][1])} < θ), single-probe window after rest {L} beats: gate {V["recs"][W[0]]["g"][0]}; events on T ' +
              ', '.join(f'{k[0]}{k[1]}0 = {fr(c)}' for k, c in evs) + f' ({sum(abs(c) > M.eps for k, c in evs)} with |c| > ε)')
    hist = form3 + [Q2] * 8 + [('A', (F(0), F(0)))]
    r = d2(M, hist, len(hist) - 1, [(0, 0), (1, 0)])
    print(f'    Chapter 11 D2 (rest 8 beats): HDyn = {r["H1"]} (B_FD declared) / {r["H0"]} (not declared); a = 0, B_FD not consumed')
    # random: the order F2 ≤ F2′ ≤ Φ; R witness on members with Φ ≥ θ where F2 fails
    rng = random.Random(20261205)
    order_bad = cnt = gain = Rgain = 0
    for _ in range(400):
        Mr, A, n, mF, m = RM.rand_member(rng)
        for _ in range(3):
            nn = max(1, n - rng.randint(0, 3))
            gaps = [mF + rng.randint(0, 3) for _ in range(nn - 1)]
            w, incs = X.formation_outcome(Mr, 0, A, gaps, nn)
            phi = sum(w.values())
            f2p, _ = X.f2prime(Mr, 0, A, nn, mF)
            dF = Mr.Ap - Mr.Am / (1 - Mr.dy[0] ** (mF + 1))
            f2 = sum(min(Mr.hi[i][0], Mr.b[i][0] + nn * dF) for i in A)
            cnt += 1
            order_bad += not (f2 <= f2p <= phi)
            if phi >= Mr.theta[0] and f2 < Mr.theta[0] and min(w[i] - Mr.b[i][0] for i in A) > Mr.eps:
                gain += 1
                th = Mr.theta[0]; no = Mr.n_out
                Qr = ('none', (F(0),) * no)
                hist = []
                for k in range(nn):
                    if k:
                        hist += [Qr] * gaps[k - 1]
                    hist.append(('pA', (th,) + (F(0),) * (no - 1)))
                pn = len(hist) - 1
                for _ in range(rng.randint(0, 10)):
                    hist.append((rng.choice(list(Mr.pat)), tuple(rng.choice([F(0), th]) for _ in range(no))))
                hist += [Qr] * m + [('pA', (F(0),) * no)]
                tp = len(hist) - 1
                V = C.verify(Mr, 0, A, hist, (pn + 1, tp), nn, mF, m, probe=tp)
                Rgain += V['R'] == 1
    print(f'  random members 400 × 3 (random spacing ≥ m_F): violations of the order F2 ≤ F2′ ≤ Φ {order_bad}/{cnt}; Φ ≥ θ while F2 fails in {gain} cases, on which (Theorem 14-1⁺ with Φ in place of F2) the R witness holds in {Rgain}')
    print()

def d5():
    print('D5  K2′ and boundaries: A₋ = 0 (d_y may be 1), λ = 1 (Σ_A b = 0); behavior of the erosion construction when K2 fails at d_x = 1 (registered)')
    rng = random.Random(20261206)
    a = b_ = c_ = d_ = 0
    for _ in range(200):
        M0, A, n, mF, m = RM.rand_member(rng)
        M = C.Mem(M0.n_in, M0.n_out, M0.pat, M0.lam, M0.theta, M0.vreset, M0.dx, [F(1)] * M0.n_out, M0.Ap, F(0), 0, M0.b, M0.lo, M0.hi, M0.eps)
        hist, W, tp = RM.rand_traj(rng, M, 0, A, n, 0, m, rng.randint(0, 30), rng.randint(0, 30))
        pn = C.check_regime(M, 0, A, hist, W, n, 0, m)['pairs'][-1]
        pre, recs = M.run(hist)
        a += 1
        b_ += all(pre[t + 1][0][i][0] >= pre[t][0][i][0] for t in range(pn + 1, len(hist)) for i in A)
    print(f'  A₋ = 0, d_y = 1 (original K2 fails, K2′ holds): T positions monotone nondecreasing after formation in {b_}/{a} random trajectories')
    for _ in range(200):
        n_in = 2; A = [0, 1]
        pat = {'none': (0, 0), 'pA': (1, 1)}
        M = C.Mem(2, 1, pat, [F(1)], [F(1)], [0], [F(1, 2)] * 2, [F(1, 2)], F(1, 4), F(1, 16), 0, [[F(0)], [F(0)]], [[F(0)], [F(0)]], [[F(3)], [F(3)]])
        hist, W, tp = RM.rand_traj(rng, M, 0, A, 4, 2, 0, rng.randint(0, 10), rng.randint(0, 5))
        V = C.verify(M, 0, A, hist, W, 4, 2, 0, probe=tp)
        c_ += 1; d_ += V['R'] == 1
    okC = C.check_C(M, 0, A, 4, 2, 0)[0]
    print(f'  λ = 1, Σ_A b = 0 (K3(0) holds: C = {okC}): R witness in {d_}/{c_} random trajectories')
    # d_x = 1: erosion construction with K2 failing
    M = C.Mem(4, 2, {'A': (1, 1, 0, 0), 'B': (0, 0, 1, 1), 'AB': (1, 1, 1, 1), 'none': (0, 0, 0, 0)}, [F(3, 4)] * 2, [1, 1], [0, 0],
              [F(1)] * 4, [F(1, 2)] * 2, F(1, 8), F(1, 12), 0, [[F(1, 4)] * 2 for _ in range(4)], [[F(0)] * 2 for _ in range(4)], [[F(2)] * 2 for _ in range(4)])
    hist, ts = RM.erosion_hist(M, A01, 9, 2, K1=12, K2=12, cycles=200)
    pre, recs = M.run(hist)
    zmin = min(min(o[0][i][0] - M.b[i][0] for i in A01) for o in pre[ts:])
    dec = sum(1 for t in range(ts, len(hist)) if any(pre[t + 1][0][i][0] < pre[t][0][i][0] for i in A01))
    print(f'  d_x = 1, A₋ = 1/12 (K2 fails): after 200 cycles of the erosion construction of Proposition 14-3, min z = {fr(zmin)}, beats with a T-position decrease {dec}. When the eligibility trace x does not decay, erosion is outweighed by potentiation, so the antecedent d_x < 1 of Proposition 14-3 cannot be dropped (exactness of K2 at d_x = 1 is registered as to be proven)')
    print()

def d6():
    print('D6  random input regime (Proposition 14-8; reading: after formation the letters of the beats are i.i.d.; declare the distribution π first, then report a probability lower bound)')
    M = C.frozen_snn_member()
    pi = [(('none', (F(0), F(0))), F(1, 2)), (('A', (F(0), F(0))), F(1, 8)), (('B', (F(0), F(0))), F(1, 8)),
          (('AB', (F(0), F(0))), F(1, 16)), (('none', (F(1), F(0))), F(1, 16)), (('A', (F(1), F(0))), F(1, 16)), (('B', (F(1), F(0))), F(1, 16))]
    assert sum(p for _, p in pi) == 1
    pq = F(1, 2); pp = F(1, 8)
    rng = random.Random(20261207)
    cum = []
    acc = F(0)
    for sig, p in pi:
        acc += p; cum.append((acc, sig))
    def draw():
        u = F(rng.randint(0, 2 ** 20 - 1), 2 ** 20)
        for c, sig in cum:
            if u < c:
                return sig
    print('  π: (none,0) 1/2, (A,0) 1/8, (B,0) 1/8, (AB,0) 1/16, (none,I₀=1) 1/16, (A,I₀=1) 1/16, (B,I₀=1) 1/16; m = 2')
    for L in (6, 12, 24, 48):
        N = 1000
        clean = cert = Rw = 0
        for _ in range(N):
            win = [draw() for _ in range(L)]
            hist = FORM + win
            W = (18, len(hist) - 1)
            rg = C.check_regime(M, 0, A01, hist, W, 6, 2, 2)
            cp = X.certified_probes(M, 0, A01, hist, W, 15)
            clean += rg['ok']; cert += bool(cp)
            if cp:
                V = C.verify(M, 0, A01, hist, W, 6, 2, 2, probe=cp[0])
                Rw += V['R'] == 1
        lb = X.iid_lower_bound(pq, pp, 2, L)
        print(f'    L = {L}: lower bound 1 − (1 − pq²·pp)^⌊L/3⌋ = {fr(lb)}; sample {N}: with a clean probe {clean}, with a certificate probe {cert}, R witness on the certificate probe {Rw}')
    print('  Reading: the universal part of R (invariants, retention, pivot) is universal over paths; randomness enters only the probability of the event “W contains a certificate probe”. Probability-1 version: in an infinitely long continuation, certificate probes occur infinitely often almost surely.')
    print()

def rand_pa(rng, need=True):
    while True:
        n_in = rng.choice([2, 3, 4])
        A = sorted(rng.sample(range(n_in), rng.randint(1, n_in - 1)))
        pats = {'none': (0,) * n_in, 'pA': tuple(1 if i in A else 0 for i in range(n_in))}
        for kk in range(rng.randint(0, 2)):
            wa = rng.choice([0, 1]); pats[f'q{kk}'] = tuple(wa if i in A else rng.choice([0, 1]) for i in range(n_in))
        lam = [F(rng.randint(0, 7), 8) for _ in range(3)]
        th = [F(1), F(rng.randint(1, 4), 4), F(rng.randint(1, 8), 2)]
        dx = [F(rng.randint(0, 6), 8) for _ in range(n_in)]
        dy = [F(rng.randint(0, 6), 8) for _ in range(3)]
        Am = F(rng.randint(0, 4), 32)
        Ap = max(Am / (1 - dy[1]), F(1, 100)) + F(rng.randint(1, 4), 32)
        m = rng.randint(0, 4)
        room = th[0] - lam[0] ** (m + 1) * th[0]
        if room <= 0:
            continue
        bM = [room / len(A) * F(rng.randint(1, 7), 8) if i in A else F(rng.randint(0, 4), 8) for i in range(n_in)]
        u = th[1] + F(rng.randint(0, 4), 8) if need else F(rng.randint(0, 8), 8)
        P = X.PA3(n_in, pats, lam, th, dx, dy, Ap, Am, u, bM, F(3))
        P.A, P.m = A, m
        if not need:
            return P
        for n in range(1, 300):
            ok, items = X.check_C_PA(P, A, n, 1, m)
            if ok:
                P.n = n
                return P

def pa_traj(rng, P, inter, tail):
    A, n, m = P.A, P.n, P.m
    Q = ('none', (F(0), F(0), F(0)))
~~~
