~~~python
    hist = []
    for k in range(n):
        hist += [Q] * (1 + rng.randint(0, 2) if k else rng.randint(0, 1))
        hist.append(('pA', (F(rng.randint(0, 2), 2), P.theta[1] + F(rng.randint(0, 2), 4), F(0))))
    pn = len(hist) - 1
    for _ in range(inter):
        hist.append((rng.choice(list(P.pat)), (rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 2), F(1)]), rng.choice([F(0), P.theta[1]]), F(0))))
    hist += [Q] * m + [('pA', (F(0), F(0), F(0)))]
    tp = len(hist) - 1
    for _ in range(tail):
        hist.append((rng.choice(list(P.pat)), (rng.choice([F(0), F(1)]), rng.choice([F(0), P.theta[1]]), F(0))))
    return hist, pn, tp

def d7():
    print('D7  gated extension class 𝒦_PA^≺ (Chapter 13 v1.0, Definitions 5 and 7; PA-2 closure): FD-level C_PA ⇒ R_FD on the three-gate design M ≺ D (Z never fires) (Theorem 14-9)')
    rng = random.Random(20261208)
    tot = ok = inv = zf = sat = 0
    for _ in range(250):
        P = rand_pa(rng)
        for _ in range(4):
            hist, pn, tp = pa_traj(rng, P, rng.randint(0, 25), rng.randint(0, 15))
            Wt = (pn + 1, len(hist) - 1)
            res = X.pa_witness(P, P.A, hist, Wt, tp)
            pre = res['pre']
            mono = all(pre[t + 1][0][i][0] >= pre[t][0][i][0] for t in range(pn + 1, len(hist)) for i in P.A)
            # (6) first pairing beat: removal down to the rest letter (none, 0, 0, 0) opens no gate and PLUS_iM does not occur
            p1 = next(t for t, sg in enumerate(hist) if sg[0] == 'pA' and sg[1][1] >= P.theta[1])
            o1 = pre[p1]; r1 = res['recs'][p1]
            _, rq = P.step(o1, ('none', (F(0), F(0), F(0))))
            six = all(rq['g'][k] <= r1['g'][k] for k in range(3)) and any(('PLUS', i, 0) in r1['ev'] and abs(r1['ev'][('PLUS', i, 0)]) > P.eps
                                                                          and ('PLUS', i, 0) not in rq['ev'] for i in P.A)
            tot += 1
            ok += bool(res['eff']) and bool(res['fd']) and res['path'] and res['q'] and not res['zfire'] and six
            inv += not mono; zf += res['zfire']; sat += bool(res['sat'])
    print(f'  random PA3 members 250 (satisfying C_PA) × 4 trajectories: PLUS_iM effective at the probe beat; with T set to b neither M nor D fires and PLUS_iM does not occur; retention at window end; Z does not fire; at the first pairing beat, removal down to the rest letter opens no gate and PLUS_iM does not occur; all hold in {ok}/{tot}; '
          f'T-position decreases after formation {inv}; Z fires {zf}; clipping at the probe beat {sat}')
    # contrast: PD1 fails (u < θ_D)
    rng = random.Random(20261209)
    c1 = c1ok = 0
    for _ in range(100):
        P = rand_pa(rng)
        P.u = P.theta[1] / 2
        hist, pn, tp = pa_traj(rng, P, 0, 0)
        res = X.pa_witness(P, P.A, hist, (pn + 1, tp), tp)
        c1 += 1; c1ok += bool(res['fd'])
    print(f'  contrast with PD1 failing (u = θ_D/2): of 100 single-probe windows, an FD witness at the probe beat in {c1ok} (M fires but D does not, so PLUS_iM does not occur in the first place)')
    print('  Strength: the conclusion is FD-level: effective action, a 𝒟₁ functional-dependence witness, an actual event-graph path (T → SYN → M → FIN(M,D) → D → PLUS_iM), and retention at window end;')
    print('       reading back HistCond needs a local correspondence lemma on 𝒦_PA^≺ (Chapter 13 U13-16, Chapter 11 U8; unproven), and r needs the correspondence of criterion PS on the extension class (unproven).')
    print()

def d8():
    print('D8  Family 1ᵍ: a non-neural reading of 𝒦_LS (threshold-scour pool network); formal applicability of Theorem 14-1⁺ and the physical budget')
    # Reading: entry i = an upstream water source (out-of-boundary inflow pulse); gating unit j = a sink pool (water level v, leakage coefficient λ; when the level reaches the critical value θ it scours and empties);
    # Structural Position w_ij = channel conductance from source i to sink j (the part above baseline is the Trace-Response); x_i = recent wetness of source i; y_j = loose material left by the sink's recent scour;
    # PLUS = scour widening the channel (by wetness); MINUS = inflow carrying loose material into the channel and clogging it (by loose material); I_j = direct out-of-boundary water injection.
    M = C.frozen_snn_member()
    ok, items, der = C.check_C(M, 0, A01, 6, 2, 2)
    print(f'  frozen constants taken as declared parameters (nondimensionalized): C_LS = {ok}, so Theorem 14-1⁺ applies word for word under this reading (formal layer)')
    rng = random.Random(20261210)
    neg = tot = 0
    worst = F(0)
    letters = [(nm, (F(i0), F(0))) for nm in ['none', 'A', 'B', 'AB'] for i0 in (0, 1)]
    for _ in range(500):
        hist = FORM + [rng.choice(letters) for _ in range(40)]
        pre, recs = M.run(hist)
        L, mn = X.loose_store(M, 0, recs, 0)
        tot += 1; neg += mn < 0; worst = min(worst, mn)
    bound = M.Am * 2 * 4 * 58
    print(f'  loose-material store L₀ = 0: in 500 random histories (length 58) the store goes negative in {neg}, minimum {fr(worst)} (random histories expose no problem)')
    h = FORM + ([('none', (F(1), F(0)))] * 8 + [('B', (F(0), F(0)))] * 3 + [Q2] * 6) * 60
    pre, recs = M.run(h)
    L, mn = X.loose_store(M, 0, recs, 0)
    t_neg = next(t for t, q in enumerate(L) if q < 0)
    print(f'  adversarial history (after formation repeat [injection scours on its own 8 beats → B inflow 3 beats without firing → rest 6 beats] × 60): store at the end of the formation period {fr(L[18])}, first negative at beat {t_neg}, minimum {fr(mn)}; '
          f'B inflow carries the loose material left by scour into the channel (MINUS), and these beats bring no new scour, so the material budget of the reading does not close automatically')
    print(f'  sufficient condition: with L₀ ≥ A₋·y_sup·n_in·(history length) = {fr(bound)} the store stays nonnegative (clogging per beat ≤ A₋·y_sup·n_in); this is an additionally declared resource antecedent, not implied by C_LS')
    print('  Conclusion: Family 1ᵍ gives a non-trivial non-neural witness at the formal layer (HDyn only through threshold gating, ρ = 0), but the physical construction certificate (budget, energy account, empirical basis of the constitutive law) has not been obtained.')
    print()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    print('The Necessity Form of Field-Concretization Qualification: extended verification (run_ext.py)')
    print()
    d0(); d1(); d2_sat(); d3(); d4(); d5(); d6(); d7(); d8()
    print('End.')
~~~

A.14  run\_cert.py  Run entry: inclusion and soundness of certificate probes, bilinear comparison (E1–E5)

~~~python
"""Appendix program of The Necessity Form of Field-Concretization Qualification: inclusion and soundness of certificate probes, bilinear comparison (E1–E5). Standard library only, exact rational arithmetic; fixed seeds.
Run (in this directory): python3 run_cert.py
E1  The endpoint where K3 holds with equality: the test without the strictness flag misses a clean probe; the certificate with the strictness flag includes it.
E2  Inclusion: every m-clean probe is a certificate probe—the random members cover m = 0, λ = 0, λ = 1, and K3 both strict and at the equality endpoint; exhaustive over the frozen variants.
E3  Soundness: on certificate probes the gate does close once T is set to b (random members, adversarial prefixes); the trap example for “mechanically switching to ≤”.
E4  Coverage statistics (the Chapter 11 random family; exhaustive over the frozen member).
E5  State-dependent functional support of the ungated F-layer bilinear process c = k·x·y (k ≠ 0).
"""
import sys, os, random, itertools
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
sys.path.insert(0, HERE)
from fractions import Fraction as F
import t14_core as C
import t14_ext as X
from t14_core import fr
import run_main as RM
Q2 = ('none', (F(0), F(0)))
FORM = [('A', (F(1), F(0))), Q2, Q2] * 6
A01 = [0, 1]

def e1():
    print('E1  Endpoint where K3 holds with equality: λ = 1/2, W0 = 7/16 (Σ_A b = 7/8, λ³θ + Σ_A b = 1, K3 with equality); 6 pairings + (B, I = (10, 0)) + 2 rest beats + A probe')
    M = C.frozen_snn_member(W0=F(7, 16), lam=F(1, 2))
    h = FORM + [('B', (F(10), F(0))), Q2, Q2, ('A', (F(0), F(0)))]
    t = len(h) - 1; W = (t, t)
    rg = C.check_regime(M, 0, A01, h, W, 6, 2, 2)
    Us = X.membrane_upper_strict(M, 0, A01, h, 15)
    v1 = X.certified_probes_nostrict(M, 0, A01, h, W, 15)
    v2 = X.certified_probes(M, 0, A01, h, W, 15)
    V = C.verify(M, 0, A01, h, W, 6, 2, 2, pairs=rg['pairs'])
    print(f'  C = {C.check_C(M, 0, A01, 6, 2, 2)[0]}; old regime (clean probe) = {rg["ok"]}, clean probes {rg["probes"]}')
    print(f'  upper-bound recursion: after beat 18 (B, I₀ = 10) (U, strict) = ({fr(Us[19][0])}, {Us[19][1]}); after beat 19 ({fr(Us[20][0])}, {Us[20][1]}); after beat 20 ({fr(Us[21][0])}, {Us[21][1]})')
    print(f'  test without the strictness flag {v1} (λU + Σ_A b = 1 but U ≠ θ: missed); certificate probes {v2} (strict and λ > 0, so λU + Σ_A b ≤ θ suffices); actual probe input with T set to b {fr(V["probe"]["vt_cf"])} < 1; R witness {V["R"]}')
    print()

def rand_member_eq(rng):
    """Random members covering the endpoints: λ ∈ {0, 1, other}, m ∈ {0,…,4}, K3 strict or with exact equality (Σ_A b = 0 when λ = 1)."""
    while True:
        n_in = rng.choice([2, 3, 4]); n_out = rng.choice([1, 2])
        A = sorted(rng.sample(range(n_in), rng.randint(1, n_in - 1)))
        pats = {'none': (0,) * n_in, 'pA': tuple(1 if i in A else 0 for i in range(n_in))}
        for kk in range(rng.randint(1, 3)):
            wa = rng.choice([0, 1]); pats[f'q{kk}'] = tuple(wa if i in A else rng.choice([0, 1]) for i in range(n_in))
        th = F(rng.choice([1, 2, 3]), rng.choice([1, 2]))
        lam = rng.choice([F(0), F(1), F(rng.randint(1, 7), 8)])
        m = rng.randint(0, 4)
        room = th - lam ** (m + 1) * th
        eq = rng.random() < 0.5
        if lam == 1:
            sb = F(0)
        elif lam == 0:
            sb = th * F(rng.randint(1, 7), 8)        # λ = 0: K3 reduces to Σ_A b < θ
        else:
            sb = room if eq else room * F(rng.randint(1, 7), 8)
        if sb >= th:
            continue
        b = [[F(0)] * n_out for _ in range(n_in)]
        for i in A:
            b[i][0] = sb / len(A)
        for i in range(n_in):
            if i not in A:
                b[i][0] = F(rng.randint(0, 8), 8) * th
            for k in range(1, n_out):
                b[i][k] = F(rng.randint(0, 8), 8)
        hi = [[b[i][k] + F(rng.randint(1, 16), 4) * th for k in range(n_out)] for i in range(n_in)]
        lo = [[F(0)] * n_out for _ in range(n_in)]
        dy = F(rng.randint(0, 7), 8); Am = F(rng.randint(0, 6), 32)
        Ap = max(Am / (1 - dy), F(1, 100)) + F(rng.randint(1, 6), 64)
        M = C.Mem(n_in, n_out, pats, [lam] * n_out, [th] * n_out, [0] * n_out, [F(rng.randint(0, 7), 8) for _ in range(n_in)],
                  [dy] * n_out, Ap, Am, 0, b, lo, hi)
        mF = rng.randint(0, 3)
        n = 1
        while not C.check_C(M, 0, A, n, mF, m)[0] and n < 300:
            n += 1
        if n >= 300:
            continue
        return M, A, n, mF, m, eq

def e2_e3():
    print('E2  Inclusion and E3  soundness: random endpoint members (λ ∈ {0, 1, other}, m = 0–4, K3 strict/equality) × random trajectories')
    rng = random.Random(20261211)
    stat = dict(mem=0, traj=0, net=0, net_in=0, cert=0, cert_ok=0, v1miss=0, lam0=0, lam1=0, eq=0, m0=0)
    for _ in range(400):
        M, A, n, mF, m, eq = rand_member_eq(rng)
        stat['mem'] += 1; stat['lam0'] += M.lam[0] == 0; stat['lam1'] += M.lam[0] == 1; stat['eq'] += eq; stat['m0'] += m == 0
        for _ in range(4):
            hist, W, tp = RM.rand_traj(rng, M, 0, A, n, mF, m, rng.randint(0, 20), rng.randint(0, 12))
            rg = C.check_regime(M, 0, A, hist, W, n, mF, m)
            pn = rg['pairs'][-1]
            c2 = X.certified_probes(M, 0, A, hist, W, pn)
            c1 = X.certified_probes_nostrict(M, 0, A, hist, W, pn)
            stat['traj'] += 1
            stat['net'] += len(rg['probes']); stat['net_in'] += sum(1 for t in rg['probes'] if t in c2)
            stat['v1miss'] += sum(1 for t in rg['probes'] if t not in c1)
            pre, recs = M.run(hist)
            for t in c2:
                o = pre[t]
                vt_cf = M.lam[0] * o[1][0] + sum(M.b[i][0] for i in A)
                stat['cert'] += 1; stat['cert_ok'] += vt_cf < M.theta[0]
    print(f'  {stat["mem"]} members (λ = 0 {stat["lam0"]}, λ = 1 {stat["lam1"]}, K3 equality {stat["eq"]}, m = 0 {stat["m0"]}), {stat["traj"]} trajectories')
    print(f'  E2: {stat["net"]} clean probes in W in total, of which {stat["net_in"]} are certificate probes; clean probes missed by the test without the strictness flag: {stat["v1miss"]}')
    print(f'  E3: {stat["cert"]} certificate probes in total, of which {stat["cert_ok"]} have gate argument < θ once T is set to b (certificate sound)')
    # adversarial prefixes: check soundness in the various cases where “the upper bound is attainable”
    rng = random.Random(20261212)
    adv = adv_ok = 0
    for _ in range(400):
        M, A, n, mF, m, eq = rand_member_eq(rng)
        th = M.theta[0]; no = M.n_out
        Qr = ('none', (F(0),) * no)
        hist = []
        for k in range(n):
            hist += [Qr] * (mF if k else 0) + [('pA', (th,) + (F(0),) * (no - 1))]
        pn = len(hist) - 1
        for _ in range(rng.randint(0, 4)):
            c = rng.choice([F(0), th / 2, th - F(1, 64), th, 2 * th])
            hist.append(('none', (c,) + (F(0),) * (no - 1)))
            hist += [Qr] * rng.randint(0, 2)
        hist.append(('pA', (F(0),) * no))
        tp = len(hist) - 1
        c2 = X.certified_probes(M, 0, A, hist, (tp, tp), pn)
        if c2:
            pre, recs = M.run(hist)
            o = pre[tp]
            adv += 1; adv_ok += M.lam[0] * o[1][0] + sum(M.b[i][0] for i in A) < th
    print(f'  E3 (adversarial: after formation, preload the membrane potential by injection only, then the probe): {adv} certificate probes, all sound: {adv_ok}')
    # the “mechanically switching to ≤” trap
    M = C.frozen_snn_member(W0=F(3, 8), lam=F(1, 2))
    c = F(1, 2)
    h = FORM + [('none', (c, F(0))), ('A', (F(0), F(0)))]
    t = len(h) - 1
    Us = X.membrane_upper_strict(M, 0, A01, h, 15)
    pre, recs = M.run(h)
    vt_cf = M.lam[0] * pre[t][1][0] + sum(M.b[i][0] for i in A01)
    mech = M.lam[0] * Us[t][0] + F(3, 4) <= 1
    print(f'  trap example (λ = 1/2, Σ_A b = 3/4; one beat after formation injects only I₀ = 1/2 without firing, immediately followed by the probe): (U, strict) = ({fr(Us[t][0])}, {Us[t][1]}), the upper bound is attained exactly; '
          f'the mechanical test “λU + Σb ≤ θ” gives {mech}, certificate probes {X.certified_probes(M, 0, A01, h, (t, t), 15)}, actual gate argument with T set to b {fr(vt_cf)} ≥ θ—no certificate may be issued')
    print()

def e2b():
    print('E2b  Exhaustive inclusion over λ and W0 variants of the frozen member: formation + 2-beat interlude ({none, A, B, AB} × I₀ ∈ {0, 1, 10}) + m rest beats + probe')
    tot = inn = 0
    for lam, W0 in [(F(3, 4), F(1, 4)), (F(1, 2), F(7, 16)), (F(1, 2), F(1, 4)), (F(0), F(3, 8)), (F(1), F(0))]:
        M = C.frozen_snn_member(W0=W0, lam=lam)
        m = C.min_margin(M, 0, A01)
        letters = [(nm, (F(i0), F(0))) for nm in ['none', 'A', 'B', 'AB'] for i0 in (0, 1, 10)]
        t1 = i1 = 0
        for combo in itertools.product(letters, repeat=2):
            h = FORM + list(combo) + [Q2] * m + [('A', (F(0), F(0)))]
            t = len(h) - 1
            rg = C.check_regime(M, 0, A01, h, (18, t), 6, 2, m)
            for p in rg['probes']:
                t1 += 1; i1 += p in X.certified_probes(M, 0, A01, h, (18, t), 15)
        tot += t1; inn += i1
        print(f'    λ = {fr(lam)}, W0 = {fr(W0)}, minimum margin m = {m}: clean probes {t1}, of which certificate probes {i1}')
    print(f'  total clean probes {tot}, of which certificate probes {inn}')
    print()

def e4():
    print('E4  Coverage statistics (certificate-probe definition)')
    M = C.frozen_snn_member()
    rng = random.Random(20261031)
    cls = dict(noA=0, clean=0, cert_only=0, uncert=0)
    for kk in range(200):
        win = [(rng.choice(['A', 'B', 'none', 'none']), (F(0), F(0))) for _ in range(18)]
        hist = FORM + [Q2] * 8 + win
        rg = C.check_regime(M, 0, A01, hist, (26, 43), 6, 2, 2)
        cp = X.certified_probes(M, 0, A01, hist, (26, 43), 15)
        if not any(s[0] == 'A' for s in win):
            cls['noA'] += 1
        elif rg['ok']:
            cls['clean'] += 1
        elif cp:
            cls['cert_only'] += 1
        else:
            cls['uncert'] += 1
    print(f'  Chapter 11 random family, 200 histories: no A {cls["noA"]}; with a clean probe {cls["clean"]}; no clean probe but a certificate probe {cls["cert_only"]}; A present but no certificate probe {cls["uncert"]} (same as the test without the strictness flag: K3 holds strictly for the frozen member, so the endpoint case does not arise)')
    letters = [(nm, (F(i0), F(0))) for nm in ['none', 'A', 'B', 'AB'] for i0 in (0, 1)]
    c1 = c2 = 0
    for combo in itertools.product(letters, repeat=5):
        hist = FORM + list(combo[:3]) + [('A', (F(0), F(0)))] + list(combo[3:])
        W = (18, len(hist) - 1)
        c1 += bool(X.certified_probes_nostrict(M, 0, A01, hist, W, 15)); c2 += bool(X.certified_probes(M, 0, A01, hist, W, 15))
    print(f'  frozen member, exhaustive over 32768 histories: with a probe under the test without the strictness flag {c1}, with a certificate probe {c2}')
    print()

def e5():
    print('E5  Ungated F-layer bilinear process c = k·x·y, k = 1 ≠ 0 ([2] D12 bilinear law; D15 functional support taken as nonzero partial derivatives)')
    k, eps = F(1), F(1, 10)
    x = F(1)
    for y in (F(0), F(1, 2), F(1)):
        c = k * x * y
        dcdx = k * y
        print(f'  x = 1 (in T, formed, > ε), partner y = {fr(y)}: c = {fr(c)}, ∂c/∂x = {fr(dcdx)}, x ∈ Supp_act: {dcdx != 0}; effective |c| > ε: {abs(c) > eps}')
    print('  Reading: the process runs every beat from the same inventory and its occurrence is not gated, yet whether x lies in the functional support depends on the state of the partner y—“support can fail” does not require the occurrence of the action to change with the Trace-Response.')
    print('  When k = 0, c ≡ 0 and ∂c/∂x ≡ 0, so x is never in the functional support; “y > 0 implies support” holds only for k ≠ 0.')
    print('  Hence non-triviality is not confined to the single route of gating; the structural observation on linear own-position families coexists with the threshold-gating route.')
    print()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    print('The Necessity Form of Field-Concretization Qualification: certificate probes and bilinear comparison (run_cert.py)')
    print()
    e1(); e2_e3(); e2b(); e4(); e5()
    print('End.')
~~~

A.15  run\_scope.py  Run entry: regression on the domains of validity of antecedents — L5′ of Lemma 14-L and component-wise compatibility, antecedent S4 of Proposition 14-5(2), the condition table of the conditional prediction of 9.1, effective PLUS components and net weight change (G1–G5)

~~~python
"""Appendix program of The Necessity Form of Field-Concretization Qualification: regression on the domains of validity of antecedents (G1–G5). Standard library only, exact rational arithmetic, no randomness.
Run (in this directory): python3 run_scope.py
G1  resolution compatibility L5 of Lemma 14-L: equivalence of the closed form L5′ with component-wise compatibility (all histories considered, beats 0 to t_e, both encoding positions);
    two cases wrongly accepted by checking only the partial arithmetic of the z1 formation lower bound; the subthreshold, equality and suprathreshold branches for b = 0, k = 0 and b = 0, k > 0; relaxation of the unseeded position z2.
G2  antecedent S4 of Proposition 14-5 (2): with a decomposable alphabet, the return value of the blocking test does not constitute blocking (an M1₀ removal that removes only part of A exists); indecomposable contrast with the decomposable letter removed.
G3  condition table of the conditional prediction in 9.1: a member with K2′, K3 holding and formation crossing threshold but ρ > 0; S3 fails and the condition table excludes it; contrast with ρ = 0 restored.
G4  summary.
G5  reading of prediction (ii) in 9.1: under saturation, an effective potentiation component PLUS at the probe beat coexists with a weight net increment of 0; unsaturated contrast and the lower bound of the sufficient condition for strict net potentiation.
"""
import sys, os, itertools
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
sys.path.insert(0, HERE)
from fractions import Fraction as F
import t14_core as C
import t14_ext as X
from t14_core import fr
A01 = [0, 1]
Q2 = ('none', (F(0), F(0)))

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- G1 L5 of Lemma 14-L
def partial_check(L, ts, te):
    """Checks only the partial arithmetic of the z1 formation lower bound (L1, L2, L3 and “b > ε when b ≠ 0, and γ·a(1−γ)^(t_e−1) > ε”). This is not L5."""
    a, b, g, e = L.a, L.b, L.g, L.eps
    return (a > e and g * a * (1 - g) ** (ts - 1) > e and a * (1 - g) ** te > e
            and (b == 0 or b > e) and g * a * (1 - g) ** (te - 1) > e)

def l5_closed(L, te):
    """L5′ (closed form; premises b, k ≥ 0, 0 < γ < 1, a > ε, t_e ≥ 1):
    γ·a(1−γ)^(t_e−1) > ε, and
      if b = 0: k = 0 or k·a(1−γ)^(t_e−1) > ε;
      if b > 0: b > ε, and t_e = 1 or γ·b(1−γ)^(t_e−2) > ε."""
    a, b, k, g, e = L.a, L.b, L.k, L.g, L.eps
    if not g * a * (1 - g) ** (te - 1) > e:
        return False
    if b == 0:
        return k == 0 or k * a * (1 - g) ** (te - 1) > e
    return b > e and (te == 1 or g * b * (1 - g) ** (te - 2) > e)

def compat_all(L, te):
    """Component-wise compatibility: for every sequence in {drive, rest}^t_e, every encoding position at beats 0 to t_e and every component c satisfy c = 0 or |c| > ε."""
    for seq in itertools.product(['drive', 'rest'], repeat=te):
        if not L.run(['seed'] + list(seq), (te, te))['compat']:
            return False
    return True

def g1():
    print('G1  resolution compatibility L5 of Lemma 14-L (main text 6.7)')
    e = F(1, 100)
    # two cases wrongly accepted by the partial arithmetic
    L = C.Lin(1, 0, F(1, 200), F(1, 2), e)
    r = L.run(['seed', 'drive'], (1, 1))
    print(f'  Case 1: a = 1, b = 0, k = 1/200, γ = 1/2, ε = 1/100, history (seed, drive), W = [1, 1]: partial arithmetic {partial_check(L, 1, 1)}; L5′ {l5_closed(L, 1)}; '
          f'component-wise compatible {compat_all(L, 1)}; the drive write k·z1 = {fr(L.k * 1)} ≤ ε is discarded, F-layer final z1 = {fr(r["z_end"][0])}, mechanism layer {fr(r["zm_end"][0])}; formal R = {r["R"]}')
    L = C.Lin(1, F(1, 10), 0, F(1, 10), e)
    r = L.run(['seed', 'drive', 'drive'], (2, 2))
    print(f'  Case 2: a = 1, b = 1/10, k = 0, γ = 1/10, ε = 1/100, history (seed, drive, drive), W = [2, 2]: partial arithmetic {partial_check(L, 2, 2)}; L5′ {l5_closed(L, 2)}; '
          f'component-wise compatible {compat_all(L, 2)}; the relaxation γ·b = {fr(L.g * L.b)} = ε of the unseeded position z2 at the second drive beat is discarded, F-layer final state ({fr(r["z_end"][0])}, {fr(r["z_end"][1])}), '
          f'mechanism layer ({fr(r["zm_end"][0])}, {fr(r["zm_end"][1])}); formal R = {r["R"]}')
    print('  Reading: in both cases the formal qualification is still 1 while fidelity (F layer identical to mechanism layer beat by beat) fails; a fidelity failure is not a negative determination of qualification; it only means the F-layer reading is not read back.')
    # branches
    rows = []
    a, g, te = F(1), F(1, 2), 3
    base = a * (1 - g) ** (te - 1)                 # = 1/4
    rows.append(('b = 0, k = 0', C.Lin(a, 0, 0, g, e)))
    for lab, k in (('subthreshold', e / base / 2), ('equality', e / base), ('suprathreshold', 2 * e / base)):
        rows.append((f'b = 0, k > 0, k·a(1−γ)^(t_e−1) {lab}', C.Lin(a, 0, k, g, e)))
    bz = e / (g * (1 - g) ** (te - 2))             # the b with γ·b(1−γ)^(t_e−2) = ε
    for lab, b in (('subthreshold', bz / 2), ('equality', bz), ('suprathreshold', 2 * bz)):
        rows.append((f'b > 0, k = 0, relaxation of the unseeded position γ·b(1−γ)^(t_e−2) {lab}', C.Lin(a, b, 0, g, e)))
    rows.append(('b > 0 but b ≤ ε (first write sub-resolution)', C.Lin(a, e, 0, g, e)))
    gz = None
    print(f'  branches (a = 1, γ = 1/2, t_e = 3, ε = 1/100; component-wise compatibility over all 8 sequences in {{drive, rest}}^3):')
    for lab, L in rows:
        print(f'    {lab}: b = {fr(L.b)}, k = {fr(L.k)}; L5′ {l5_closed(L, te)}; component-wise compatible {compat_all(L, te)}')
    # grid: L5′ ⇔ component-wise compatibility
    tot = agree = acc_partial_bad = 0
    vals_a = [F(1, 50), F(1, 10), F(1, 2), F(1), F(2)]
    vals_b = [F(0), F(1, 200), F(1, 100), F(1, 50), F(1, 10), F(1, 2)]
    vals_k = [F(0), F(1, 200), F(1, 100), F(1, 40), F(1, 10), F(1, 2)]
    vals_g = [F(1, 10), F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(9, 10)]
    for a, b, k, g in itertools.product(vals_a, vals_b, vals_k, vals_g):
        for te in (1, 2, 3, 4):
            L = C.Lin(a, b, k, g, e)
            if not a > e:
                continue
            c1, c2 = l5_closed(L, te), compat_all(L, te)
            tot += 1; agree += (c1 == c2)
            if partial_check(L, 1, te) and not c2:
                acc_partial_bad += 1
    print(f'  grid (a 5 values × b 6 values × k 6 values × γ 4 values × t_e = 1–4, a > ε): {tot} cases, L5′ agrees with component-wise compatibility in {agree}; wrongly accepted by checking only the partial arithmetic (component-wise incompatible) {acc_partial_bad}')
    print()
    return tot == agree

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- G2 antecedent S4 of Proposition 14-5 (2)
def source_model(partial=True):
    pats = {'none': (0, 0), 'A': (1, 1)}
    if partial:
        pats['partial'] = (1, 0)
    return C.Mem(2, 2, pats, [1, 1], [1, 1], [0, 2], [0, 0], [0, 0],
                 F(1, 2), 0, 0, [[1, -3], [1, 1]], [[0, -3], [0, -3]], [[3, 3], [3, 3]])

def s4_ok(M, A):
    return all(len({M.pat[nm][i] for i in A}) == 1 for nm in M.pat)

def g2():
    print('G2  antecedent S4 of Proposition 14-5 (2) (main text 6.3): target j = 0, A = {entry 0, entry 1}; letters none = (0, 0), A = (1, 1); the other case adds the decomposable letter partial = (1, 0)')
    print('  parameters: λ = (1, 1), θ = (1, 1), v_reset = (0, 2), d_x = d_y = 0, A₊ = 1/2, A₋ = ρ = 0, ε = 1/100; baseline b = [[1, −3], [1, 1]]; lo = [[0, −3], [0, −3]], hi = 3; prefix (none, I = (0, 1)), first pairing (A, I = (1, 0))')
    sig = ('A', (F(1), F(0)))
    ok = True
    for partial in (True, False):
        M = source_model(partial)
        o, _ = M.step(M.init(), ('none', (F(0), F(1))))
        f3u = bool(X.f3star(M, 0, A01, sig)) or X.f3deg(M, 0)
        blk = X.blocker2(M, 0, A01, sig)
        wd, _ = X.m10_any(M, 0, A01, o, sig, F(1, 100))
        lab = 'with decomposable letter partial' if partial else 'partial removed (indecomposable contrast)'
        print(f'  {lab}: S4 {s4_ok(M, A01)}; F3ᵤ {f3u}; blocking test returns {blk}; admissible same-beat removals satisfying M1₀: {len(wd)}{": " + ", ".join(f"({nm}, I = ({fr(I[0])}, {fr(I[1])}))" for nm, I in sorted(wd)) if wd else ""}')
        if partial:
            act = M.step(o, sig)[1]; cf = M.step(o, ('partial', (F(1), F(0))))[1]
            print(f'    removal down to (partial, I = (1, 0)): gates {act["g"]} → {cf["g"]} (no new opening), PLUS_10 {fr(act["ev"][("PLUS", 1, 0)])} → {fr(cf["ev"].get(("PLUS", 1, 0), 0))}; '
                  f'antecedent S4 of Proposition 14-5 (2) fails, this case lies outside the proposition, and the return value of the blocking test does not constitute blocking')
            # every admissible prefix before the first pairing: w = b, x = 0, v_0 = 0, the other output v_1 ∈ [0, 1) ∪ {2}
            allp = all(('partial', (F(1), F(0))) in X.m10_any(M, 0, A01, ([[z for z in row] for row in M.b], [F(0), v], [F(0), F(0)], [F(0), F(0)]), sig, F(1, 100))[0]
                       for v in [F(q, 20) for q in range(20)] + [F(2)])
            print(f'    pre-beat v_1 of the other output taken at 20 values in [0, 1) and at the reset value 2: the removal of only part of A satisfies M1₀ in every case {allp} (actual vt_1 = v_1 − 2 ≤ 0; after removal v_1 − 3 < 1)')
            ok = ok and blk is not None and len(wd) > 0 and allp
        else:
            ok = ok and s4_ok(M, A01) and not f3u and blk is not None and len(wd) == 0
    print('  Reading: the necessity argument of Proposition 14-5 (2) uses “changing the write on T requires closing gate j or removing all of A”, which holds only under S4; the domain of validity of the blocking test blocker2 is alphabets satisfying S4. The main Theorem 14-1⁺ already contains S4 and is unaffected by this case.')
    print()
    return ok

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- G3 condition table of the conditional prediction in 9.1
def g3():
    print('G3  condition table of the conditional prediction in 9.1 (main text 9.1): frozen member with only A₊ = 1/4 changed, ρ = 1/10 and 0; 6 pairings (spaced by 2 rest beats) + 100 rest beats + A probe (W = the probe beat)')
    form = [('A', (F(1), F(0))), Q2, Q2] * 6
    h = form + [Q2] * 100 + [('A', (F(0), F(0)))]
    t = len(h) - 1; W = (t, t)
    res = {}
    for rho in (F(1, 10), F(0)):
        M = C.frozen_snn_member(Ap=F(1, 4), rho=rho)
        okC, items, _ = C.check_C(M, 0, A01, 6, 2, 2)
        bad = [nm.split()[0] for nm, v, _ in items if not v]
        pre, recs = M.run(h)
        formed = sum(pre[len(form)][0][i][0] for i in A01)
        zend = min(pre[t + 1][0][i][0] - M.b[i][0] for i in A01)
        V = C.verify(M, 0, A01, h, W, 6, 2, 2) if okC else None
        k2 = M.Ap * (1 - M.dy[0]) >= M.Am
        k3 = M.lam[0] ** 3 * M.theta[0] + sum(M.b[i][0] for i in A01) <= M.theta[0]
        print(f'  ρ = {fr(rho)}: K2′ {k2}; K3(2) {k3}; Σ_A w at the end of the formation period = {fr(formed)} > θ = 1 {formed > M.theta[0]}; failing items of the condition group {bad or "none"}; '
              f'gate at the probe beat g_j = {recs[t]["g"][0]}; min z at window end = {fr(zend)}; R witness {V["R"] if V else "no verdict (conditions incomplete)"}')
        res[rho] = (okC, recs[t]['g'][0], V['R'] if V else None)
    print('  Reading: at ρ = 1/10, S3 fails, relaxation has eroded the potentiation, and the probe no longer fires; this is a case the condition table excludes, not a counterexample to Theorem 14-1⁺ or the 9.1 prediction. With ρ = 0 restored, all conditions hold, the probe fires, and the R witness is 1.')
    print()
    return res[F(1, 10)][0] is False and res[F(1, 10)][1] == 0 and res[F(0)] == (True, 1, 1)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- G5 effective PLUS component and net weight change
def g5():
    print('G5  reading of prediction (ii) in 9.1 (main text 9.1): frozen member, n = 12, m_F = m = 2, history [(A, I = (1, 0)), rest, rest]^12 + [(A, I = (0, 0))], W = the probe beat; (hi, A₋) taken as (7/10, 1/16), (2, 1/16), (2, 1/32)')
    h = [('A', (F(1), F(0))), Q2, Q2] * 12 + [('A', (F(0), F(0)))]
    t = len(h) - 1; W = (t, t)
    res = {}
    for hi, am in ((F(7, 10), F(1, 16)), (F(2), F(1, 16)), (F(2), F(1, 32))):
        M = C.frozen_snn_member(wmax=hi, Am=am)
        okC = C.check_C(M, 0, A01, 12, 2, 2)[0]
        rg = C.check_regime(M, 0, A01, h, W, 12, 2, 2)
        cert = X.certified_probes(M, 0, A01, h, W, rg['pairs'][-1])
        pre, recs = M.run(h)
        ev = recs[t]['ev']
        plus = [ev.get(('PLUS', i, 0), F(0)) for i in A01]
        net = [pre[t + 1][0][i][0] - pre[t][0][i][0] for i in A01]
        cf = ([row[:] for row in pre[t][0]], pre[t][1][:], pre[t][2][:], pre[t][3][:])
        for i in A01:
            cf[0][i][0] = M.b[i][0]
        gcf = M.step(cf, h[t])[1]['g'][0]
        dy = M.dy[0]
        delta = M.Ap - M.Am / (1 - dy)
        lb = [min(delta, M.hi[i][0] - pre[t][0][i][0]) for i in A01]
        V = C.verify(M, 0, A01, h, W, 12, 2, 2, pairs=rg['pairs'])
        print(f'  hi = {fr(hi)}, A₋ = {fr(am)}: condition group {okC}, regime {rg["ok"]}, certificate probes {cert}; w before the probe beat = ({fr(pre[t][0][0][0])}, {fr(pre[t][0][1][0])}); gate g_j = {recs[t]["g"][0]}; '
              f'PLUS = ({fr(plus[0])}, {fr(plus[1])}) (> ε {all(p > M.eps for p in plus)}); net weight increment = ({fr(net[0])}, {fr(net[1])}); gate with T set to b in the same beat {gcf}; R witness {V["R"]}')
        print(f'    lower bound of the sufficient condition: δ = A₊ − A₋/(1 − d_y) = {fr(delta)}, min(δ, hi − w) = ({fr(lb[0])}, {fr(lb[1])}); net increment ≥ lower bound {all(n >= l for n, l in zip(net, lb))}; lower bound > ε {all(l > M.eps for l in lb)}')
        res[(hi, am)] = (okC and rg['ok'] and cert == [t], recs[t]['g'][0], all(p > M.eps for p in plus), net, gcf, V['R'], all(n >= l for n, l in zip(net, lb)), all(l > M.eps for l in lb))
    print('  Reading: at hi = 7/10, PLUS is effective, the pivot holds and the qualification witness is 1, while clipping cancels it and the net weight increment is 0; a net change of 0 negates neither PLUS nor the historical dynamics. At hi = 2 there is no saturation and the net increment is positive (for the frozen member δ = 0; the positive net increment comes from the eligibility trace x′ > 1 and is not given by the sufficient condition). At A₋ = 1/32, δ = 1/16 > 0 and the net increment is at least min(δ, hi − w) > ε, an instance of the proven sufficient condition for strict net potentiation.')
    print()
    a, b, c = res[(F(7, 10), F(1, 16))], res[(F(2), F(1, 16))], res[(F(2), F(1, 32))]
    return (a[0] and a[1] == 1 and a[2] and a[3] == [0, 0] and a[4] == 0 and a[5] == 1
            and b[0] and b[1] == 1 and b[2] and all(n > 0 for n in b[3]) and b[4] == 0 and b[5] == 1 and b[6]
            and c[0] and c[2] and c[5] == 1 and c[6] and c[7] and all(n > M.eps for n in c[3]))

if __name__ == '__main__':
    print('The Necessity Form of Field-Concretization Qualification: regression on the domains of validity of antecedents (run_scope.py)')
    print()
    r1 = g1(); r2 = g2(); r3 = g3(); r5 = g5()
    print(f'G4  summary: G1 L5′ agrees with component-wise compatibility {r1}; G2 domain of validity of S4 and indecomposable contrast {r2}; G3 exclusion by S3 and contrast {r3}; G5 distinction between effective PLUS and net increment, and unsaturated contrast {r5}')
    print('End.')
~~~

## Appendix B  Run Results

B.1  Runtime Environment and Checksums  
Runtime environment: Linux 6.18 x86\_64; Python 3.13.17, standard library only. The 9 commands of B.2–B.10 are run with python3 -E -s in an empty directory containing only the files of Appendix A; all exit with code 0, and the output is deterministic (random generation uses fixed seeds and the output contains no timings; it is listed verbatim in each section, without truncation). Run alone in this environment, run\_main.py, run\_xcheck.py c and run\_ext.py each take about 2–3 minutes, run\_xcheck.py b about 1 minute, and each of the others under 30 seconds; about 10 minutes in total.

　gate\_class.py  b07c0327be0383f020de11e03c656e6967df12b972a4e7e97dd0af99885eb630

E5  Ungated F-layer bilinear process c = k·x·y, k = 1 ≠ 0 (\[2\] D12 bilinear law; D15 functional support taken as nonzero partial derivatives)  
&nbsp;&nbsp;x = 1 (in T, formed, \> ε), partner y = 0: c = 0, ∂c/∂x = 0, x ∈ Supp\_act: False; effective \|c\| \> ε: False

&nbsp;&nbsp;Reading: the process runs every beat from the same inventory and its occurrence is not gated, yet whether x lies in the functional support depends on the state of the partner y—“support can fail” does not require the occurrence of the action to change with the Trace-Response.  
&nbsp;&nbsp;When k = 0, c ≡ 0 and ∂c/∂x ≡ 0, so x is never in the functional support; “y \> 0 implies support” holds only for k ≠ 0.  
&nbsp;&nbsp;Hence non-triviality is not confined to the single route of gating; the structural observation on linear own-position families coexists with the threshold-gating route.  
End.
